Method: 作者提出"可操作不确定性"的形式化框架：当所有可接受（admissible）偏好或目标都共享某个行动时应当执行；当每种可能都可行但无共享行动时应当澄清；当请求不可行时，应提出成本最小的、被允许的约束修复。据此构建求解器支撑的基准，覆盖对象分配、会议安排、公寓选择与稳定匹配四类场景，并采用匹配对（matched pairs）设计：同一来源、仅改变是否需要干预。评测分离为决策正确性、匹配对可靠性与完全正确响应三个层面。

Result: 模型在识别"干预不必要"上存在反复出现的困难：它们能识别需要澄清或修复的情形，却在已有正当行动时仍然干预。正确的决策标签不能保证生成可用的行动、问题或修复。响应要求不仅影响决策如何表达，还影响做出哪种决策；即使输出已可解析，明确写出所需内容仍能大幅提升完全正确响应，并改变干预决策。

Conclusion: 可靠的智能体能力不止于识别不确定性，还需做到仅在必要时干预，并把选定的下一步转化为可验证的响应。评测应同时覆盖决策正确性、匹配对可靠性和完全正确性，并重视响应格式要求对决策本身的塑造作用。

Abstract: An LLM agent can recognize uncertainty yet still choose the wrong next step: asking when action is already justified, or seeking clarification when the constraints must change. We formalize actionable indeterminacy: act when an accepted action is shared across all admissible preferences or objectives, clarify when each possibility is feasible but no action is shared, and propose a minimum-cost permitted constraint repair when the request is infeasible. We construct a solver-grounded benchmark spanning object allocation, meeting scheduling, apartment choice, and stable matching. Matched pairs retain the same source while changing whether intervention is necessary, and evaluation separates decision correctness, matched-pair reliability, and fully correct responses. Our findings reveal a recurring difficulty in recognizing when intervention is unnecessary: models can identify situations requiring clarification or repair yet still intervene when a justified action already exists. Correct decision labels also fail to guarantee usable actions, questions, or repairs. Crucially, response requirements shape not only how decisions are expressed but also which decisions are made. Making the required content explicit substantially improves fully correct responses and can change intervention decisions, even when outputs are already parseable. These findings highlight that reliable agency requires more than recognizing uncertainty: it requires intervening only when necessary and translating the chosen next step into a verifiable response.

</details>


### [142] [Emergent Structure in the Marginal Attention Space of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03109)
*Valentino Maiorca,Walter Nelson,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究后softmax注意力权重在查询位置上的边缘化结构，发现token维还原得到跨模型稳健保留的文本内在信号，head维还原得到跨文档保留的模型私有签名，并将其用于KV缓存逐头预算估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型间的表示相似性已被广泛记录，但注意力等内部机制在不同模型间如何表现仍缺乏刻画；作者希望填补这一空白，理解注意力的结构性规律并找到实际应用。

Method: 对后softmax注意力权重沿query位置边缘化，映射到token-head联合的"边缘注意力空间"；在60多个不同LLM上评估，分别沿token轴和head轴降维；将边缘注意力与网络输入-输出Jacobian经验联系，并在平滑性假设下理论证明相似next-token分布的模型具有相似Jacobian统计；据此为逐头KV缓存淘汰预计算预算。

Result: token向降维得到文本内在信号，跨模型稳健保留；head向降维形成模型私有签名，跨文档保留；在标准淘汰基准上，离线预计算的逐头预算结合免训练token打分，性能与逐文档重算预算或逐目标训练的方法相当。

Conclusion: 边缘注意力空间沿不同轴降维揭示了可分离的两类性质：通用文本信号与模型私有签名，并将模型特定预算分配与文本内在token打分有效解耦，为KV缓存淘汰提供实用方案。

Abstract: While representation similarity across independently trained language models is well-documented, how internal mechanics such as attention behave across models remains far less characterized. Inspired by this gap, we examine the structure of post-softmax attention weights by marginalizing over query positions, mapping them into a joint token-head "marginal attention space". Evaluating across 60+ diverse LLMs, we find that different properties emerge when reducing this space along its token and head axes. When reduced token-wise, marginal attention yields a text-intrinsic signal robustly conserved across models. To explain this property, we empirically connect marginal attention to the input-output Jacobian of the network, and prove theoretically that under a smoothness assumption, models with similar next-token distributions are guaranteed to have similar input-output Jacobian statistics. When reduced head-wise, it forms a model-private signature conserved across documents. Practically, this provides a natural way to estimate a per-head budget for key-value (KV) cache eviction, effectively decoupling model-specific budget allocation from text-intrinsic token scoring. On standard eviction benchmarks, a per-head budget precomputed offline on pretraining text, combined with a training-free token score, shows competitive performance with methods that recompute the budget on every document or train it per target. Code available at https://github.com/Flegyas/marginal-attention

</details>


### [143] [Ontological Instability and Statistical Amplification: The Paradox of "Humanizing" LLM-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03110)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过语义、结构和分词器层面的扰动，分析RoBERTa AI文本检测器决策依据，发现其检测分数与统计复杂度相关，在正式人类写作上误报率高达76.3%，而基于事件的潜在空间检测也并不稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式AI文本检测器虽报告高基准准确率，但其决策依据不明确。作者希望探究检测器究竟依赖哪些特征进行判断，以及这些特征在扰动和分布偏移下是否稳健。

Method: 使用M4数据集（N=10,000）和受控生成数据（N=300），对基于RoBERTa的检测器施加语义、结构和分词器层面的扰动；同时以基于事件的潜在空间（Latent Space）检测作为对照，考察其在不同扰动下的表现。

Result: 让Mistral-7B-Instruct将机器文本改写得更像人类时，动词多样性从0.77升至0.92，反而更易被检测；检测分数似乎追踪统计复杂度，并在正式人类写作上产生76.3%的误报率。对照实验中，改写改变了87%的事件序列（Jaccard=0.067），同形字符替换改变了70%的抽取动词（Jaccard=0.30），其最佳领域AUC仅为0.577。

Conclusion: RoBERTa检测器的稳健性似乎特定于其使用的特征，结构抽象并未使检测更稳健；基于事件的潜在空间检测同样脆弱，说明当前AI文本检测方法在真实扰动下存在明显局限。

Abstract: Supervised AI-text detectors report high benchmark accuracy, but it is not clear what their decisions are based on. We analyze a RoBERTa-based detector under semantic, structural, and tokenizer-level perturbations, using the M4 dataset (N = 10,000) and controlled generations (N = 300). When Mistral-7B-Instruct was asked to make machine text sound more human, Verb Diversity rose from 0.77 to 0.92 and the outputs became easier to detect. Detection scores appear to track statistical complexity, which also leads to a 76.3% false-positive rate on formal human writing. As a control, we evaluate event-based Latent Space detection. Paraphrasing changed 87% of its event sequences (Jaccard = 0.067), and homoglyphs altered 70% of the extracted verbs even though extraction still ran (Jaccard = 0.30). Its best domain AUC was 0.577. RoBERTa's robustness seems specific to the features it uses, and structural abstraction did not make detection more robust.

</details>


### [144] [Building Interpretable Feature Representations for Resume-Vacancy Matching by Distilling Production LLM Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03112)
*Ilya Chekin,Vyacheslav Malyugin,Vladimir Chirkov,Mikhail Yurushkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种由LLM标注器与特征双编码器组成的可解释人岗匹配方法，用八个可解释维度替代单一不透明相关性分数，线上仅用蒸馏后的双编码器推理，与招聘人员记录决策的一致率为95.79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘场景中，仅给出一个不透明的相关性分数不足以支撑决策，招聘人员需要理解候选人为何匹配岗位，即需要可解释、可命名的匹配证据维度。

Method: 方法分两部分：一是基于LLM的标注器，其提示词与特征定义由招聘人员反馈迭代精炼，当前仅用于离线标注；二是从该标注器蒸馏出的特征双编码器（LoRA适配的嵌入骨干加紧凑的逐维度预测头），可在CPU上运行并承担全部线上请求。两部分持续迭代：提示词随反馈修订，双编码器用更新后的标签重新训练。训练数据为168,772对已标注的职位-简历对（17,921个职位、180,030份简历）。

Result: 在927条由招聘人员记录的生产反馈子集数值上，部署的学生模型与记录决策一致888例，一致率为95.79%。该结果为运营中的非盲一致性评估，而非独立人工评测。

Conclusion: 该方案以可解释的八个匹配维度为招聘人员提供可操作的证据，并在生产环境中以高效的学生模型实现高一致性，但结论受限于非盲、非独立的评估设置。

Abstract: Matching candidates to vacancies is central to recruitment, and a recruiter needs to see why a candidate fits, not only a single opaque relevance score. We provide this evidence as named, interpretable matching dimensions recruiters can act on - eight in our current deployment. We propose a two-part approach. The first is an LLM-based labeler whose prompts and feature definitions were refined from recruiter feedback while it served as an earlier production matching stage. In the current architecture, it is used only for offline labeling and is not called on online requests. The second is a feature bi-encoder distilled from it: a LoRA-adapted embedding backbone with compact per-dimension heads that runs on CPU and serves all online requests. Both parts keep improving: prompts are revised as feedback arrives, and the bi-encoder is retrained on the updated labels. The model is trained on 168,772 labeled vacancy-resume pairs (17,921 vacancies and 180,030 resumes). Recruiters using the service can confirm or revise surfaced feature predictions. On 927 recruiter-recorded values from this selected production-feedback subset, the deployed student agrees with the recorded decisions in 888 cases (95.79%). This is operational, non-blinded agreement rather than an independent human evaluation.

</details>


### [145] [Investigating the Role of Reasoning-Language Alignment in Monolingual Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03136)
*Oliver Hauck,Mario Sanz-Guerrero,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个纯德语的RAG问答测试平台，研究强制推理语言与查询、检索文档语言一致性对准确率的影响，发现语言对齐有帮助但无法超越模型原生的英语推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM主要用英语训练推理，已有研究表明强制用其他语言推理会降低准确率，但仅在短提示设定下验证。本文关注在RAG场景下（模型需阅读并整合大量目标语言的检索证据）该结论是否依然成立。

Method: 基于桌面角色扮演游戏《黑暗之眼》构建完全单语的德语RAG问答测试平台（该领域德语资料丰富但过于小众，模型无法凭记忆回答，必须依赖检索）。在agentic RAG系统上改变强制推理语言进行实验。

Result: 推理语言与查询及检索文档语言一致有助于提升表现：强制德语推理优于强制法语推理，尽管模型在法语上基准分更高，说明收益来自语言对齐而非语言熟练度。检索上下文越丰富、结构感知越强，优势越明显。但强制德语仅达到模型原生无约束英语推理的水平，未能超越。

Conclusion: 语言对齐（推理语言与查询、文档语言一致）能在RAG中带来收益，但仍无法超越原生英语推理，表明模型需要真正的原生多语言推理能力。作者公开了测试平台和QA基准。

Abstract: Reasoning traces improve large language models (LLMs), but current models are trained to reason mostly in English. It has been shown that forcing a model to reason in another language degrades accuracy, even when the reasoning language matches the language of the prompt -- but only for a setting where the model reasons over a short prompt. Here, we ask whether the same holds for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where the model must read and integrate a large amount of retrieved evidence in the target language. To study this, we build a fully monolingual German RAG question-answering testbed over the fictional world of the tabletop role-playing game The Dark Eye, a domain that is richly documented in German but too niche for the model to answer from memory, so that it has to rely on retrieval. Varying the forced reasoning language of an agentic RAG system on this testbed, we find that aligning the reasoning language with the language of the query and the retrieved documents helps. Forced German reasoning outperforms forced French, although the model benchmarks higher in French, so the benefit comes from alignment and not from language proficiency. The advantage grows when the retrieved context is richer and structure-aware. However, forced German only reaches the level of the model's native, unconstrained English reasoning without surpassing it, showing that native multilingual reasoning is needed. We publicly release the testbed and QA benchmark.

</details>


### [146] [Predicting Steering Vectors and Adapter Weights for Few-Shot Author-Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03163)
*Leonard Popp,Danni Liu,Supriti Sinhamahapatra,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了从少量示例摘要中进行作者风格条件化的摘要生成，提出对比激活引导、引导向量预测网络和预测LoRA适配器的超网络三种方法。发现风格模仿与输出质量存在权衡，超网络在已知和未知作者上取得最佳平衡，且引导方法在作者层面操作，优于基于风格清单的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型适配到单个作者的写作风格具有挑战性，而科学写作更难：正式规范限制了表面变化，且作者书写自己的主题，使提取的"风格"容易与内容纠缠。因此作者希望研究如何从每位作者的少量示例摘要中实现风格条件化的摘要生成，并解决风格-内容纠缠问题。

Method: 提出三种方法：(1) 对比激活引导；(2) 预测引导向量的网络；(3) 预测LoRA适配器的超网络。其中引导在作者层面操作，将某位作者的摘要与针对相同内容的风格中立生成进行对比，从而固定主题、无需预定义风格清单。

Result: 发现风格模仿与输出质量之间存在一致的权衡：微调获得了大部分可用风格信号但牺牲了流畅性，而超网络在已知和未知作者上均取得最佳权衡。引导方法优于基于清单的引导。此外，手动提取和预测的引导向量几乎正交但得分相当，表明风格条件化可以容纳至少两个不相关的方向，而非要求某一特定轴。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在少样本科学写作风格条件化中，超网络方法能最好地平衡风格模仿与输出质量；作者层面的对比引导避免了风格与内容的纠缠且无需风格清单；风格条件化可存在多个不相关方向，因此不依赖单一最优轴。

Abstract: Adapting large language models to an individual author's style from a few examples is challenging, and scientific writing sharpens the difficulty: formal conventions leave little surface variation, and authors write about their own topics, so extracted ``style'' easily entangles with content. We study style-conditioned abstract generation from a few example abstracts per author and propose three methods: (1) contrastive activation steering, (2) a network that predicts steering vectors, and (3) a hypernetwork that predicts LoRA adapters. We find a consistent trade-off between style imitation and output quality: fine-tuning buys most of the available style signal but forfeits fluency, while the hypernetwork achieves the best trade-off on both seen and unseen authors. Our steering operates at author level, contrasting an author's abstracts against style-neutral generations for the same content. This holds topic fixed, removes the need for a predefined style inventory, and outperforms inventory-based steering. % [EDIT 1a] softened "no single optimal axis" claim Moreover, our analyses demonstrate that manually extracted and predicted steering vectors are near-orthogonal yet score comparably, indicating that style conditioning here can admit at least two unrelated directions rather than requiring one particular axis.

</details>


### [147] [Hindsight-Guided Rationale Distillation for Rare Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03176)
*Aarav Singh,Animesh Pathak,Navyansh Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在ZebraMap罕见病诊断任务上研究"事后引导蒸馏"：1.5B学生模型对8B教师（生成时可看到真值诊断）的思维链进行微调。绝对准确率普遍偏低，但经过滤的学生变体（StudentF）在统计上显著优于教师（p<0.001），集中在数据更充分的疾病上；未经过滤的学生优势不显著（p=0.129），说明增益来自污染过滤而非事后蒸馏本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病诊断在中小规模模型上极具挑战，作者希望验证：让教师模型在生成思维链时"偷看"真值（hindsight），能否把这种事后知识蒸馏给学生模型，从而提升诊断能力，并探究其真实收益来源。

Method: 以ZebraMap罕见病诊断为任务，用1.5B学生模型对8B教师生成的思维链进行SFT蒸馏；教师生成时可见真值诊断。设置三种对照：未经过滤学生、经过滤学生（StudentF，用正则过滤掉"真值is X"式片段）、以及教师模型本身，并进行显著性检验和频率相关的知识迁移分析。

Result: 所有模型绝对准确率都低。StudentF显著优于教师（p<0.001），提升集中在代表性更好的疾病；未经过滤学生不显著优于教师（p=0.129）。未过滤学生在33.9%的推理中复现"真值is X"式短语（GT幻觉），当真值猜错时准确率严重下降。正则过滤后污染降至近零，带来上述增益，但效应量仍小。此外还量化了增益—成本权衡、频率依赖的知识迁移，以及SFT未能弥合的校准差距。

Conclusion: 增益主要由"污染过滤"驱动，而非事后蒸馏本身；GT幻觉是核心影响因素。整体收益有限，频率依赖的知识迁移与校准差距被识别为未来工作方向。

Abstract: We study hindsight-guided distillation for rare disease diagnosis on ZebraMap: a 1.5B student is fine-tuned on chain-of-thought traces from a 8B teacher that observes the ground-truth diagnosis during generation. Absolute accuracy remains low for all models - the task is hard at this scale - but within this ceiling a filtered variant (StudentF) achieves a small, statistically significant accuracy advantage over the teacher (p < 0.001), concentrated in better-represented diseases. The unfiltered student does not significantly outperform the teacher (p = 0.129), establishing that contamination filtering - not hindsight distillation alone - drives the gain. The gap traces to an artifact we term GT hallucination. Label-visible generation causes the teacher to embed "ground truth is X" phrases in its reasoning chain; SFT copies the pattern. At inference, the unfiltered student reproduces the phrase in 33.9% of cases, with severe accuracy degradation when the hallucinated label is wrong. A regex filter removing these slots reduces contamination to near-zero, producing the observed gain - though the effect remains small. We precisely quantify this gain-cost tradeoff, document frequency-dependent knowledge transfer absent from the RL-trained teacher, and characterize a calibration gap that SFT does not close - identifying both as directions for future work.

</details>


### [148] [Not Until the Evidence Says So: Teaching LLM Investigators When to Close a Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03190)
*Tingzhu Bi,Ping Wang,Meng Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出并研究"案件是否足以结案"这一决策问题，构建了Nautil数据集（731个航空、铁路、海运、化工、车辆缺陷及服务器事故案例），设计三项评估（结案准确率、证据依赖性、结论与缺口质量），发现微调9B模型可让结案遵循证据，强化学习仅以结案决策为奖励可进一步提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事故、缺陷与故障调查最终都要面对一个普通问答不会遇到的决策：现有证据是否足以结案。这一判断并不随模型能力提升而自然具备——未经训练的9B模型在97%的回答中夸大证据，而能识别正确原因的前沿模型仍有91%夸大、并在官方结论为"原因未定"的案件中错误结案17/41例。同时，评估本身也很困难，因为案件来源几乎能预测标签，仅凭来源规则的基线就能达到83.0的平衡准确率，因此需要更严谨的评估设计。

Method: 构建Nautil数据集：731个来自航空、铁路、海运、化工安全和车辆缺陷报告及生产服务器事件的审计案例，包含教师轨迹、分布外测试集和反事实证据版本。评估采用三项测试：结案准确率（对照来源规则及来源内评估）、证据依赖性（移除结论依据，检验模型是否停止结案）、结论与缺口质量（评判模型断言内容与所述缺失内容）。方法上先对9B模型进行轨迹微调，再使用仅奖励结案决策的强化学习。

Result: 微调9B模型后，其结案行为遵循证据：移除依据使结案率相对匹配对照下降26个百分点，夸大陈述从97%降至35%，正确且非夸大的结论从3%升至43%。仅以结案决策为奖励的强化学习将平衡准确率从69.2提升至83.3，与教师模型相当；来源内准确率从60.4提升至74.1，但在证据依赖性上有一定代价。

Conclusion: 结案判断是一种独立于常规问答能力的决策能力，需要专门的数据与评估方法。研究表明，通过教师轨迹微调可显著改善模型遵循证据结案，而仅奖励结案决策的强化学习能进一步提升准确率，接近教师水平，但会部分牺牲证据依赖性，提示准确率与证据敏感性之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Accident, defect and outage investigations end with a decision that ordinary question answering never faces: whether the evidence gathered so far is enough to close the case. We study this decision for LLM investigators, which request evidence from a case file, revise their hypotheses, and either close the case with a conclusion grounded in what they read or leave it open and name what is missing. This judgment does not come with capability: an untrained 9B model overstates its evidence in 97% of its answers, and a frontier model that identifies the right cause in 84% of cases still overstates in 91% and closes 17 of the 41 cases whose official finding is "cause undetermined". Measuring it is also non-trivial: the source of a case largely predicts its label, and a rule that reads only the source reaches 83.0 balanced accuracy on our test cases. We therefore evaluate closure with three tests: closure accuracy, reported against this rule and within each source; evidence dependence, which removes the grounds of a conclusion and checks whether the model stops closing; and conclusion and gap quality, a judged checklist of what the model asserts and what it says is missing. We build Nautil, 731 audited cases from aviation, rail, maritime, chemical-safety and vehicle-defect reports and production server incidents, with teacher trajectories, an out-of-distribution test set and counterfactual evidence versions. Fine-tuning a 9B model on these trajectories makes its closures follow the evidence: removing the grounds lowers its closure rate by 26 points relative to a matched control, overstatement falls from 97% to 35%, and correct, non-overstated conclusions rise from 3% to 43%. Reinforcement learning that rewards only the closure decision then raises balanced accuracy from 69.2 to 83.3, on par with the teacher, and within-source accuracy from 60.4 to 74.1, at some cost in evidence dependence.

</details>


### [149] [Source Preference in the Wild: How LLM Agents Favor Items by Source, and How to Reduce It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03195)
*Jonghyun Song,Haewon Park,Jeonghoon Shim,Woojung Song,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体在端到端搜索中的"来源偏好"：12个模型在3个领域均偏好某些来源、回避另一些来源，且偏好程度可超过需求满足度本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体代替用户做购买、预订、引用等决策时，物品来源会影响用户得到什么以及哪些来源被选中，因此需要理解并刻画这种来源偏好及其成因。

Method: 在三个领域对12个智能体模型进行端到端搜索实验：比较同一位置、满足相同需求但来源不同的物品；通过隐藏来源信息、将物品重新标注为偏好来源来检验来源信息本身的作用；并测试两条成因路径（训练使来源成为需求满足的捷径；信息缺失触发对来源的先入之见）。

Result: 每个模型在每个领域都偏好某些来源、回避另一些，且模型间判断大体一致；当偏好来源的物品少满足一个需求、较差物品来自非偏好来源时，前者被选中的概率约三分之二，反向情形几乎不发生；隐藏来源信息削弱偏好，重新标注为偏好来源则提高选中率；补充缺失信息或给出对抗先入之见的提示可降低来源偏好。

Conclusion: 来源偏好普遍存在于各模型和领域，且可压倒需求满足度；它既源于训练中形成的捷径，也源于信息缺失引发的先入之见，并可通过补充信息或提示加以缓解。

Abstract: As LLM agents decide on users' behalf which product to buy, which hotel to book, or which paper to cite, a preference for items from certain sources (the sites or services they come from) shapes what users receive and which sources are selected. We study source preference in end-to-end search with 12 agent models across three domains. Comparing items from different sources that satisfy the same requirements at the same position, we find that each model prefers some sources and avoids others in every domain, largely agreeing on which. This preference can outweigh how well items satisfy the request: an item satisfying one requirement fewer is selected about two-thirds of the time when it comes from a preferred source and the better one from a dispreferred source, but almost never in the reverse case. The information identifying an item's source affects selection by itself: hiding it weakens the preference, and relabeling an item with a preferred source raises its selection rate. We test two routes to this preference: training that rewards better items can make a source a shortcut for requirement satisfaction, and missing information can trigger preconceptions about the source. Supplying missing information or a prompt countering these preconceptions reduces source preference.

</details>


### [150] [StanceEval 2026: The Second Stance Detection Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03215)
*Rasha Albalawi,Nuha Albadi,Hamzah Luqman,Asma Yamani,Maram Kurdi,Saad Ezzini,Ahmed Ashraf,Maged Al-Shaibani,Nora Alturayeif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StanceEval 2026 是阿拉伯语社交媒体立场检测共享任务的第二届，聚焦跨目标泛化，设有主题相关跨目标任务（Track 1）和完全未见目标的跨域任务（Track 2），共吸引 80 支注册队伍，最终 Track 1 有 21 队、Track 2 有 13 队获得官方排名，最佳系统 F_avg2 分别达 0.8994 和 0.9400。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立场检测旨在识别作者对特定主题的态度（支持、反对或无立场）。本届任务的核心动因是检验系统在跨目标场景下的泛化能力，即模型能否将训练中学到的立场判别能力迁移到与训练主题相关的新目标，以及完全未见的新领域目标上，从而推动阿拉伯语立场检测在真实、多样化话题中的实用性。

Method: 任务给定一条推文和一个目标，要求系统判断作者立场为 Favor、Against 或 None。评测分为两条赛道：Track 1 考察主题相关的跨目标迁移（训练数据为 Women Empowerment，测试目标为 Women Driving）；Track 2 考察向完全未见目标的跨域迁移（E-Cars 与 Trimester System）。参赛队伍采用的方法包括微调预训练语言模型、基于提示与检索增强的大语言模型（LLM）、微调 LLM 以及混合级联系统。评价指标为 F_avg2，即对 Favor 和 Against 两类取宏平均的 F1 分数。

Result: 任务共吸引来自 12 个国家的 80 支注册队伍，评测阶段有 30 支不同队伍提交结果，经有效性筛选后 Track 1 有 21 队、Track 2 有 13 队获得官方排名，另有 20 队提交了系统描述论文。最佳系统在 Track 1 取得 0.8994、在 Track 2 取得 0.9400 的 F_avg2 分数，显著优于最强基线（分别为 0.7366 和 0.7475）。值得注意的是，未见目标上的表现反而高于相关目标，这一反直觉差异可能由目标的极端极化、类别不平衡以及不同话题间的方言或讽刺性表达差异所导致。

Conclusion: StanceEval 2026 表明，当前先进方法（尤其是微调与 LLM 相关方案）在阿拉伯语跨目标立场检测上已大幅超越强基线，但跨目标泛化仍受目标极化程度、类别分布和方言/讽刺等语言现象影响，是未来研究需重点关注的方向。

Abstract: StanceEval 2026 is the second edition of the StanceEval shared task series on stance detection in Arabic social media text. Stance detection aims to identify a writer's stance toward a given topic. Given a tweet and a target, participating systems must determine whether the writer's stance is Favor, Against, or None. This edition focuses on cross-target generalization across two distinct evaluation tracks: Track 1 evaluates thematically related cross-target transfer (testing on Women Driving, related to Women Empowerment from training data), while Track 2 evaluates cross-domain transfer to completely unseen targets (E-Cars and Trimester System). The shared task attracted 80 registered teams from 12 countries. During the evaluation phase, 30 unique teams submitted entries, with 21 teams officially ranked in Track 1 and 13 in Track 2 following validation filtering, and 20 teams submitting system-description papers. Participating teams employed diverse methodologies, including fine-tuned pretrained language models, prompt-based and retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs), fine-tuned LLMs, and hybrid cascades. Top systems achieved impressive $F_{avg2}$ scores of 0.8994 on Track 1 and 0.9400 on Track 2, substantially outperforming the strongest baselines (0.7366 and 0.7475, respectively), where $F_{avg2}$ denotes the macro-averaged F1 score over the Favor and Against classes. Counterintuitively, performance on the unseen targets was higher than on the related target, a disparity could be driven by extreme target polarization, class imbalance, and dialectal or sarcastic nuance across topics.

</details>


### [151] [Collective Bias Mitigation via Model Routing and Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03240)
*Mingzhe Du,Luu Anh Tuan,Xiaobao Wu,Yichong Huang,Yue Liu,Dong Huang,Huijun Liu,Bin Ji,Jie M. Zhang,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出集合偏见缓解框架CBM，通过对多个LLM进行细粒度行为学习与知识共享来降低偏见，实验显示其显著优于单模型基线的去偏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在公共卫生、金融和治理等领域广泛应用，需兼顾准确性与社会价值对齐。但LLM常延续或放大训练数据中的偏见，损害公平性。仅依赖单模型自省式去偏（self-debiasing）难以应对根深蒂固的刻板印象。

Method: 提出Collective Bias Mitigation（CBM）框架，通过学习细粒度模型行为并促进多个不同LLM之间的知识共享来缓解偏见；系统探索不同LLM的有效选择与组织方式，设计了Debating（辩论）与Committee（委员会）等拓扑结构。

Result: CBM显著优于单模型基线。例如在top-7设置下，Committee将年龄偏见分数从0.25降至0.10；Debating与Committee拓扑均实现大幅偏见削减，其中Committee在缓解效果与推理成本之间取得较好平衡。

Conclusion: 首次系统研究多LLM的选择与组织以实现更公平的LLM响应，表明集体式知识共享与拓扑设计能有效降低偏见，CBM为构建更公平的LLM提供了有潜力的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in public health, finance, and governance, requiring both accuracy and societal value alignment. Despite recent advances, LLMs often perpetuate or amplify bias embedded in their training data, posing challenges to fairness. While self-debiasing encourages an LLM to identify and correct its own biases, relying on a single model's intrinsic knowledge may be insufficient to address deeply ingrained stereotypes. To address this limitation, we introduce Collective Bias Mitigation (CBM), a framework that alleviates bias by learning fine-grained model behavior and fostering knowledge sharing among diverse LLMs. This work is the first to systematically explore the effective selection and organization of distinct LLMs to cultivate fairer LLM responses. Experiments show CBM substantially outperforms standalone baselines (e.g., in the top-7 setting, Committee lowers the age bias score from 0.25 to 0.10). Our Debating and Committee topologies achieve substantial bias reduction, with the latter balancing mitigation effectiveness and inference cost, highlighting the potential of CBM for fairer LLMs.

</details>


### [152] [Shrome at Touché: Soft-Vote Ensembling and Counter-Causal Augmentation for Causality Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03268)
*Roham Zendehdel Nobari,Shayan Sooratgar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对 Touché 2026 的反因果主张抽取任务，分三个子任务各建一个模型，在 CCNC 测试集上检测 F1 达 0.869、极性 macro-F1 达 0.817，抽取 granularity-adjusted F1 达 0.728，为所有提交中最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻句子常表面呈因果形式而实义否定因果（如"人们误以为 X 导致 Y"）。依赖"caused""led to"等表层线索的系统会误判为因果并给出错误极性，因此需要将因果抽取扩展到反因果主张这一新设定。

Method: 每个子任务各建一个模型：检测用微调分类器，并加入一条跨任务规则，利用抽取出的跨度剔除假阳性；抽取对三个 RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF 标注器做集成，先平均 token 级分数再解码，而非对跨度投票；极性任务因标注的反因果样本最稀缺，用大语言模型按改写自 Hagen 等人的九种反因果表达模式生成训练句，仅保留通过自动结构校验的句子。

Result: 在留出的 CCNC 测试集上，检测 F1 为 0.869，极性 macro-F1 为 0.817；在组织方最终仅针对因果的抽取评测中，granularity-adjusted F1 为 0.728，为包括组织方基线在内的所有提交中最高。

Conclusion: 跨任务规则与 token 级分数集成可有效提升检测与抽取表现，基于模式提示的 LLM 生成数据能缓解反因果标注稀缺问题。开发集仅用于组件选择及论文中报告的消融实验。

Abstract: Touché 2026 extends causality extraction to counter-causal claims: news sentences whose surface form appears causal but whose meaning denies the causation, as in "It is falsely believed that X caused Y." A system that relies on surface cues such as "caused" or "led to" will accept such a sentence as causal and give it the wrong polarity. On the Countercausal News Corpus (CCNC), the task has three subtasks: deciding whether a sentence is causal (detection), locating its cause and effect spans (extraction), and labeling its polarity as procausal, counter-causal, or uncausal. We build one model per subtask. Detection is a fine-tuned classifier with a single cross-task rule that uses the extracted spans to remove false positives. For extraction, we ensemble three RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF taggers by averaging their token-level scores before decoding, rather than voting on the spans each tagger produces. For polarity, where labeled counter-causal examples are scarcest, we add training sentences generated by a large language model prompted with nine patterns of counter-causal expression adapted from Hagen et al., keeping only those that pass automatic structural checks. On the held-out CCNC test set, the system reaches F1 0.869 on detection and macro-F1 0.817 on polarity, and in the organizers' final causal-only evaluation of extraction it scores granularity-adjusted F1 0.728, the highest extraction score among all submissions including the organizers' baseline. The development split is used only for component selection and the ablations reported in the paper.

</details>


### [153] [To Jev or Not? Evaluating the Accuracy and Efficiency of Structured Decision Models for Hate-Speech Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03324)
*Demetris Paschalides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出HATEDECIDE评测框架，在4个仇恨言论数据集上比较6种结构化决策模型配置与专用审核、零样本、商用及监督基线，考察定义供给与问题分解是否能提升分类效果，并测量延迟与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线内容规模庞大使仇恨言论审核困难，而大语言模型又让有害内容更易生成与改写。审核需要能适应不同仇恨言论定义的高效分类器，因此研究者关心：接受自然语言标准并在指定答案中选择的结构化决策模型，能否在无需任务特定训练的前提下满足这些需求。

Method: 构建HATEDECIDE评测，在4个仇恨言论数据集上测试6种决策模型配置，与专用审核模型、零样本、商用及监督基线对比。实验考察两类干预：提供数据集定义，或将任务分解为多个问题；并测量其延迟与成本。

Result: 商用LLM仅在1个数据集上显著优于所有决策模型。提供定义最多改变28%的预测，但未持续提升分类效果；问题分解仅在20%的对比中显著提升性能。在诊断性测试集上，最佳托管决策模型与最佳商用LLM仅相差1.6个macro-F1点，而推理成本约低97%。

Conclusion: 结构化决策模型为低成本审核提供了机会，但显式标准与额外提问并不能可靠地改善分类。商用模型优势有限且成本高，说明在多数场景下托管决策模型是更经济的可行方案。

Abstract: The scale of online content makes hate-speech moderation challenging, while Large Language Models (LLMs) enable harmful material to be produced and adapted more easily. Moderation therefore requires efficient classifiers that can accommodate different definitions of hate speech. Recent structured decision models accept natural-language criteria and select among specified answers, raising the question of whether they can meet these requirements without task-specific training. We present HATEDECIDE, an evaluation of six decision-model configurations on four hate-speech datasets against specialized moderation, zero-shot, commercial, and supervised baselines. We examine whether supplying a dataset's definition, or decomposing it into multiple questions, improves classification, and we measure their latency and cost. We find that commercial LLMs significantly outperform all decision models on only one dataset. Supplying definitions changes up to 28\% of predictions without consistently improving classification, and decomposition significantly improves performance in only 20\% of the comparisons. On a diagnostic set of test cases, the best hosted decision model comes within 1.6 macro-F1 points of the best commercial LLM at approximately 97\% lower inference cost. These results identify opportunities for inexpensive moderation, while showing that explicit criteria and additional questions do not reliably improve classification.

</details>


### [154] [SyntaxBench: A Statistical Diagnostic Framework for Character-Level Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03329)
*Mohsen Larni,Sobhan Ebrahimi Azar,Pouyan Nahed,Kazem Taghva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SyntaxBench，一个面向字符级推理的诊断基准与统计评估框架，包含六个任务和多种统计指标，评估八个开放权重模型后得出三个主要发现：分词方式影响准确率、推理模式并非普遍有益、子串抽取任务仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地应用于小句法错误会造成影响的场景，但字符级推理目前主要通过孤立探针和总体准确率来评估，缺乏受控输入、配对检验以及对分词和推理模式的分析。

Method: 构建 SyntaxBench，包含五个核心任务（字符计数、字母包含、回文检测、编辑距离、最长字符串选择）以及更难的压力测试任务 index_to_span。五个核心任务采用配对英文与字符长度匹配的随机字符串输入，index_to_span 文档为 200-500 词且不做字符长度匹配，全部六个任务使用零样本、单样本和四样本提示。评估八个 2B 到 32B 参数的开放权重模型、共 11 种推理模式配置。评估框架报告精确匹配与宽松准确率、Cohen's kappa、配对 McNemar 检验及优势比、自助法置信区间、Kendall's tau、类别条件指标、分词分析以及多重比较校正检验。

Result: 三个主要发现：其一，分词方式影响准确率，随机字符串比英文字符串更易被字符级观察（每 token 1.892 对 3.169 个字符），且当英文单词占用更多 token 时字符计数准确率下降；其二，推理模式并非普遍有益，Gemma4-31B 在接近饱和任务上几乎不变，而 Qwen3.6-27B 在回文检测上开启思考反而更差（四样本下 0.952 非思考 对 0.886 思考）；其三，index_to_span 基本未被解决，最佳四样本精确匹配准确率仅为 6.75%。

Conclusion: 字符级评估需要受控输入、配对检验以及对分词和推理模式的分析，而不能仅依赖总体准确率。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used where small syntactic errors matter, yet character-level reasoning is still evaluated mostly through isolated probes and aggregate accuracy. We introduce SyntaxBench, a diagnostic benchmark and statistical evaluation framework for character-level reasoning. It contains five core tasks, character counting, letter containment, palindrome detection, edit distance, and longest-string selection, plus index_to_span, a harder substring-extraction stress test. The five core tasks use paired English and character-length-matched random-string inputs. index_to_span documents share a 200-500 word band and are not character-length matched. All six tasks use zero-, one-, and four-shot prompts.
  We evaluate eight open-weight models from 2B to 32B parameters across 11 reasoning-mode configurations. The framework reports exact-match and relaxed accuracy, Cohen's kappa, paired McNemar tests with odds ratios, bootstrap confidence intervals, Kendall's tau, class-conditional metrics, tokenization analysis, and multiple-comparison-corrected tests. Three findings stand out. First, tokenization shapes accuracy: random strings are more character-visible than English strings (1.892 vs. 3.169 characters per token), and character-counting accuracy falls as English words occupy more tokens. Second, reasoning mode is not uniformly helpful: Gemma4-31B is nearly unchanged across modes on the near-saturated tasks, while Qwen3.6-27B is worse with thinking on palindrome detection (0.952 non-thinking vs. 0.886 thinking at four-shot). Third, index_to_span remains largely unsolved; the best four-shot exact-match accuracy is 6.75%.
  Character-level evaluation needs controlled inputs, paired tests, and analyses of tokenization and reasoning mode rather than aggregate accuracy alone.

</details>


### [155] [CLIMB: Confidence-Guided Complementary Evidence for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03421)
*Hang Gao,Wujiang Xu,Zhixing Zhang,Kai Mei,Jingyi Yang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLIMB：一种免训练的多模态RAG推理框架，通过互补证据池构建与置信度控制的答案精炼，在百科类视觉问答上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型视觉问答往往需要图像和模型参数知识之外的文本证据。现有多模态RAG系统多依赖Top-K检索或重排序，容易返回冗余段落，且难以控制答案更新是否有充分证据支撑。

Method: CLIMB无需训练、仅在推理阶段生效。首先用MMR式目标构建紧凑的互补证据池，平衡查询相关性与段落级冗余；随后在固定池内进行置信度控制的精炼：一个R/E/C评审器从相关性、证据特异性、跨模态对齐三方面给段落打分，一个基于证据的置信度估计器仅在估计置信度提升时接受更新答案。该设计给出简单停止准则，且不修改底层检索器或MLLM。

Result: 在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek上，CLIMB持续优于检索增强的多模态基线。消融实验表明互补池化、评审器打分和迭代置信度控制精炼均对最终性能有贡献。

Conclusion: CLIMB以免训练、即插即用的方式改进了多模态RAG的推理流程，通过证据互补与置信度门控提升了知识密集型视觉问答的可靠性，且各模块均有可验证的增益。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, but knowledge-intensive visual question answering often requires external textual evidence beyond the image and the model's parametric knowledge. Existing multimodal RAG systems commonly rely on Top-$K$ retrieval or reranking, which may return redundant passages and provide limited control over whether an answer update is sufficiently supported by the retrieved evidence. We propose \textit{CLIMB}, a training-free inference-time framework for multimodal RAG. CLIMB first constructs a compact complementary evidence pool using an MMR-style objective that balances query relevance and passage-level redundancy. It then performs confidence-controlled refinement within this fixed pool: an R/E/C critic scores passages by relevance, evidence specificity, and cross-modal alignment, while an evidence-grounded confidence estimator accepts an updated answer only when the estimated confidence increases. This design provides a simple stopping criterion and reduces unnecessary refinement without modifying the underlying retriever or MLLM. Experiments on Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek show that CLIMB consistently improves over retrieval-augmented multimodal baselines. Ablations further indicate that complementary pooling, critic-based scoring, and iterative confidence-controlled refinement each contribute to the final performance.

</details>


### [156] [Single-Pass Uncertainty Heads for Claim-Level Hallucination Detection in Persian Medical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03482)
*Mehrdad Ghassabi,Pedram Rostami,Hamidreza Baradaran Kashani,Sadra Hakim,Audrina Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将LLM不确定性头(LUH)框架适配到基于Aya-Expanse-8B的波斯语医学模型,构建配对声明级幻觉数据集并训练轻量检测头,实现单次推理的声明级不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学语言模型的幻觉检测十分重要,但重复采样方法成本高昂,且现有的不确定性头资源难以直接迁移到新的骨干网络和语言。因此需要探索面向波斯语医学模型的单次推理不确定性估计方法。

Method: 以Gaokerena-V和Gaokerena-R为骨干,先在168道伊朗医学入学考试题上考察五次运行一致性;随后直接用波斯语构建两个配对的声明级幻觉数据集,每个骨干各含1600条回答;在冻结的骨干注意力图和token概率上训练轻量声明级检测头。

Result: Gaokerena-V的五次运行一致性明显低于Aya-Expanse-8B,而Gaokerena-R与之相当。在留出测试集上,两个检测头的PR-AUC分别为0.4820和0.4652,约为各自随机基线的2.30倍和2.66倍;ROC-AUC分别为0.7852和0.7810。推理时无需检索或重复采样。

Conclusion: 该工作是对波斯语医学语言模型单次推理声明级不确定性估计的初步研究,检测头有效但测试集规模较小且标签为自动生成,结果仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Hallucination detection is particularly important for medical language models, but repeated-sampling approaches are expensive and existing uncertainty-head resources do not directly transfer to a new backbone and language. We adapt the LLM Uncertainty Head (LUH) framework to Aya-Expanse-8B-based Persian medical models, using Gaokerena-V and Gaokerena-R as two previously developed backbones. We first examine response variability on a 168-question Iranian medical entrance examination and observe substantially lower five-run consistency for Gaokerena-V than for Aya-Expanse-8B, whereas Gaokerena-R is comparable to Aya-Expanse-8B. We then construct two paired claim-level hallucination datasets directly in Persian, containing 1,600 responses for each backbone, and train lightweight claim-level heads on frozen backbone attention maps and token probabilities. On held-out test splits, the heads obtain PR-AUCs of 0.4820 and 0.4652, corresponding to 2.30 and 2.66 times their respective random baselines, and ROC-AUCs of 0.7852 and 0.7810. The heads require neither retrieval nor repeated sampling at inference time. These results provide an initial study of single-pass claim-level uncertainty estimation for Persian medical language models; the test splits are small and the labels are automatically generated.

</details>


### [157] [Learning from Repaired Reasoning: Root-Cause-Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03515)
*Chenglei Shen,Haoyang Yao,Weijie Yu,Song Jin,Xiao Zhang,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出根因引导的在线策略蒸馏方法 RC-OPD，通过修复学生自身推理中的具体错误来提供指导，缓解推理不匹配与蒸馏陷阱问题，在多数据集和多模型规模上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）使用参考解答作为特权后见之明来监督学生生成的推理轨迹。但基于参考的指导可能只解释了正确解法，而未针对学生自身推理失败的原因，这种推理不匹配会让学生照搬正确结论却未解决其推理错误；同时对整条轨迹施加同样的后见之明还可能引发蒸馏陷阱，使对有效推理的多余约束与对实质性错误的纠正相互竞争。

Method: 提出 RC-OPD：针对每次失败尝试定位最早的实质性错误，进行局部修正，并以修正后的中间结果作为有效前缀的锚点；通过迭代的“诊断—修复—延续”流程，在学生延续中检验修复效果，并在固定修复预算内识别后续错误。对于能到达正确答案的修复链，根因引导蒸馏利用失败诊断和纠正目标监督错误片段，锚点引导蒸馏则用通向修复中间结果的推理链支撑相应的有效前缀。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种模型规模上进行评估，大量实验与分析表明 RC-OPD 缓解了推理不匹配和蒸馏陷阱，带来显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: RC-OPD 通过基于学生自身错误修复的根因引导监督，优于基于参考解答的 OPSD，能更有效地纠正推理错误并提升蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses reference solutions as privileged hindsight to supervise student-generated reasoning trajectories. However, reference-based guidance may explain a correct solution without addressing why the student's own reasoning fails. This reasoning mismatch between the guidance provided and the correction needed can encourage the student to borrow correct conclusions while leaving its reasoning errors unresolved. Moreover, applying the same hindsight throughout the trajectory risks a distillation trap, where unnecessary constraints on valid reasoning compete with correction of substantive errors. To address these issues, we propose Root-Cause-Guided On-Policy Distillation (RC-OPD), which uses repairs of the student's own reasoning to provide guidance that addresses its specific errors while building on valid progress. For each failed attempt, RC-OPD locates the earliest substantive error, develops a local correction, and uses the corrected intermediate result as an anchor for the valid prefix. An iterative diagnosis--repair--continuation process tests the repairs through student continuation, identifying further errors within a fixed repair budget. For repair chains that reach a correct answer, root--cause--guided distillation uses failure diagnoses and corrective goals to supervise the erroneous segments, while anchor-guided distillation supports the corresponding valid prefixes with reasoning chains leading to the repaired intermediate results. We evaluate RC-OPD across multiple datasets and model scales. Extensive experiments and analyses show that it mitigates reasoning mismatch and the distillation trap, yielding substantial performance gains.

</details>


### [158] [Structured Composition of Verifiable Atomic Insights for Table-to-Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03525)
*Teng Lin,Xinyu Liu,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ComInsight，将表格到报告生成中的洞察发现重构为原子证据的组合，构建多关系洞察图并通过组合算子生成高阶结论，在三个基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格到报告生成需要系统发现可验证的复合洞察，并组织成连贯、完整、可追溯的证据链。现有基于顺序反应式数据智能体或直接 LLM 生成的方法存在探索偏差：早期局部观察会约束后续动作，导致模型过早聚焦局部分析，遗漏跨表或跨维度证据。

Method: ComInsight 将洞察发现重构为原子证据的组合。首先定义原子洞察为符合预定义分析模式的最小可执行分析单元，并从数据库模式与内容中枚举所有有效原子洞察；随后将其组织为多关系洞察图，节点表示已验证的数据事实，边编码逻辑、时序或层次关系；最后用一组组合算子将原子节点系统融合为高阶复合结论。每个复合输出附带可执行 SQL 和细粒度溯源，保证完全可验证。

Result: 在 InsightBench、DDR-Bench 和 T2R-Bench 三个基准上，ComInsight 在事实正确性、新颖性和结构完整性方面持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: ComInsight 为表格到报告生成提供了一条可靠、高效且可解释的路径。

Abstract: Table-to-report generation refers to the task of automatically generating article-level analyt- ical reports from relational tables and is an essential capability for automated data science and decision support. Its central challenge lies in systematically discovering verifiable com- posite insights across tables, attributes, and analytical perspectives, and organizing them into coherent, complete, and traceable evidence chains. Existing methods primarily rely on sequential, reactive data agents or direct Large Language Model(LLM) generation. They suffer from exploration bias: early local observations constrain subsequent actions, causing models to focus prematurely on local analyzes and miss cross-table or cross-dimensional evidence. We propose ComInsight, which reformulates insight discovery as the composition of atomic evidences. We first define an atomic insight as the smallest executable analytical unit conforming to a predefined analysis pattern and enumerate all valid atomic insights from database schema and content. These atoms are then organized into a multi-relational insight graph, where nodes represent verified data facts and edges encode logical, temporal, or hierarchical relations. Finally, a set of composition operators systematically fuses atomic nodes into higher-order composite conclusions. Every composite output is accompanied by executable SQL and fine-grained provenance, ensuring full verifiability. Across three benchmarks InsightBench, DDR-Bench, and T2R-Bench, ComInsight consistently outperforms strong baselines in factual correctness, novelty, and structural completeness. We believe ComInsight offers a reliable, efficient, and explainable path toward table-to-report generation.

</details>


### [159] [Author Representation Strategies for Zero-Shot Authorship Attribution: A Comparative Study of LLM-Based and Embedding-Based Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03531)
*Nudrat Habib,Tosin Adewumi,Sana Sabah Al-Azzawi,Marcus Liwicki,Elisa Barney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究零样本作者归属（ZS AA）中作者表示的作用，比较标签提示、代表性写作样本、LLM生成描述和风格嵌入（LISA）四种策略，并提出两阶段嵌入框架，发现作者特定表示能显著提升性能，其中两阶段LISA框架整体最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本作者归属缺乏任务特定监督，需捕捉细粒度风格特征，极具挑战性。作者表示如何影响ZS AA性能尚不明确，因此本文探究不同作者表示策略的作用。

Method: 评估标签提示基线与三种作者表示策略：代表性写作样本、LLM生成描述、风格嵌入（LISA）。前三者通过LLM提示进行归属，嵌入方法用余弦相似度。考察提示设计影响，并提出两阶段嵌入框架，结合候选空间缩减与嵌入维度选择。

Result: 仅标签的ZS AA效果不佳；引入作者特定表示可稳定提升性能。两阶段LISA框架整体表现最强，LLM生成风格描述则以牺牲部分性能换取更紧凑的表示。

Conclusion: 作者表示对ZS AA至关重要；当前开源LLM在缺乏更有效表示学习时，仍不足以实现稳健的作者归属。

Abstract: Authorship Attribution (AA) requires capturing fine-grained stylistic characteristics, making it particularly challenging in zero-shot (ZS) settings where no task-specific supervision is available. In this work, we investigate the effect of author representations on ZS AA by evaluating a label-only prompting baseline together with three author representation strategies: representative writing samples, LLM-generated descriptions, and style embeddings (LISA). The first three approaches perform attribution using LLM prompting, while the embedding-based approach uses style embeddings with cosine similarity. We investigate the influence of prompt design and propose a two-stage embedding-based attribution framework that combines candidate space reduction with embedding-dimension selection. The results show that label-only ZS AA is ineffective, while incorporating author-specific representations consistently improves attribution performance. Among the evaluated approaches, the proposed two-stage LISA framework achieves the strongest overall performance, whereas LLM-generated style descriptions provide a substantially more compact representation of author style at the cost of some attribution performance. These findings demonstrate the importance of author representation in ZS AA, while indicating that current open-source LLMs remain insufficient for robust attribution without more effective representation learning.

</details>


### [160] [Writerslogic at PAN 2026: Process over Content for Robust Detection under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03565)
*David L. Condrey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Writerslogic 系统参加 PAN at CLEF 2026 三项共享任务的方案，核心思想是：特征在分布偏移下的鲁棒性取决于训练与测试分布的支持重叠度，而非训练集效应量，并据此提出领域锚定、领域可迁移、领域不变的三分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望解释并解决一个现象：在跨领域场景下，依赖生成器特定特征的方法会失效，而词汇指纹、压缩度量与字符 n-gram 等特征却能够存活。其动机是把特征鲁棒性问题从"训练集效应量"转向"训练与测试分布的支持重叠"，从而为三个风格分析与检测任务提供统一的分析框架。

Method: 论文围绕统一框架设计三套系统：(1) 推理轨迹检测任务中，训练集全为数学领域而测试集 84% 为未见领域，框架指导采用 Opus-Sonnet 一致性进行来源检测、查询拒答分解进行安全分类；(2) Voight-Kampff 生成式 AI 检测中，构建 DeBERTa-v2(ONNX)、多随机种子 LightGBM（44 个领域可迁移风格计量特征）与 n-gram TF-IDF 上的 SVM 的校准集成，通过学习式堆叠与等渗校准合并；(3) 多作者写作风格分析中，融合字符 n-gram 相似图上的谱聚类、归一化压缩距离局部边界检测与 SmolLM-135M 困惑度神经变点检测。

Result: 推理轨迹检测任务中，来源检测获第一名（宏 F1 0.85），安全分类获第三名（宏 F1 0.66）。Voight-Kampff 任务中最佳配置在 PAN 2026 测试集上达到 0.891，各评估维度子指标均衡（0.853 至 0.902）。多作者写作风格分析系统因平台混淆未能进入官方评测，故仅报告设计与先验预测。

Conclusion: 三个任务共同表明，在分布偏移下，衡量生成过程属性的特征设计上优于衡量生成内容属性的特征；特征鲁棒性由训练与测试分布的支持重叠度决定，而非训练集效应量。该三分类体系（领域锚定、领域可迁移、领域不变）能够解释并指导跨领域系统设计。

Abstract: We describe the Writerslogic systems for three PAN at CLEF 2026 shared tasks (Reasoning Trajectory Detection, Voight-Kampff Generative AI Detection, and Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis), unified by a shared analytical framework: feature robustness under distribution shift is governed by support overlap between training and test distributions, not by training-set effect size. This yields a taxonomy (domain-anchored, domain-portable, domain-invariant) that explains why generator-specific features die under domain shift while vocabulary fingerprints (hapax ratio, Yule's K, Heaps' exponent), compression measures, and character n-grams survive. On Reasoning Trajectory Detection, where training was entirely mathematics and 84 percent of test was unseen domains, the framework guided system design to 1st place in source detection (0.85 macro F1 via Opus-Sonnet agreement) and 3rd place in safety classification (0.66 macro F1 via query-refusal decomposition). For Voight-Kampff, we built a calibrated ensemble of DeBERTa-v2 (ONNX), multi-seed LightGBM with 44 domain-portable stylometric features, and SVM on n-gram TF-IDF, combined via learned stacking with isotonic calibration; the best configuration achieved 0.891 on the PAN 2026 test set with balanced sub-metrics (0.853 to 0.902 across all evaluation dimensions). For Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis, we describe a system fusing spectral clustering over character n-gram similarity graphs, normalized compression distance for local boundary detection, and SmolLM-135M perplexity for neural change-point detection; a platform mix-up meant our run never reached the official evaluation, so we report the design and its a priori predictions. Across all three tasks, features measuring generation process properties are designed to outperform features measuring generated content properties under domain shift.

</details>


### [161] [Writerslogic at the CLEF 2026 SimpleText Track: Multi-Candidate LLM Simplification and Stacked Complexity Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03567)
*David L. Condrey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Writerslogic 团队参加 CLEF 2026 SimpleText 任务，在文本简化任务中取得句子级 SARI 最高的系统排名，在复杂内容识别任务中取得识别赛道第一的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向 Cochrane 系统综述的英文及多语言生物医学文本，需要提升文本可读性与识别生成内容中的幻觉/过度生成问题，因此参与 CLEF 2026 SimpleText 的文本简化与复杂性识别两项任务。

Method: 任务1采用 GPT-4o-mini 多候选生成流程，每句在不同温度下生成五个简化候选，并用无参考评分启发式（奖励压缩、原词保留、Cochrane 简明语言摘要词汇使用及词汇简洁性）选择最佳候选；任务2将幻觉检测建模为自然语言推理，基于 35 万对标注(源句, 候选句)微调 DeBERTa-v3-large NLI 模型，以最相关源句为前提、候选为假设进行判断。

Result: 任务1.1 句子级简化中 Claude Sonnet 4 系统获得 SARI 47.43、BLEU 14.21，为句子级排名第一（合并榜单第三，位列两个文档级系统之后）；任务2.1 二分类过度生成识别中微调 DeBERTa 达到文档级 macro F1 0.8081（最佳集成 0.8085），为识别赛道第一、团队总排名第二（仅次于 AIIR Lab 0.8197）；任务2.2 多类错误分类最佳提交准确率 0.804，在独立团队中排名第二（仅次于 AIIR Lab 0.827）。

Conclusion: 该团队在 CLEF 2026 SimpleText 两项任务中均取得领先成绩，表明多候选生成加无参考启发式选择以及基于 NLI 微调的 DeBERTa 方法在生物医学文本简化与幻觉识别中有效。

Abstract: We describe the Writerslogic team's participation in the CLEF 2026 SimpleText shared task, addressing Task 1 (text simplification) and Task 2 (complexity spotting). For Task 1, we develop a multi-candidate generation pipeline using GPT-4o-mini that produces five simplification candidates per sentence at varying temperatures, then selects the best candidate using a reference-free scoring heuristic that rewards compression, source word retention, Cochrane Plain Language Summary vocabulary usage, and lexical simplicity. On Task 1.1 (sentence-level simplification), our Claude Sonnet 4 submission achieves SARI 47.43 and BLEU 14.21, the top-ranked sentence-level system (3rd on the combined Task 1 leaderboard, behind two document-level submissions). For Task 2, we fine-tune a DeBERTa-v3-large NLI model on 350K labeled (source, sentence) pairs, framing hallucination detection as natural language inference. The model reads the most relevant source sentence as premise and the candidate as hypothesis, directly learning to distinguish grounded from hallucinated content. On Task 2.1 (binary overgeneration identification), our fine-tuned DeBERTa system achieves 0.8081 document-level macro F1 (0.8085 in our best ensemble), the top-ranked entry within the identification track and 2nd among teams overall, behind AIIR Lab (0.8197). On Task 2.2 (multi-class error classification), our best submission reaches 0.804 multiclass accuracy, ranking 2nd among unique teams behind AIIR Lab (0.827). We evaluate both tasks on English and multilingual biomedical text from Cochrane systematic reviews.

</details>


### [162] [FALCON: A Model and Dataset Agnostic Framework for Synthetic Data Generation for NL2SQL Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03625)
*Darian Lee,Shannon Rumsey,Jack St. Clair,Xinyi Tang,Aditya Bansal,Yuanming Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FALCON框架，用紧凑开源模型低成本生成真实、含歧义的NL-to-SQL训练数据，其数据在SQL复杂度和自然语言丰富度上超越现有基准，训练效果随查询复杂度提升而更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL-to-SQL合成数据生成方法忽视了真实世界中请求表述的歧义性，生成的查询过于简化，无法让模型应对真实结构化知识访问的复杂性。同时，高质量训练数据生成通常成本高昂。

Method: FALCON结合保留字SQL种子（reserved-word SQL seeding）与基于人设的提示（persona-based prompting）生成结构复杂的查询；再用基于对齐的过滤（alignment-based filtering）区分真正错误的样本与复杂但有效的查询，从而保留数据难度。整个流程使用紧凑开源模型、与模型和数据库无关。

Result: 人工评估证实不同模型规模下数据质量稳定且高；生成数据在SQL复杂度和自然语言丰富度上均超过现有基准。难度分层分析显示，随着查询复杂度提升，用FALCON数据训练的模型日益优于基线训练的模型。与少量现有基准数据混合训练，可在简单查询上恢复性能，同时保持复杂查询上的优势。

Conclusion: FALCON能以低成本、本地化的方式生成高复杂度且含歧义的NL-to-SQL训练数据，模型和数据库无关的设计使组织无需外部API即可自行生成，为真实场景结构化知识访问提供了有效训练方案。

Abstract: Relational databases are among the most widely deployed forms of structured knowledge, and natural language access to them requires grounding language onto schema entities and relations while handling the ambiguity inherent in how people phrase requests. Existing synthetic NL-to-SQL data generation methods largely ignore this ambiguity and produce oversimplified queries that fail to prepare models for the complexity of real-world structured knowledge access. We present FALCON, a framework that generates realistic, ambiguity-aware NL-to-SQL data matching the complexity of challenging real-world benchmarks, at low cost using compact open models. Our approach combines reserved-word SQL seeding and persona-based prompting to generate structurally complex queries, while alignment-based filtering preserves difficulty by distinguishing genuinely incorrect examples from complex but valid queries. Human evaluation confirms consistent high quality across model sizes, and our generated data exceeds existing benchmarks in both SQL complexity and natural language richness. Difficulty-stratified analysis shows models trained on FALCON data increasingly outperform baseline-trained models as query complexity increases, validating our pipeline's success in generating challenging training data. When combined with a small proportion of existing benchmark data, mixed training recovers performance on simpler queries while preserving these advantages on complex ones. The model- and database-agnostic design enables organizations to generate high-complexity NL-to-SQL training data locally without external APIs.

</details>


### [163] [Language Models that Play Chess and Explain Their Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03695)
*Adithya Bhaskar,Jeffrey Cheng,Danqi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Queen，一个 4B 参数的国际象棋语言模型，能在达到特级大师水平对弈的同时解释其走法与计划，通过在棋力与解释质量上超越前沿模型，验证了"静默专家编码器+语言模型"的通用配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代棋类引擎棋力超人却无法解释走法，而语言模型虽能生成看似合理的解释，但棋力太弱，使解释缺乏实用价值。作者希望同时获得超人棋力与高质量自然语言解释。

Method: 框架包含两个互补组件：一是编码器-解码器架构，将静默的国际象棋专家编码器通过交叉注意力与指令微调的语言模型整合，并用问答课程训练以从编码器表示中提取棋类概念；二是迭代蒸馏算法，采用"贝尔曼更新的自然语言类比"——模型分析其最优候选走法之后局面并整合为对当前局面的解释，再蒸馏回模型，如此迭代。

Result: 经过七轮迭代，模型棋力从 1782 Elo 提升到 2697 Elo，增加逾 900 分，尽管参数量少三个数量级，仍在棋力与谜题准确率上大幅超越所有前沿模型；基于语言模型的评测显示解释流畅，连贯性接近 GPT-5.6-Sol (high)。

Conclusion: 该架构与训练流程具有通用性，为在具备静默专家编码器的领域（如游戏、机器人、计算机操作）应用语言模型提供了可推广的配方。

Abstract: Modern chess engines are silent experts: they play at a superhuman level, but do not offer explanations for their play. On the other hand, language models (LMs) can generate plausible-sounding explanations, but their weak playing strength limits the utility of their explanations. We introduce Queen, a 4B-parameter chess-language model that can explain its moves and plans while playing at the level of a typical Grandmaster. Our novel framework enables domain-specific reasoning through complementary components: an encoder-decoder architecture and an iterative distillation algorithm. This architecture integrates a silent expert chess encoder with an instruction-tuned LM through cross-attention, which we train via a question-answering curriculum to extract chess concepts from the encoder's representations. Building on this domain-adapted model, we iteratively improve its explanations with a natural-language analog of the Bellman update: the model analyzes the positions after its top candidate moves and consolidates them into an explanation of the current position, which is then distilled back into the model. Over seven iterations, our model gains over 900 Elo points (1782 to 2697), substantially surpassing all frontier models on both playing strength and puzzle accuracy, despite containing three orders of magnitude fewer parameters. Furthermore, LM-based evaluations show that our explanations are fluent and approach GPT-5.6-Sol (high) in coherence. The generality of our architecture and training procedure suggests a recipe for applying language models to domains where silent expert encoders are available, like games, robotics, and computer use.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Turnover-Orthogonal Credit Assignment for Open-Team Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02847)
*Amit Thakur,Mukesh Singhal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出TOCA（turnover-orthogonal credit assignment），一种用于开放团队多智能体强化学习的价值分解方法，将动作效应、纯更替效应和动作-更替交互效应分离，避免幸存智能体因不可控的更替事件被错误奖惩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放团队中智能体可能在episode中进入、离开或被替换，团队回报的变化既来自动作选择也来自活跃种群本身的变化。标准的集中式critic和共享优势将两种效应混为一个标量信用信号，导致幸存智能体因外部更替事件（不可控）被奖励或惩罚，信用分配不合理。

Method: 提出TOCA价值分解，将价值拆分为动作效应、纯更替效应和动作-更替交互三部分。在外生更替下，事件条件价值存在中心化分解，其事件条件基线可去除纯更替成分，同时保留对使团队对未来的替换产生鲁棒性动作的信用。实现上采用对变尺寸智能体集合与事件token的置换不变集中式critic，推导出反事实的逐智能体信用信号，以及用于高方差控制环境的软加权交互变体TOCA-β。

Result: 受控诊断实验表明，TOCA相较事件感知的MAPPO式critic提升了回报，且移除交互信用会显著损害性能。仅在替换的Dynamic Spread基准中，TOCA-β在高更替率下取得最佳平均回报，并优于其无交互消融版本。

Conclusion: 显式地将更替效应与动作信用分离，是动态协作团队中实现鲁棒学习的一条有用原则。

Abstract: Open-team multi-agent reinforcement learning studies cooperative systems in which agents may join, leave, or be replaced during an episode. In such settings, the team return changes both because agents choose useful actions and because the active population itself changes. Standard centralized critics and shared advantages often mix these two effects into one scalar credit signal, allowing surviving agents to be rewarded or penalized for exogenous turnover events outside their control. We introduce turnover-orthogonal credit assignment (TOCA), a value decomposition for open teams that separates action effects, pure turnover effects, and action--turnover interactions. Under exogenous turnover, the event-conditioned value admits a centered decomposition whose event-conditioned baseline removes the pure turnover component while preserving credit for actions that make the team robust to future replacements. We instantiate this idea with a permutation-invariant centralized critic over variable-size agent sets and event tokens, and derive both a counterfactual per-agent credit signal and a softly weighted interaction variant, TOCA-$β$, for high-variance control environments. Controlled diagnostic experiments show that TOCA improves return over event-aware MAPPO-style critics and that removing interaction credit substantially hurts performance. In a replacement-only Dynamic Spread benchmark, TOCA-$β$ achieves the best mean return at high turnover rates and improves over its no-interaction ablation. These results suggest that explicitly separating turnover from action credit is a useful principle for robust learning in dynamic cooperative teams.

</details>


### [165] [Evaluator-in-the-Loop Monte Carlo Tree Search via LLM Agents for Motif Scaffolding in Protein Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02924)
*Haotian Hu,Oguzhan Gungordo,Siheng Xiong,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ELMS 是一种"评估器在环"的蒙特卡洛搜索框架，将结构评估反馈转化为针对性的设计动作，显著提升 motif scaffolding 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 motif-scaffolding 采用"先生成后筛选"范式，候选蛋白独立生成，结构评估仅用于末端筛选或排序。这种范式低估了评估的价值：失败的预测中其实包含关于设计需要修复 motif 几何、全局可折叠性等状态相关证据。同时，不同骨架状态有不同失败模式，反复精修单条轨迹会过早锁定在无产出的序列空间区域。

Method: ELMS 将已评估的骨架保留为持久搜索状态：Critic Agent 诊断状态局部的结构失败；Policy Agent 选择带执行参数的针对性算子；motif 锁定的算子执行合法的序列修改；MCTS 决定哪些历史状态应获得进一步设计投入。

Result: 在标准 GeomMotif 协议下（每任务 100 个候选），单 motif 任务成功率 86.41%、配对 motif 任务 84.57%，分别超过最强基线 19.3 和 21.9 个百分点。在 MotifBench 上，匹配 100 候选搜索预算时，平均解决 30 个任务中的 26.7 个（任务成功率 88.89%），而最强基线为 16.0 个（53.33%）。

Conclusion: ELMS 能有效将结构评估从末端过滤器转变为迭代 motif scaffolding 的可执行指导，是一种有效的方法。

Abstract: Motif-scaffolding systems commonly follow a generate-then-filter paradigm, in which candidate proteins are generated independently and structural evaluation is used primarily for terminal screening or ranking. This paradigm underuses evaluation: failed predictions contain state-specific evidence about whether a design requires repair of motif geometry, global foldability, or other structural constraints. We introduce \textbf{ELMS} (Evidence-based LLM-guided Monte Carlo Search), an evaluator-in-the-loop search framework for motif scaffolding that turns such evaluator feedback into targeted design actions. Effective reuse of structural feedback is nontrivial because different scaffold states exhibit different failure modes, and repeatedly refining a single trajectory can prematurely commit computation to an unproductive region of sequence space. ELMS therefore retains evaluated scaffolds as persistent search states: a Critic Agent diagnoses state-local structural failures, a Policy Agent selects targeted operators with execution parameters, motif-locked operators realize legal sequence modifications, and MCTS determines which historical states should receive further design effort. Under the standard GeomMotif protocol (100 candidates per task), ELMS achieves Successful rates of 86.41\% on single-motif tasks and 84.57\% on paired-motif tasks, exceeding the strongest prior baseline by 19.3 and 21.9 percentage points, respectively. On MotifBench, under a matched 100-candidate search budget, it solves 26.7 of 30 tasks on average (88.89\% Task Success), compared with 16.0 tasks (53.33\%) for the strongest baseline. These results establish ELMS as an effective approach for converting structural evaluation from a terminal filter into actionable guidance for iterative motif scaffolding.

</details>


### [166] [FinNextAssist: Towards Professional Financial Deep Research Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03174)
*Xiangyu Li,Fengbin Zhu,Xuan Yao,Siyu Liu,Xiaoluan Liu,Chao Wang,Huanbo Luan,Xiaofen Xing,Xiangmin Xu,Ke-Wei Huang,Richang Hong,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出FinNextAssist，一个面向专业金融分析的端到端深度研究（DR）框架，通过四阶段流程与两个轻量级子代理，在多个金融基准上超越现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究代理在复杂研究任务中表现优异，但迁移到金融领域面临独特挑战：金融分析需要联合完成异构子任务，涉及多样的数据类型、工具与分析流程。作者据此识别出专业金融DR代理的三项关键需求：整合权威异构金融数据源、配备专门的分析工具与技能、以及为领域特定子任务设置专用子代理。

Method: 提出FinNextAssist端到端深度研究框架。架构上将研究过程分解为四个阶段：任务规划器（Task Planner）、证据编译器（Evidence Compiler）、推理引擎（Reasoning Engine）与报告组装器（Report Assembler）；并引入两个新型轻量级子代理：TabAgent（面向跨市场金融表格理解）与HeteroAgent（面向跨模态异构金融数据解读）。

Result: 在FinDeepResearch、Finance Agent Benchmark与FinTMMBench-Web三个基准上的大量实验表明，FinNextAssist显著优于强专有及开源DR代理；消融实验验证了各组件在不同市场与语言场景下的贡献。

Conclusion: 面向金融的深度研究代理需要数据、工具与子代理三方面的专门化设计；四阶段流程配合表格与异构数据两个子代理的框架，能够有效提升专业金融分析的整体性能，且各模块均具必要性。

Abstract: Deep Research (DR) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex, research-oriented tasks through autonomous planning, iterative retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and structured reporting. However, adapting DR agents to finance introduces unique challenges: financial analysis demands the joint completion of heterogeneous sub-tasks spanning diverse data types, tools, and analytical workflows. We identify three key requirements for a professional financial DR agent: integration of authoritative, heterogeneous financial data sources; specialized analytical tools and skills; and dedicated sub-agents for domain-specific sub-tasks. Building on these principles, we propose FinNextAssist, an end-to-end deep research framework designed for professional financial analysis. FinNextAssist decomposes the research process into four stages: Task Planner, Evidence Compiler, Reasoning Engine, and Report Assembler, and introduces two novel lightweight sub-agents: TabAgent, for cross-market financial table understanding, and HeteroAgent, for cross-modality heterogeneous financial data interpretation. Extensive experiments on FinDeepResearch, the Finance Agent Benchmark, and FinTMMBench-Web show that FinNextAssist substantially outperforms both strong proprietary and open-source DR agents, with ablation studies confirming the contribution of each component across diverse markets and languages.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [MintFlow: Minimal Trajectory Intervention for Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02260)
*Yesom Park,Kelvin Kan,Qifan Chen,Thomas Flynn,Hayden Schaeffer. Xihaier Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MintFlow是一种无需训练的约束采样框架，将约束施加建模为对预训练流轨迹的最小干预，通过伴随公式得到闭式扰动，在满足约束的同时更好地保持预训练分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配约束采样器面临权衡：强化约束会使样本明显偏离预训练数据分布。作者希望同时实现约束满足与分布保持。

Method: 将约束施加转化为对中间流状态的最小扰动问题，保持预训练流场不变；利用伴随（adjoint）公式推导扰动的闭式解，避免昂贵的迭代优化；并自适应选择干预时刻，以平衡扰动幅度与其被剩余流放大的效应。整个方法无需训练。

Result: 在生成视觉与物理系统建模等多类任务上，MintFlow在约束满足方面具有竞争力，同时比当前最先进的约束方法更好地保持预训练生成分布。

Conclusion: MintFlow通过最小扰动的闭式干预与自适应干预时刻选择，有效缓解了约束满足与分布保持之间的权衡，是一种高效、无需训练的约束采样方案。

Abstract: Flow matching models excel at generative modeling, and many downstream applications require their samples to satisfy prescribed constraints, such as observed measurements and physical laws. However, existing constrained samplers often face a trade-off: \textit{enforcing constraints can substantially displace samples from the pretrained data distribution}. To address this trade-off, we introduce \textbf{MintFlow}, a training-free constrained sampling framework that formulates constraint enforcement as a minimal intervention on the pretrained flow trajectory. MintFlow seeks the minimal perturbation of an intermediate flow state such that its subsequent evolution under the pretrained flow field satisfies the target constraint. By minimally perturbing the flow state while keeping the pretrained flow field unchanged, MintFlow enforces the constraint while minimizing unnecessary deviation from the pretrained distribution. An adjoint formulation yields a closed-form expression for this perturbation, eliminating expensive iterative optimization. Furthermore, MintFlow adaptively selects the intervention time to balance the required perturbation magnitude with its amplification by the remaining flow. Across a range of tasks in generative vision and physical system modeling, MintFlow achieves competitive constraint satisfaction while preserving the pretrained generative distribution substantially better than state-of-the-art constrained methods.

</details>


### [168] [Fast Models, Slow Evidence: A Paired and Self-Audited Evaluation of System-1 Decision Models for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02267)
*Jiawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对两个System-1决策模型（开源Laya与托管Jev）在11个智能体决策点上做了配对评测，发现Jev多数场景更准，并公开审计了自身流程中的4处分析偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架每步都需大量小型类型化决策（选模型、选工具、判断检索相关性、检测注入），System-1模型以单次前向传播给出类别概率，理论上比调用LLM省成本、降延迟，但缺少严格可控的对比评测与自查。

Method: 构建来自18个公开来源的11个智能体决策点，含7,283个基础用例与6,640个鲁棒性变体；保证字节级一致输入、配对检验，并做跨硬件、跨天可复现性检查；对比开源Laya与托管Jev两个System-1模型，并审计自身分析流程。

Result: Jev在11个决策点中的9个显著更准（+10.8至+46.0个百分点）；两者在零样本模型路由上均未超随机，在RAG相关性门控上持平。Laya在选项顺序反转时改变30%答案，候选多或相似时性能骤降（50个最近邻工具时31%，而Jev在唯一正确工具项上为98%）。审计发现3处分析错误与1处设计混淆：漏算预筛成本（报称省23.9%，实际4.3%）、把门控准确率当作端到端质量（58% vs 98%）、样本内阈值（目标5%，留出最多17%漏检）、注入误报的“通道效应”在原生内容下消失；另两处疑似混淆未改变结论。

Conclusion: System-1模型在智能体决策上仍不可靠（路由、候选扩展、顺序敏感性等），且系统评测极易被分析选择放大结论；作者公开全部用例、原始输出与分析代码以支持可复现评测。

Abstract: Agent harnesses make many small, typed decisions per task: which model to call, which tool to use, whether retrieved text is relevant, whether an input carries an injection. System-1 decision models answer such questions in a single forward pass with class probabilities, promising large cost and latency savings over LLM calls. We present a paired evaluation of an open-weight (Laya) and a hosted (Jev) System-1 model on 11 agent decision points built from 18 public sources: 7,283 base cases plus 6,640 robustness variants, with byte-identical inputs, paired tests, and cross-hardware and cross-day reproducibility checks. Jev is significantly more accurate on 9 of 11 decision points (+10.8 to +46.0 pp). Neither model beats chance on zero-shot model routing, and they tie on RAG relevance gating. Laya changes 30% of its answers when the option order is reversed and degrades sharply with many or similar candidates (31% at 50 nearest-neighbour tools, vs. 98% for Jev on items with a unique correct tool). We also audit our own pipeline. Three analysis errors and one design confound distorted headline deployment claims: an omitted pre-screen cost (reported 23.9% saving, actual 4.3%), gate accuracy reported as end-to-end quality (58% vs. 98%), in-sample thresholds (5% target, up to 17% held-out misses), and a "channel effect" on injection false positives that vanishes with channel-native content. Two other suspected confounds did not change the conclusions. All cases, raw outputs and analysis code are available at https://github.com/David-DL-Space/sys1-eval.

</details>


### [169] [The AI Risk Observatory: What Can We Learn from AI Disclosures in Annual Reports About Societal Resilience?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02281)
*Bart Jaworski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究用LLM流水线对9,821份英国上市公司年报进行两阶段分类，发现AI风险披露从2020年2.8%升至2025年41.2%，但仅4.3%为实质性披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会韧性研究需要有用且可操作的数据，因此作者提出核心问题：大规模用LLM处理年报，能否提供关于企业如何披露其应对AI的有用信号。

Method: 采用可复现的两阶段分类流水线，处理1,362家英国上市公司2020-2025年（含部分2026年）的9,821份年报；先用474段人工标注文本验证方法，再构建"实质性"分类以评估披露质量。

Result: 方法验证显示高召回率、标签级一致性中等。三项实证发现：(i) 提及AI风险的比例从2020年2.8%升至2025年41.2%，AI采用披露从13.8%升至45.2%，供应商提及集中于少数主要厂商（以微软为首）；(ii) 披露按关键国家基础设施行业和市场板块差异显著，AIM市场AI风险披露率远低于主板，能源与数据基础设施等行业落后；(iii) 危害披露几乎缺失（全语料仅7份报告）。2025年41.2%报告提及AI风险，但仅4.3%为实质性披露。

Conclusion: 年报结合LLM可提供企业AI披露的可扩展信号，揭示披露快速增长但多数缺乏实质性，且存在行业与市场板块间的显著差异。

Abstract: Societal resilience research relies on access to useful and actionable data, which motivates our main research question: Can annual reports, processed at scale with LLMs, provide a useful signal about how companies disclose their response to AI? We test this by applying a reproducible two-stage classification pipeline to 9,821 annual reports from 1,362 UK listed companies (2020-2025, with partial 2026 data). We first validate the method against 474 human-annotated passages, finding high recall and moderate label-level agreement. We then report three empirical patterns: (i) between 2020 and 2025, the share of reports mentioning AI risk rose from 2.8% to 41.2%, while AI adoption disclosure also rose, from 13.8% to 45.2%, and named vendor mentions cluster around a small set of major providers led by Microsoft; (ii) disclosure varies substantially by Critical National Infrastructure sector and market segment: AIM reports disclose AI risk at far lower rates than Main Market reports, and sectors such as Energy and Data Infrastructure lag behind the rest in AI risk disclosure; and (iii) harm disclosures are near-absent (seven reports across the entire corpus). We develop a substantiveness classification to assess the quality of the disclosure and find that most AI risk disclosure is not substantive: in 2025, 41.2% of all reports mention AI as a risk, but only 4.3% contain AI risk disclosure we classify as substantive.

</details>


### [170] [Keep It CALM: Analyzing the Limits of Global Unsafety in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02300)
*NaHyeon Park,Minhyun Lee,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出CALM方法，用提示局部的反事实校正替代全局不安全信号移除，实现免训练的文本生成图像安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练安全防护依赖可复用的全局安全信号（如不安全方向或全局有害子空间），但作者通过受控的几何分析发现，这种全局不安全假设存在稳定的覆盖-选择性权衡：紧凑的不安全子空间无法覆盖多样化的不安全语义，而更宽泛的聚合则会逐渐扭曲与安全相邻的良性提示。因此需要一种更具选择性的替代方案。

Method: 提出CALM（反事实自适应局部调制），一种免训练防护方法。它用提示局部的反事实校正取代统一的全局移除：利用配对的不安全-良性锚点，为每个提示路由到实际激活的不安全类别，仅对违规的token表示向安全一侧做最小化编辑，并抑制正向对齐的不安全残差分量。

Result: 在广泛评估中，CALM在显著提升不安全内容抑制效果的同时，保持了良性提示的可用性，优于全局不安全信号移除方法。

Conclusion: 局部反事实校正为全局不安全信号移除提供了一种更具选择性的替代方案，证明该思路在安全性和良性效用之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Training-free safeguards for text-to-image generation often rely on a reusable safety signal, such as an unsafe direction or global toxic subspace, applied broadly across prompts. We provide a controlled geometric analysis of this global-unsafety assumption and reveal a consistent coverage-selectivity trade-off: compact unsafe subspaces fail to cover heterogeneous unsafe semantics, whereas broader aggregation increasingly distorts safety-adjacent benign prompts. Motivated by this finding, we propose CALM (Counterfactual Adaptive Local Modulation), a training-free safeguard that replaces uniform global removal with prompt-local counterfactual correction. Using matched unsafe-benign anchors, CALM routes each prompt to active unsafe categories, minimally edits only violating token representations toward the safe side, and suppresses positively aligned unsafe residual components. Across broad evaluation, CALM significantly improves unsafe content suppression while preserving benign utility, demonstrating that local counterfactual correction provides a more selective alternative to global unsafe signal removal.

</details>


### [171] [Choosing Before Acting: Comparative Value Estimation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02330)
*Yu Li,Zheng Zhang,Xin Liu,Shengtian Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CITA方法，通过训练比较推理模型（CIM）来估计长程工具调用中下一步工具调用的长期价值，从而改进LLM工具使用智能体的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在复杂任务中依赖长程工具调用序列，但最终结果奖励难以对长交互轨迹进行有效的信用分配。步骤级奖励虽能提供更精准的反馈，却往往依赖人工或LLM判断，或需要额外rollout来估计中间决策的下游影响。因此，需要在执行前估计候选工具调用的长期价值，而这需要同一上下文下对替代调用的比较监督，但日志轨迹仅包含实际执行的调用。

Method: 提出CITA，训练比较推理模型（CIM）。CIM从配对信号中学习，这些信号结合了观察到的工具行为、来自贝叶斯工具图模拟器的可扩展监督，以及基于LLM比较的语义判断。CIM学习估计在当前上下文下，某个候选的下一个工具调用支持最终任务成功的可能性。

Result: 在三个工具使用基准和多个骨干LLM上，CITA一致提升了Tool F1和任务成功率。额外分析表明，CIM能为比较性工具选择学习准确的步骤级价值估计。

Conclusion: CITA通过比较推理为长程工具使用提供有效的步骤级价值监督，无需依赖昂贵的人工标注或额外rollout，在多个基准和模型上稳定提升工具使用性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on long-horizon tool invocation sequences for complex tasks, where each invocation can alter the task state and condition subsequent decisions. In long-horizon tool use, final-outcome rewards provide weak credit assignment over long interaction traces. Step-level rewards can offer more targeted feedback, but obtaining reliable step supervision often requires human or LLM judgment, or additional rollouts to estimate the downstream effect of an intermediate decision. In this paper, we argue that effective tool-use agents should estimate the long-horizon value of a possible next tool invocation before executing it. This objective requires comparative supervision over alternative invocations under the same context, while logged trajectories only contain the invocation that was actually taken. Therefore, we propose Comparative Inference for Tool-use Agents (CITA). CITA trains a Comparative Inference Model (CIM) from paired signals that combine observed tool behavior, scalable supervision from a Bayesian tool-graph simulator, and semantic judgments from LLM-based comparison. The resulting CIM learns to estimate how likely a possible next tool invocation is to support final task success under the current context. Across three tool-use benchmarks and multiple backbone LLMs, CITA consistently improves Tool F1 and task success. Additional analysis shows that CIM learns accurate step-level value estimates for comparative tool choices.

</details>


### [172] [Coherence-Driven Belief Formation and Population Dynamics of Contagion in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02654)
*Tathagata Banerjee,Nima Moghaddas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过实证方法测量语言模型智能体的信念采纳概率，发现其采纳核呈S型（复杂 contagion 特征），阈值受三种因素影响并可归约为单一"有效维度"（与LLM先验信念的连贯性），且在AI智能体集体动力学中观察到复杂 contagion 特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社会 contagion 模型通常假设个体如何采纳信念，并据此推导群体行为，而缺乏对信念采纳过程的实证测量。本文旨在对语言模型智能体中的信念采纳进行实证量化，弥补这一空白。

Method: 实证测量语言模型智能体的信念采纳：量化智能体在有多少同伴支持某主张时采纳该主张的概率，即采纳核。分析阈值对三种来源的敏感性：主张的合理性、来源的可靠性、智能体的倾向性。并在AI智能体系统中观察集体信念采纳动力学，比较聚类网络与随机网络上的传播差异。

Result: 采纳核呈S型（复杂 contagion 特征）。阈值对主张合理性、来源可靠性和智能体倾向性敏感，这三个维度可很好地近似为单一有效维度，可理解为传入信念与LLM智能体先验信念的连贯性。在集体动力学中：聚类网络上的传播强于随机网络；系统表现出分岔的级联窗口和自维持的滞回共识。

Conclusion: 语言模型智能体的信念采纳呈现复杂 contagion 特征，其阈值可归约为单一有效维度（信念连贯性）。集体层面表现出聚类网络优先传播、分岔级联窗口及滞回共识，导致共识的移除远比建立困难。

Abstract: Models of social contagion usually assume how individuals adopt beliefs and derive population behavior from it. We instead empirically measure belief adoption in language model agents, quantifying the probability an agent adopts a claim given how many peers endorse it. We find this adoption kernel to be sigmoid, a characteristic of complex contagion, with a threshold that is sensitive to three sources: the claim's plausibility, the source's reliability, and the agent's disposition. These three dimensions are well approximated by a single effective dimension which we propose can be understood as the coherence of the incoming belief with the LLM agent's prior beliefs. Further, we observe a characteristic of complex contagion in the collective dynamics of belief adoption in a system of AI agents: further spread on clustered than random networks. These systems also exhibit a bifurcating cascade window, and self-sustaining hysteretic consensus which lead to consensus being far harder to remove than to establish.

</details>


### [173] [World Editing: Intervening on Executable Worlds at Increasing Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02331)
*Max Ku,Nok-Kan Law,Yu-Chien Tang,Shih-Ying Yeh,Ping Nie,Andy Zheng,Tat Hei Lai,Fei-Yueh Chen,Nikko Yu,Wei-Chieh Sun,Suzy Huang,Chiao-Wei Hsu,Chih-Chuan Huang,Chak-Wing Mak,Ho Yin Sam Ng,Edisy Kin Wai Chan,Min-Hung Chen,Ho Kei Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将"世界编辑"形式化为对已有可执行世界的干预，并提出干预深度这一维度，通过游戏modding实例化，构建了IGMWorld与IGMBench（110个任务、1.1K+可执行标准），评估前沿编程智能体，发现其具备较强编辑能力但仍存在可靠性随深度下降、视觉一致性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型虽能生成环境并在其中行动，但如何有意识地编辑一个已有的可执行世界仍研究不足。作者希望将"世界编辑"确立为一项独立于世界生成与交互的能力，并提供可实践的测试平台。

Method: 将世界编辑形式化为在保留不应变化属性的前提下，对已有世界进行干预；提出"干预深度"作为衡量编辑耦合世界实体、动态与系统的强度轴。通过工业级游戏modding实例化该能力，提出IGMWorld，并构建IGMBench基准，包含Minecraft与Terraria上的110个任务和超过1.1K个可执行的状态与行为判定标准，覆盖属性、实体、动态与系统四类干预，采用确定性可执行性、行为、保持性与视觉检查进行评估。

Result: 最强配置在严格任务级标准下解决78.2%的任务，标准级性能达94.8%。可靠性总体随干预深度增加而下降，且该趋势在评估标准数量相近的任务中依然存在。大多数失败的编辑仍能成功构建与加载，说明主要难点在于让编辑后的世界按预期行为运行。视觉一致性是独立弱项，所有评估配置的联合视觉通过率均低于50%。

Conclusion: 世界编辑是与世界生成和交互不同的一项独立能力，可执行游戏为其研究提供了实用的试验平台。

Abstract: Interactive world models are increasingly capable of generating environments and acting within them, yet deliberately editing an existing executable world remains underexplored. We formulate world editing as intervening on an existing world while preserving properties that should remain unchanged, and introduce intervention depth as an axis describing how strongly an edit couples world entities, dynamics, and systems. We instantiate this capability through industry-grade game modding and introduce IGMWorld, together with IGMBench, a benchmark of 110 tasks and over 1.1K executable state and behavioral criteria across Minecraft and Terraria. The tasks span property, entity, dynamics, and system interventions and are evaluated through deterministic executability, behavioral, preservation, and visual checks. Frontier coding agents already exhibit substantial world-editing capability: the strongest configuration solves 78.2% of tasks under a strict task-level criterion, while criterion-level performance reaches 94.8%. Reliability generally decreases with intervention depth, and this pattern persists even among tasks with similar numbers of evaluation criteria. Most failed edits still build and load successfully, suggesting that the main difficulty is making the edited world behave as requested. Visual consistency remains a separate weakness, with all evaluated configurations below 50% joint visual pass rate. These results show that world editing is a distinct capability from world generation and interaction, and that executable games provide a practical testbed for studying it.

</details>


### [174] [A Multi Method Importance and Performance Efficiency Analysis of Topological Metrics for Natural Visibility Graph Based Cyber Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02342)
*Ali Melih Kanca,Ilker Turker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过共识排序方法从21个NVG拓扑指标中筛选精简子集，Top3配置在CICIDS2018上取得97.148%准确率，优于全量21指标的95.999%，且运行时间减少96.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于自然可见图（NVG）的网络流量分析依赖拓扑描述符刻画结构特征，但并非所有描述符对网络攻击分类贡献相同，且提取大量指标会增加计算成本，因此需要研究能否用精简指标子集在保持分类能力的同时提升计算效率。

Method: 评估21个NVG导出的拓扑指标，整合SHAP、分组置换重要性、Boruta和递归特征消除（RFE）四种重要性分析方法，通过共识排序策略确定指标优先级；据此构建Full21、Top15、Top10、Top7、Top5、Top3六种配置，在CICIDS2018数据集上使用CNN分类器和分层5折交叉验证进行评估。

Result: 排名前三的指标为avg_clustering_coeff_median、avg_clustering_coeff_std、avg_clustering_coeff_mean。Top3取得最高平均性能：准确率97.148%、加权F1 97.055%、MCC 0.9675，而Full21分别为95.999%、95.521%、0.9549；同时总运行时间从14,961.39秒降至589.22秒，减少96.06%。

Conclusion: 在所评估设定下，重要性引导的指标精简可提供紧凑的NVG表示，既获得更高的观测平均预测性能，又大幅降低计算成本。
