Abstract: Neural surrogate models for Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) on unstructured 3D geometries are often limited by poor generalization and the high cost of generating large-scale training datasets. Consequently, pre-training on massive datasets of related PDE dynamics has emerged as a critical alternative to enhance the robustness and scalability of these models. However, this strategy is neither compute- nor data-efficient, as it relies on massive pre-computed data that is very costly to generate. In this work, we introduce a disk-data-free pre-training framework tailored to both steady-state and transient regimes. For steady-state problems, we propose a geometry-driven strategy that leverages intrinsic shape descriptors to learn representations of complex 3D domains. For transient problems, we introduce a physics-driven approach based on online generation of synthetic PDE data, enabling scalable pre-training without reliance on expensive datasets. Across multiple experiments, our approach achieves faster convergence, greater data efficiency, and higher accuracy during fine-tuning, particularly under realistic low-data regimes. This methodology provides a practical pathway toward data-efficient neural emulators for large-scale simulations.

</details>


### [275] [CVE2AP: Automated Generation of PDDL-Encoded Attack Paths via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03383)
*Lin Cui,Vincenzo Scotti,Raffaela Mirandola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CVE2AP，用LLM从自然语言CVE描述自动生成PDDL编码的攻击路径，并引入错误反馈迭代精炼，最高达86.9%语法正确、78.6%可解性和93.1%语义正确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击路径建模是网络安全分析的基础，PDDL被广泛用于将攻击路径编码为可机器验证的形式化表示，以支持漏洞利用、攻击演进及影响推理。但现有方法主要依赖专家手工构建，可扩展性差，难以跟上快速演变的网络威胁。LLM具备丰富预训练知识和推理能力，有望将威胁情报解读并转化为形式化表示，因此本文探索自动化的PDDL攻击路径生成。

Method: 提出CVE2AP，一种基于LLM的方法，从自然语言CVE描述自动生成PDDL编码的攻击路径。方法采用结构化提示（structured prompting），并引入错误反馈机制，利用规划器报告的语法错误和可解性错误迭代精炼生成结果。在多个LLM和多种生成配置上进行系统性实证评估。

Result: 在语法、可解性和语义三个维度评估生成质量，并统计token消耗与生成时间。最高达到86.9%语法正确性、78.6%可解性和93.1%语义正确性（LLM-as-expert评估）；GPT-5.5在质量与成本间取得最佳平衡，错误反馈带来最一致的质量提升。

Conclusion: CVE2AP能有效从CVE描述自动生成高质量的PDDL编码攻击路径，验证了LLM结合结构化提示与错误反馈在攻击路径形式化建模中的可行性，为提升攻击路径建模的自动化与可扩展性提供了路径。

Abstract: Attack Path (AP) modeling is fundamental to cybersecurity analysis, where the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) has been widely adopted to encode APs into formal and machine-verifiable representations for automated reasoning about vulnerability exploitation, attack progression, and their potential impacts. However, existing AP modeling approaches largely rely on expert-driven manual construction, limiting their scalability and ability to keep pace with rapidly evolving cyber threats. Large language models (LLMs) are promising candidates, as their extensive pre-trained knowledge and reasoning capabilities enable them to interpret and transform threat intelligence into formal representations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CVE2AP}, an LLM-based approach for automatically generating PDDL-encoded attack paths from natural language CVE (Common Vulnerability Exposure) descriptions. CVE2AP leverages structured prompting and incorporates an error-feedback mechanism that iteratively refines the generated paths using planner-reported syntactic and solvability errors. We conduct a systematic empirical evaluation across multiple LLMs and generation configurations, assessing generation quality across syntactic, solvability and semantic dimensions, together with token consumption and generation time. The results demonstrate that CVE2AP effectively generates high-quality PDDL-encoded attack paths, achieving up to 86.9\% syntax correctness, 78.6\% solvability, and 93.1\% semantic correctness under LLM-as-expert evaluation, while \texttt{GPT-5.5} offers the best quality-cost trade-off and error feedback yields the most consistent quality improvement.

</details>


### [276] [Becoming Suspicious Across Borders: Algorithmic Extraterritoriality and AI-Driven Financial Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03425)
*Georgios Pavlidis,Savvas Chatzichristofis,Eleni Gavriil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"算法域外性"概念，指出AI驱动的金融监控使"怀疑"从人类在特定司法辖区内的情境化法律判断，转变为跨数据基础设施的数据驱动过程，从而带来问责与可争议性的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上"怀疑"被理解为人类行为者在可识别司法辖区内的情境化法律判断，是反洗钱与反恐融资中低于证明门槛的干预依据。但随着人工智能成为金融监控的组成部分，这一理解已不再充分，需要重新审视怀疑的生产机制及其监管后果。

Method: 概念分析与理论建构。作者从认识论与空间两个维度剖析AI驱动金融监控的转型，并提出"算法域外性"这一概念，将其界定为经由数据基础设施而非正式管辖权主张所中介的监管权力形式。

Result: AI驱动的金融监控通过跨国数据基础设施运作，使监管覆盖范围不再取决于行为发生地，而取决于该行为能否在数据系统中变得可见。同时，个体日益被构成为"数据化的怀疑主体"，通过分散且不透明的评估过程被治理。

Conclusion: 怀疑的算法化与数据化使问责与可争议性面临挑战，因为怀疑变得更难被定位、解释或质疑。这要求对金融监控中的监管权力与个体权利保护进行重新思考。

Abstract: Suspicion is an important, yet elusive concept in anti-money laundering and counter-terrorist financing (AML/CFT), which allows for intervention below the threshold of proof. In its traditional form, suspicion can be understood as a situated legal judgement by human actors within identifiable jurisdictions. It is argued that this understanding is no longer adequate. As artificial intelligence (AI) becomes an integral part of financial surveillance, suspicion is increasingly produced through data-driven processes. This transformation is epistemic, but also spatial. Since AI-driven financial surveillance operates through transnational data infrastructures, regulatory reach is less a matter of where conduct occurs than a question of whether such conduct becomes visible within data systems. This article develops the concept of algorithmic extraterritoriality, understood as a form of regulatory power mediated by data infrastructures rather than formal assertions of jurisdiction. Moreover, since individuals are increasingly constituted as datafied subjects of suspicion, they are rendered governable through dispersed and opaque processes of evaluation. This constitutes a challenge for accountability and contestability because suspicion becomes more difficult to locate, explain or contest.

</details>


### [277] [Jumping the Line: Exploiting Length Predictions in LLM Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03430)
*Yuyang Dai,Rana Shahout,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 JIL，一种针对基于预测的 LLM 调度器的攻击，通过优化对抗后缀使输出长度探针低估请求长度，从而获取更高调度优先级、缩短自身完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模 LLM 服务中，请求调度对降低完成时间至关重要。基于长度的策略（如最短作业优先）偏好短请求，但生成前输出长度未知，实际调度器只能依赖预测长度，这一预测信号因而成为可被操纵的攻击面。

Method: 以 TRAIL 为案例研究，JIL 优化一个对抗后缀，使轻量级输出长度探针低估请求长度；在两个数据集、四种 LLM 及多种请求画像和部署配置下评估，并测试调度器侧防御。

Result: JIL 将预测输出长度最多降低 83.4%，端到端实验中对抗请求平均完成速度快至 1.53 倍；预测长度的降幅显著大于实际输出长度变化，暴露估计与真实规模之间的错配；响应质量因模型和任务而异，存在调度优势与响应质量的权衡。将长度预测粗粒度分组可削弱 JIL 的调度优势并缓解对良性请求的延迟。

Conclusion: 基于预测长度的 LLM 调度器存在可被利用的安全与公平性缺陷，攻击者可藉由构造对抗输入获取不当优先级；粗粒度分组等调度器侧防御可在一定程度上缓解该问题。

Abstract: Efficient request scheduling is increasingly important for reducing completion time in large language model (LLM) serving. Size-based policies such as Shortest Job First prioritize shorter requests, but output lengths are unknown before generation, so practical schedulers rely on predicted lengths. We introduce JIL, an attack on prediction-based LLM schedulers that manipulates the scheduling signal to obtain higher priority and reduce completion time. Using TRAIL as a case study, JIL optimizes an adversarial suffix that causes a lightweight output-length probe to underestimate a request's length. We evaluate JIL on two datasets and four LLMs across varied request profiles and deployment configurations. JIL reduces predicted output lengths by up to 83.4 percent, and adversarial requests complete up to 1.53 times faster on average in end-to-end serving experiments. The reduction in predicted length is substantially larger than the change in actual output length, revealing a mismatch between the scheduler's estimate and the request's realized size. Response utility varies across models and tasks, exposing a trade-off between scheduling advantage and response quality. We also evaluate scheduler-side defenses and find that grouping length predictions into coarse intervals reduces JIL's scheduling advantage and mitigates delays to benign requests.

</details>


### [278] [Efficient Reasoning Training Does Not Always Harm CoT Faithfulness and Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03509)
*Samuel Lewis-Lim,Xingwei Tan,Mario Sanger,Zhixue Zhao,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究"高效推理"（减少CoT token数）对推理链忠实性和可监控性的影响，通过三种长度压力方法微调多种模型后发现：忠实性在多数情况下下降（主因是模型一致性变差），而可监控性更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理可让人类审查模型的决策过程，但推理成本高，因此需要训练模型用更少token完成任务。然而，这种高效化训练可能导致模型跳过关键推理步骤，使CoT不再忠实反映决策。由于不同高效方法与任务对长度压力的施加方式不同，这种风险是否发生、何时发生尚不清楚。

Method: 用三种不同长度压力方式微调多种模型：(1)固定生成预算；(2)按样本设定的长度目标；(3)组内相对长度奖励。评估高效推理对两方面的作用：忠实性（CoT能否反映模型在相关输入上的决策）和可监控性（CoT能否揭示输入干预何时改变了输出）。

Result: 长度压力对忠实性和可监控性的影响不同。忠实性在大多数设置下下降，主要原因是被训练模型的一致性变差；可监控性更稳健，即使CoT大幅缩短，模型仍会承认输入对其答案的影响。

Conclusion: 高效推理训练会以不同方式影响CoT的忠实性与可监控性，二者需分别评估；可监控性对推理精简具有较强鲁棒性，而忠实性的下降主要源于模型一致性降低。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows humans to inspect how large language models reach their answers, and oversee model behaviour. This reasoning comes at an increased inference cost, motivating efficient methods that train models to solve tasks using fewer tokens. However, a common concern is that such training may cause models to skip important reasoning steps, so the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the model's decision. It is unclear whether or when this occurs in practice, since different efficiency methods apply length pressure to models' CoT in distinct ways, and faithfully explaining a model's decision takes more tokens on some tasks than others. To understand these dynamics, we fine-tune a variety of models with three methods that apply length pressure differently, namely a fixed generation budget, a per-example length target, and a group-relative length reward. We evaluate how efficient reasoning affects CoT faithfulness (i.e., how well the CoT reflects model decisions on related inputs) and monitorability (i.e., whether the CoT reveals when input interventions alter the output). We find that it affects faithfulness and monitorability differently. Faithfulness falls in most settings, primarily because the trained models are less consistent. Monitorability is more robust, as models keep acknowledging the influence on their answer even when the CoT is much shorter.

</details>


### [279] [From Benchmarks to Production: A Text-to-SQL System for Complex Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03524)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Bruno Gomes Coelho,Guillaume Michel,Sophia Zhi,Valerie Faucon-Morin,Kristen Howell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLINT是一个面向金融数据库的领域专用Text-to-SQL系统，通过查找代理、模板检索和模式链接三个组件，解决了生产金融库中因列值为不透明整数键导致的性能差距，并在359个问题上超越同LLM的多种SOTA基线，已部署上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用Text-to-SQL系统在Spider、BIRD等学术基准上表现优异，但那些数据集的模式较浅、列值多为人类可读。在生产金融数据库中，概念以不透明整数键存储而非可读字符串，即使是简单查询也需多次连接，过滤条件引用不透明ID，导致通用方法性能跌破50%。

Method: FLINT包含三个核心组件：(1) 查找代理，动态将自然语言概念解析为特定问题所需的参考表约束；(2) 基于嵌入的检索，从紧凑的专家编写查询模板库中检索结构相似的模板；(3) 模式链接，通过遍历外键链将大表模式裁剪到相关子集，而非仅依赖名称相似度。

Result: 在两个共359个问题的生产金融模式数据集上评估，FLINT在使用相同LLM的情况下优于多种最先进的基线方法。系统已作为金融数据检索服务的一部分部署到生产环境。

Conclusion: 针对金融领域不透明键和深层模式的特点，通过概念解析、模板检索与外键链遍历式模式链接的组合，可有效弥补通用Text-to-SQL在生产金融数据库上的性能缺口，并具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: General-purpose Text-to-SQL systems achieve strong performance on academic benchmarks like Spider and BIRD, where schemas are relatively shallow and column values are often human readable. In production financial databases, where concepts are stored as opaque integer keys rather than human-readable strings, these methods fall below 50%, as even simple queries require multiple joins and filter predicates reference opaque IDs. We present Financial LINking Text-to-SQL (FLINT), a domain-specialized Text-to-SQL system that closes this gap through three key components: (1) a lookup agent that dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints, (2) embedding-based retrieval of structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank, and (3) schema linking that prunes a large table schema to the relevant subset by traversing foreign-key chains, rather than relying on name similarity alone. We evaluate on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas. FLINT outperforms various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM. The system is deployed in production as part of a financial data retrieval service.

</details>


### [280] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Frontier Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03548)
*Wenlong Zhang,Zhengbo Jiao,Chenxu Zhang,Lekang Jiang,SiYuan Ma,Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“任务-工具协同演化”框架，实现推理数据合成中的递归式工具自我改进，可生成越来越难的有效任务并提升下游训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务级递归只是复用生成的问题作为种子，而构造工具本身保持不变，难以随任务分布演化而持续提升难度，因此需要让合成工具与任务一同进化。

Method: 提出任务-工具协同演化框架：在线自我改进把求解器的中间失败转化为可复用技能；任务后自我改进在每批之后修订技能、提示与工作流，仅在有界成本增加内能生成更难有效任务时采纳候选；模型权重与验证标准保持固定。

Result: 在数学、编程与科学领域，十四轮演化后平均求解准确率从100.0%降至54.8%；消融显示两种更新调度结合优于固定工具递归或任一单独调度；合成数据提升下游SFT与GRPO表现，27B学生模型用1万条数学样例微调后在APEX上达到62.5% mean-16准确率，与部分前沿模型参考结果相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，让合成工具与任务协同适配，能够生成越来越具挑战性且具有下游训练价值的数据。

Abstract: Generating progressively harder reasoning problems requires synthesis procedures that adapt as the task distribution evolves. Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but leaves the construction harness unchanged. We present task-harness co-evolution, a framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. Across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds. Ablations show that combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. The resulting data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance. In particular, a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references. These results support adapting the synthesis harness alongside the tasks to generate increasingly challenging data with downstream training value.

</details>


### [281] [Knowledge or Calculator? Decomposing the Skill Premium in Verifiable Financial Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03564)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FinSkillBench 评估套件，通过 2,603 个时点任务与确定性验证器，量化金融 AI 智能体中"技能包"带来的性能提升（skill premium），并揭示该提升取决于模型、资源条件与执行框架的整体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融 AI 智能体不能仅靠事实检索，还需正确的量化执行、可靠使用程序性资源以及可审计的结构化输出。现有评估往往无法区分这些能力，也缺乏对"技能/工具/文档"等外部资源贡献的量化分析。因此需要一个具备隐藏可复现真值与任务专用验证器的评测基准，来测量并拆解技能带来的增益。

Method: 构建 FinSkillBench：包含组合构建、风险管理、基本面分析三大类共 12 个子任务、2,603 个时点 episodes，配备隐藏可再生成真值与任务专用确定性验证器。对 9 个模型、3 种资源条件共执行 17,820 个 episodes，进行配对分析；并把"精选技能包"拆分为人工撰写流程文档与可执行领域工具分别授予，考察各自增益及组合效果；再用 10 种评分变体、聚类 bootstrap 和独立实现的第二套执行框架验证方向稳健性。

Result: 精选技能包使平均分提升 +16.2 分（0.366→0.528）；单 episode 内生成的技能仅提升 +0.5 分且消耗更多 token 与轮次。拆分后：仅文档 +5.6 分，仅工具 +19.5 分，二者组合呈次可加性。增益与工作流强相关：数值密集型流程中可执行工具占主导，程序或输出模式指导成为瓶颈时文档更重要，解释性任务两者皆有收益。效应在 10 种评分变体与聚类 bootstrap 下符号稳定，第二套独立框架复现了方向性模式，但幅度取决于工具与数据的暴露方式。

Conclusion: 可测量的"技能溢价"是整个模型、资源与执行框架系统的属性，而非仅由底层模型决定。评估与部署金融智能体时，必须同时考虑模型能力、资源形态与工程框架的相互作用。

Abstract: Financial AI agents must do more than retrieve facts: investment workflows require correct quantitative execution, reliable use of procedural resources, and auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite of 2,603 point in time episodes across 12 subtasks in portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, with hidden regenerable ground truth and task specific deterministic verifiers. Executing 17,820 episodes across 9 models and 3 resource conditions, the paired analysis across 8 models shows that curated skill packages raise mean scores by +16.2 points (0.366 to 0.528), whereas skills generated within a single episode add only +0.5 points while consuming more tokens and turns. We then decompose the curated premium by granting human authored procedural documents and executable domain tools separately: documents alone add +5.6 points, tools alone add +19.5 points, and their combination is subadditive. The premium is strongly workflow dependent: executable tools dominate numerically intensive workflows, documentation matters more when procedural or output schema guidance is the bottleneck, and interpretive tasks benefit from both. The effects are sign stable across 10 scoring variants and cluster bootstrap analyses, and an independently implemented second harness reproduces the directional pattern while showing that effect magnitudes depend on how tools and data are exposed. Overall, a measured "skill premium" is a property of the full model, resource, and harness system rather than of the underlying model alone.

</details>


### [282] [Learning to Assess Heartbeat Observability for mmWave Heart-Rate Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03570)
*Yuxuan Hu,Shilin Shan,Jianfei Yang,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HEAR 双任务 Transformer，从毫米波雷达相位谱学习心跳可观测性评分并联合估计心率，仅用仿真数据训练即可零样本迁移到真实数据集，实现选择性预测并降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达非接触式心率测量需要判断单次测量是否可靠；由于相干叠加等效应会压制心跳分量，即使观测几何相似，宏观条件也无法反映可观测性，因此须直接从采集的测量数据中评估可靠性。

Method: 构建可控多散射体 FMCW 仿真器以获得不同可观测性条件下的训练监督，用主心跳频带峰值与已知心率的一致性自动生成可观测性标签；提出紧凑的双任务 Transformer HEAR，联合预测可观测性分数与心率，输入为谱幅度及相对于呼吸基频的频率以提供呼吸谐波上下文。

Result: 仅在仿真数据上训练即可零样本迁移到 60 GHz 与 120 GHz 两个公开真实数据集（134 名受试者）；学习到的分数同时支持 HEAR 心率头与多个现有估计器的选择性预测，在 120 GHz 数据集上将心率头平均绝对误差从全覆盖率 17.9 BPM 降至 50% 覆盖率下 1.6 BPM；端到端处理延迟为边缘设备上 50.8 ms。

Conclusion: 直接从测量中学习心跳可观测性可有效支撑选择性心率估计，仿真训练具备良好零样本迁移能力，且方法轻量、可在边缘设备实时运行。

Abstract: Contactless heart-rate sensing with millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires assessing whether individual measurements support reliable estimation. We study learning to assess heartbeat observability, defined as the readability of the heartbeat component in an acquired phase spectrum, for selective heart-rate estimation. Coherent superposition of scatterer returns can suppress this component even under similar macroscopic observation geometry, motivating assessment directly from acquired measurements. To obtain training supervision across different observability conditions, we develop a controllable multi-scatterer frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) simulator. Agreement between the dominant heartbeat-band peak and the known heart rate provides an automatic observability label for each simulated measurement. We propose HEAR (Heartbeat Estimation with Assessed Reliability), a compact dual-task Transformer that jointly predicts an observability score and heart rate. Its input combines spectral magnitudes with frequencies relative to the respiration fundamental, providing context for respiratory harmonics. Trained solely on simulated observations, HEAR transfers zero-shot to two public real-world datasets collected at 60 and 120 GHz from 134 subjects. The same learned score supports selective prediction with both HEAR's own heart-rate head and multiple existing estimators. On the 120 GHz dataset, score-based selection reduces the HR head's mean absolute error from 17.9 BPM at full coverage to 1.6 BPM at 50% coverage. The complete pipeline achieves an end-to-end processing latency of 50.8 ms on an edge device. Project page: https://yuxuanhu9.github.io/HEAR/.

</details>


### [283] [HyperBrowseComp: A Multilingual and Multimodal Stress Test for Web-Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03574)
*Alham Fikri Aji,Faiz Rizki Ramadhan,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Seung Hun Eddie Han,Ryandito Diandaru,Qinrong Cui,Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Badrinath Chandana,Peerawat Chomphooyod,Ahmed Attia,Jonibek Mansurov,Emilio Villa-Cueva,Canh Duong Nguyen,Imran Turganov,Minghao Wu,Peerat Limkonchotiwat,Irina Nikishina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HyperBrowseComp，一个涵盖 13 种语言、423 道人工编写并验证的多语言多模态浏览基准，旨在评估模型在开放网络上进行高难度信息检索与多步推理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览类基准在多语言、多模态以及题目难度上存在不足，许多问题可仅凭模型参数化知识回答，无法真正考察持久的网络信息检索与证据串联能力，因此需要一个更严苛的测试平台。

Method: 由母语或高水平使用者人工编写 423 道题，覆盖 13 种语言，答案需公开可验证且精炼；通过无联网模型过滤掉较易题目；采用统一的智能体协议，结合提供商原生搜索与共享外部检索工具评估多个模型，并对部分题目进行人工评测以提供参照。

Result: 构建了 HyperBrowseComp 基准，题目需定位冷门证据、追踪多步线索或检视视频、扫描文档、图像、地图等异构来源；模型评测与人工评测结果共同揭示了当前模型在跨语言、跨模态持续信息检索上的性能与投入差距。

Conclusion: HyperBrowseComp 为跨语言、跨证据模态的持久信息检索提供了具有挑战性的测试平台，其难度主要来源于在开放网络上发现并连接证据。

Abstract: We introduce HyperBrowseComp, a multilingual and multimodal browsing benchmark comprising 423 manually authored and human-validated questions across 13 languages, written by native or highly proficient speakers. Questions are designed to be extremely challenging. Each question targets a concise, publicly verifiable answer whose discovery requires locating obscure evidence, following multi-step clue chains, or inspecting heterogeneous sources such as videos, scanned documents, images, or maps. Easier questions are filtered out by evaluating them with models without internet access to reduce the likelihood that they can be answered with parametric knowledge alone. We evaluate several models using provider-native search and a shared external retrieval harness under a common agent protocol. To contextualize model performance and effort, we also conduct a human evaluation on a sample of the questions. HyperBrowseComp provides a challenging testbed for persistent information seeking across languages and evidence modalities, with difficulty arising from discovering and connecting evidence on the open web.

</details>


### [284] [HazardWeaver: Scientific Route Selection for Hazard Analysis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03591)
*Wangshu Zhu,Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出状态依赖的科学路线选择问题，构建HazardWeaver系统及配套基准，实现灾害分析中方法适配与可执行路线的自动选择与动态修正，性能优于现有智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害的理解与评估对防灾减灾和风险降低至关重要。大语言模型的进展推动了AI智能体在灾害分析中的应用，但有效自动化要求智能体判断哪些科学方法适用于特定事件，并且能在现有数据与工具条件下可执行；随着新证据和执行结果的出现，这些条件会发生变化，需要智能体重新考虑其选择。

Method: 将问题形式化为状态依赖的科学路线选择，提出HazardWeaver系统：首先用Hazard Knowledge Compiler提取以证据关联的适用性条件；再用Hazard Capability Graph表示可执行的科学能力，并检查其输入输出之间的兼容性；最后Hazard Weaver Agent利用这两种互补表示选择适用且可执行的路线，执行工作流，并随分析状态变化修正决策。

Result: 提出Hazard Weaver Benchmark，包含7个单一灾害领域和4类多灾害交互场景的141个实例，支持多条有效科学路线，评估输出正确性、路线有效性和有依据的弃答。大量实验显示HazardWeaver优于现有智能体系统，在存在多条合格科学路线的任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: HazardWeaver通过知识编译与能力图建模，实现了状态依赖的科学路线动态选择，并提供面向决策与输出的新基准，验证了其在多路线灾害分析任务上的优势；代码已公开。

Abstract: Understanding and assessing natural hazards is essential for disaster preparedness and risk reduction. Recent advances in large language models have spurred growing interest in AI agents for hazard analysis, particularly their ability to integrate scientific data, models, and tools into automated workflows. However, effective automation requires agents to determine which scientific methods are appropriate for a given event and executable with the available data and tools. As new evidence and execution results become available, these conditions can change, requiring agents to reconsider their choices. We formulate this problem as state-dependent scientific route selection and introduce HazardWeaver. Specifically, HazardWeaver first leverages the Hazard Knowledge Compiler to extract evidence-linked conditions governing scientific applicability, then its Hazard Capability Graph represents executable scientific capabilities and checks compatibility between their inputs and outputs. Using these complementary representations, the Hazard Weaver Agent component selects applicable and executable routes, carries out their workflows, and revises its decisions as the analysis state changes. To evaluate both the scientific outputs and the decisions that produce them, we introduce the Hazard Weaver Benchmark, comprising 141 instances across seven single-hazard domains and four multi-hazard interaction classes. The benchmark accommodates multiple valid scientific routes and evaluates output correctness, route validity, and justified abstention. Extensive experiments on this benchmark show that HazardWeaver outperforms existing agent systems, with the largest gains on tasks with multiple eligible scientific routes. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LabRAI/HazardWeaver.

</details>


### [285] [Depth as Time in One-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03626)
*Arnold Caleb Asiimwe,William Yang,Sanghyuk Chun,Esin Tureci,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一步生成模型将多步扩散压缩为单次前向传播，本文发现扩散的"去噪时间计算"并未消失，而是沿网络深度展开（depth as time），并可据此压缩模型参数达16.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步生成模型（蒸馏或学习流映射）已能高质量生成图像，但作者追问：当多步扩散被压缩为单次前向传播时，其去噪轨迹发生了什么？这一问题关乎理解一步生成的内在机理。

Method: 通过用模型自身的输出头解码中间层，探测单次前向传播中各层的去噪行为；对比不同生成范式（MeanFlow、drifting模型）在不同传输区间下的逐层计算表现；并将逐层计算显式视为一个流，训练单个时间条件块以跨层去噪。

Result: 发现depth as time现象：去噪计算沿网络深度展开；该现象依赖流映射所训练的传输任务。MeanFlow在更短传输区间下同时表现出去噪与再加噪；drifting模型无相同逐层去噪现象。具备该现象的模型更易压缩，MeanFlow SiT-L/2可压缩16.6倍参数为单个时间条件块。

Conclusion: 扩散的时间计算并未被一步生成消除，而是被重组到网络深度上。这一视角不仅解释了denoise-then-renoise行为，也为模型压缩提供了新途径。

Abstract: The recent wave of one-step generative models, which compress the multi-step trajectory of diffusion via either distillation or learned flow maps, has reached an inflection point where they can generate high-quality images. Here, we ask a natural question that follows from these advances: what happens to the denoising trajectory of multi-step diffusion when generation is compressed into a single forward pass? We offer an empirical observation we call \textit{depth as time}: the denoising computation that multi-step diffusion performs across sampling steps appears to unfold across the depth of a single forward pass, and can be recovered by decoding intermediate layers with the model's own output head. Most interestingly, we show that this depthwise computation depends on the transport task a flow map is trained to solve. The most surprising case is MeanFlow, where probing shorter transport intervals reveals both denoising and renoising within a single network evaluation. In contrast, generators trained without a time-indexed transport task, such as drifting models, do not exhibit the same depthwise denoising. Consequently, we show that models that exhibit the depthwise denoising phenomenon are more compressible across the layerwise computation: a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model can be compressed by $16.6\times$ in parameters into a single time-conditioned block. We offer an explanation for this denoise-then-renoise behavior and show that, when we treat the layerwise computation explicitly as a flow, a single time-conditioned block can be trained to denoise across layers, compressing a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model by $16.6\times$ in parameters. Together, these results suggest that the temporal computation of diffusion is not eliminated by one-step generation, but reorganized across network depth.

</details>


### [286] [NeutronGym: Physics-Graded Neutron Instrument Design for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03631)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NeutronGym，首个面向中子仪器设计的可执行评测环境：智能体通过工具构建仪器，McStas进行光线追踪，分层的梯级评分对语法、运行、结构和科学四个层面打分，全程无LLM评判。七个模型在16项任务中最多复现7项；在其奖励上做强化学习可将Qwen3-8B在留出实例上从11%提升到77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证语言模型智能体是否真正具备"做物理"的能力而非仅仅"回忆物理"，关键前提是评分标准必须客观、无可争辩，因此需要一个可执行、可验证、无LLM主观评判的评测环境。

Method: 构建NeutronGym环境：智能体通过验证工具设计仪器，McStas生成光线追踪模拟；采用分层级评分体系（语法、运行、结构、科学）并提供部分学分；用程序化任务族生成无限实例并设置留出参数区间；另建McStasBench，含16个来自已发表仪器的任务，配有记忆探针和沙箱；再以该奖励做强化学习训练。

Result: 七个模型最多复现16项中的7项，无一检索到参考实现，也无一达标；RL训练使Qwen3-8B从11%提升至77%（第二随机种子69%），超过未训练的Qwen3-32B，并在另外三个受限任务族上各自验证；去掉梯级部分学分则性能下降60分；仅凭奖励，训练模型在相同模拟预算下达到经典优化器水平（77%对81%，在此规模下无显著差异），而前沿模型仍为98-99%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可训练且评分客观的中子仪器设计评测环境，表明精细的部分学分奖励是性能提升的关键；同时通过失败四种可被无模型基线解出的任务设计，强调了评测可信度需以严格的任务设计为前提，并公开了相应的探测工具。

Abstract: Designing a scientific instrument tests whether language-model agents can do physics rather than recall it, provided the grading cannot be argued with. We introduce NeutronGym, to our knowledge the first executable environment for neutron instrument design: agents build instruments through validating tools, McStas ray-traces what they build, and a level-resolved ladder grades syntax, runtime, structure and science with no LLM judge. Procedural families supply unlimited instances of a fixed layout whose design parameters the agent must set, with held-out parameter regimes; a curated slice, McStasBench, adds 16 tasks from published instruments behind memorization probes and a sandbox. Seven models reproduce at most 7 of the 16, none retrieves a reference, and none meets an improvement target. The environment also trains. Reinforcement learning on its reward takes Qwen3-8B from 11% to 77% of held-out instances of a family whose targets come from a hidden design (69% at a second seed), past an untrained Qwen3-32B, and the recipe holds, at one seed each, on three further gated families. The analysis says what that gain is. Without the ladder's partial credit it collapses by 60 points. From reward alone the trained model reaches what a classical optimizer reaches, at the agent's simulation budget, only when handed the closed-form physics (77% against 81%, a gap that does not separate at this size), while frontier models still solve 98-99%. Getting a trustworthy result meant failing four task designs that no-model baselines could solve, and we release the probes that found them.

</details>


### [287] [Credit Where It Matters: Dependency-Aware Policy Optimization for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03634)
*Yu Li,Guangfeng Cai,Long-Fei Li,Shuo Han,Shengtian Yang,Han Luo,Kaibing Yang,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出依赖感知组策略优化（DepGPO），利用命令间的执行依赖关系指导终端智能体的信用分配，提升复杂终端任务的表现与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体在编码、调试等多步任务中，后续命令常依赖前序命令的信息或中间结果。现有轨迹级和步级信用分配方法未显式追踪命令间影响最终结果的读写依赖，导致训练信号可能被分配到无关操作，削弱了对相关步骤的学习。

Method: DepGPO 依据命令间的执行依赖指导信用分配：从执行轨迹构建命令依赖图，并从任务验证器检查的资源反向追溯；沿这些路径为相关写入及其支撑读取分配信用，并据此在步骤间重新分配轨迹优势值。

Result: 大量对比实验与消融研究表明，DepGPO 在复杂终端任务上提升了任务性能与训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并追溯命令读写依赖，DepGPO 实现了更精准的信用分配，从而改善了终端智能体的学习效果。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents benefit from reinforcement learning (RL) in coding, debugging, and other multi-step terminal tasks. In these tasks, later commands often depend on information or intermediate results produced by earlier commands. However, existing trajectory-level and step-level credit assignment methods do not explicitly trace the read-write dependencies through which commands affect the final outcome. Consequently, training signals could still be assigned to irrelevant operations, weakening learning from relevant steps. In this paper, we propose Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization (DepGPO), which uses execution dependencies between commands to guide credit assignment for terminal agents. Specifically, we construct a command dependency graph from execution traces and trace backward from the resources inspected by the task verifier. We then assign credit to relevant writes and their supporting reads along these paths, and use it to redistribute trajectory advantages across steps. Extensive comparative experiments and ablation studies demonstrate that DepGPO improves task performance and training stability on complex terminal tasks.

</details>


### [288] [Do Large Language Models Know Colombian Law? A Reliability Benchmark for the Colombian Legal System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03639)
*Rubén Manrique,Michelle Castellanos,Jorge Morales,Juan David Gutiérrez,Antonio Barreto Rozo,Joaquín Vélez Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个经专家验证的哥伦比亚法律体系LLM可靠性基准（1,042项，涵盖10个法律领域和3种题型），评估15个模型后发现：闭式选择题最高准确率0.905，但自由文本法律回答的事实正确率均不超过0.45，且答案相关性越高反而越容易错误，说明当前LLM在哥伦比亚法律任务中仍需专家监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在法律实践、教育与研究中应用日益广泛，但其在美国以外国家法律体系中的可靠性几乎缺乏系统记录，尤其缺少针对特定国家（如哥伦比亚）法律体系的专家验证评估工具。

Method: 通过人在回路（human-in-the-loop）流程与多阶段专家评审，构建包含1,042个条目的基准，覆盖10个法律领域与3种题型（闭式多选、半开放、开放式IRAC）；对15个当代专有与开放权重模型采用与题型匹配的指标进行评估，并结合独立基于评分量表的LLM裁判与盲评人类专家进行验证。

Result: 闭式题准确率跨度大（Gemini 3.1 Pro 0.905 至 0.577），而自由文本法律答案的事实正确率任何模型均不超过0.45；答案相关性与正确性出现分离（Spearman rho = -0.46），模型常显得对答如流却实际出错；闭式题准确率与自由文本正确率排名高度相关（rho = 0.94），选择题筛查可预测模型排名但会高估绝对可靠性；LLM裁判与盲评人类专家评分均重现自由文本排名（rho >= 0.88）；模型引用的法条仅约一半正确，其余为错误或不存在；可靠性按法律领域系统性变化，并随问题复杂度呈倒U型。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在哥伦比亚法律任务中需要专家监督，将答案锚定于权威来源是提升可靠性的有前景路径；作者公开基准构建流程以支持可复现评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support legal practice, education, and research, yet their reliability in national legal systems outside the United States remains largely undocumented. We introduce an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLM reliability on the Colombian legal system. The benchmark comprises 1,042 items spanning ten areas of law and three question formats (closed multiple-choice, semi-open, and open-ended IRAC), built through a human-in-the-loop pipeline with multi-stage expert review. We evaluate 15 contemporary proprietary and open-weight models with format-appropriate metrics. Accuracy on closed questions ranges widely, from 0.905 (Gemini 3.1 Pro) to 0.577, but on free-text legal answers factual correctness never exceeds 0.45 (on a 0-1 scale) for any model. We find a dissociation between answer relevancy and correctness (Spearman rho = -0.46): models reliably sound responsive while frequently being wrong, a pattern of particular concern for non-expert users. Closed-question accuracy and free-text correctness are strongly rank-correlated (rho = 0.94), so cheap multiple-choice screening predicts model ranking but overstates absolute reliability. An independent rubric-based LLM judge and blind human expert scoring both reproduce the free-text ranking (rho >= 0.88). The judge further reveals that only about half of the norms models cite are correct; the rest are wrong or non-existent. Reliability varies systematically by legal area and follows an inverted-U across question complexity. Our results indicate that current LLMs require expert supervision for Colombian legal tasks, and that grounding answers in authoritative sources is a promising path to higher reliability. We release the benchmark construction pipeline to support reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [289] [MRVQ: One Resident Index for Dimension- and Rate-Elastic Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03651)
*Sean Culatana,Shang-En Huang,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MRVQ残差量化器，用单一常驻产物同时支持多维度、多码率的稠密检索，内存占用显著低于多种基线，但质量略逊于逐码率单独训练的QINCo2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索服务需随延迟、质量与内存预算变化，在嵌入前缀维度与索引比特率之间切换。为每个速率单独调优量化器质量最佳，但检索层需同时保存多份码流与量化器状态，内存开销大。因此需要一种低内存、可弹性调节的方案。

Method: 提出Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization（MRVQ），一种针对冻结嵌入的后处理残差量化器。其最高速率码可通过两种方式截断：丢弃残差级以降低速率，丢弃嵌入坐标以降低维度。单一常驻产物即可覆盖所有评估的（维度, 速率）组合。

Result: 在FiQA与NFCorpus、四种嵌入家族、{4,8,16}字节码上：MRVQ内存比三个单独训练的QINCo2索引少17.8-22.0倍，比精简共享模型基线少1.89-2.02倍；但逐速率QINCo2在FiQA上nDCG@10高0.026-0.107。MRVQ在同码长下优于PQ、OPQ、AdANNS-OPQ。另评估低构建成本的PCA-scalar设计，质量接近RaBitQ及其扩展，中位拟合速度快420倍。报告两个负结果：QINCo2在高码率训练时崩溃，排序界假说未达预设接受标准。

Conclusion: MRVQ是弹性检索的低内存工作点，而非普适的质量最优方案。

Abstract: Dense-retrieval services must switch among embedding-prefix dimensions and index bit rates as latency, quality, and memory budgets change. Tuning a quantizer separately for each rate gives the best quality, but the retrieval tier then holds several code streams and quantizer states at once. We introduce Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization (MRVQ), a post-hoc residual quantizer for frozen embeddings. Its maximum-rate code can be truncated two ways: dropping residual stages lowers the rate, and dropping embedding coordinates lowers the dimension. One resident artifact therefore serves every (dimension, rate) pair we evaluate. Across FiQA and NFCorpus, four embedding families, and {4, 8, 16}-byte codes, MRVQ is the lowest-RAM design we evaluate. It uses 17.8-22.0x less memory than three separately trained QINCo2 indices, and 1.89-2.02x less than a lean shared-model steelman. The saving is not free: per-rate QINCo2 is 0.026-0.107 nDCG@10 better on FiQA. But MRVQ beats PQ, OPQ, and AdANNS-OPQ at matched code size. We also evaluate a low-build-cost PCA-scalar design that attains quality comparable to RaBitQ and its extension while fitting 420x faster at the median. Finally, we report two negative results: QINCo2 collapses when trained at high rates, and a ranking-bound hypothesis misses its pre-specified acceptance criteria. MRVQ is therefore a low-memory operating point for elastic retrieval, not a universal quality winner.

</details>


### [290] [Transcriptome-informed multi-modal AI for predicting neoadjuvant therapy response from breast cancer biopsies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03693)
*Jungkyu Park,Dhruva Biswas,Joseph Cappadona,Cerise Tang,Ken G. Zeng,Bartosz Machura,Chuwen Liu,Paolo Tarantino,Coral Omene,Francisco J. Esteva,Rohit Bhargava,Marcin Braun,Kamila Paździerz,Jakub Czerwiński,Hanna Romańska-Knight,Albert Grinshpun,Bareket Daniel,Michele Buchinger,Frederick Howard,Piotr Wysocki,Brie Chun,Freya Schnabel,Rich Caruana,Jan Witowski,Krzysztof J. Geras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个两阶段AI模型，先从病理图像推断转录组，再结合临床变量预测乳腺癌新辅助治疗的病理完全缓解（pCR），在多队列中实现0.79的合并AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学中深度学习生物标志物的发展受限于标注数据稀缺，直接训练端到端模型难以获得稳健的预测性能。

Method: 采用两阶段建模：第一阶段利用32种癌症类型、8,742例患者的组织病理图像学习转录组表达（经病理学家复核及与实测表达的空间一致性验证）；第二阶段将推断的表达与临床变量结合，预测pCR。开发使用1,080例（5个队列），评估使用1,412例（9个队列）。

Result: 模型合并AUROC为0.79（95% CI 0.73-0.85），可在分子亚型内区分应答者，优于组织病理学生物标志物，且在瘤内采样差异和极少量活检组织下保持稳定。消融实验表明，全转录组推断优于仅用临床变量或单阶段病理模型，并因规避基因组检测的基因选择限制而更具稳健性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，生物学信息驱动的压缩式表征有望推广到精准肿瘤学中数据稀缺的应用场景。

Abstract: Scarcity of labeled data limits development of deep learning biomarkers in oncology. We develop a two-stage AI model predicting pathological complete response (pCR) to neoadjuvant therapy in breast cancer. The first stage learns the transcriptome from histopathology using 8,742 patients across 32 cancer types, corroborated by pathologist review and spatial agreement with measured expression. This simplifies the second stage to predicting pCR from inferred expression and clinical variables. Developed using 1,080 patients (five cohorts) and evaluated in 1,412 patients (nine cohorts), the model achieves a pooled AUROC of 0.79 (95% CI, 0.73-0.85), discriminating responders within molecular subtypes. It outperforms histopathological biomarkers, remaining stable across intratumoral sampling and with minimal biopsy tissue. Ablations show transcriptome-wide inference improves discrimination over clinical variables alone or one-stage pathology models, and robustness by avoiding genomic assays' gene selection constraints. These results indicate that biologically informed compression may generalize to data-sparse applications in precision oncology.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [SimuVerity: Benchmarking Agents for Engineering-Grade Simulink Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02304)
*Ruiqi Zhang,Jiahao Wang,Mingxuan Li,Haichen Luo,Chaoting Wang,Guoyu Mou,Keyu Lai,Hanchao Lv,Jiaxu Wang,Yibo Zheng,Aijun Yang,Xiaohua Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 SimuVerity——一个包含 101 个文本到可执行 Simulink 模型生成任务、覆盖十个工程领域的基准，并采用分层评估器从工程需求满足角度评估生成模型，揭示了结构相似性无法代表工程性能的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Simulink 基准主要以生成模型能否编译、执行或与参考模型的相似度来评价，这些标准无法判断模型是否真正满足其工程需求。因此需要一个能够系统评估工程能力的基准。

Method: 构建 SimuVerity 基准，包含 101 个文本到可执行 Simulink 模型生成任务，覆盖十个工程领域。每个任务以可执行系统配置（executable-system profiles）界定工程规范与四类原生仿真场景。评估采用分层评估器：先检查产物交付、原生可执行性与工程合格性，再对合格模型从六个维度打分，包括准确性、输出质量、机理保真度、控制与因果完整性、工作域鲁棒性和动态响应。并对六个智能体系统进行评测。

Result: 最佳系统总体得分仅为 42.86。结果表明结构相似性是工程性能的糟糕代理指标：能力瓶颈既出现在生成合格实现阶段，也出现在合格后满足多维需求阶段；同时部分高分模型仍存在严重的视觉布局混乱问题。

Conclusion: SimuVerity 为评估智能体的工程能力及诊断可执行 Simulink 模型生成中的失败提供了系统化依据。

Abstract: Existing Simulink benchmarks mainly evaluate whether generated models compile, execute, or resemble a reference model. These criteria do not establish whether a model satisfies its engineering requirements. We introduce SimuVerity, a benchmark of 101 text-to-executable Simulink model-generation tasks across ten engineering domains. For each task, executable-system profiles ground the engineering specification and four families of native simulation scenarios. A hierarchical evaluator first checks artifact delivery, native executability, and engineering qualification, then scores qualified models across six dimensions covering accuracy, output quality, mechanistic fidelity, control and causal integrity, operating-domain robustness, and dynamic response. We evaluate six agent systems with SimuVerity. The best system achieves an overall score of only 42.86. The results show that structural similarity is a poor proxy for engineering performance: capability bottlenecks arise both in producing qualified implementations and in satisfying multidimensional requirements after qualification. Meanwhile, some high-scoring models still exhibit severe visual-layout disorder. SimuVerity provides a systematic basis for assessing agents' engineering capabilities and diagnosing failures in executable Simulink model generation.

</details>


### [292] [Compound AI System Reliability: A Failure Taxonomy and Resilience Pattern Catalog from 150 Production Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02503)
*Rudrendu Kumar Paul,Sourav Nandy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析150份复合AI系统生产事故报告，构建了包含23种故障模式（分属检索、生成、工具、编排、集成五类）的分类体系，并提出配套韧性模式。实验表明：熔断器减少89%级联传播、质量门控捕获73%静默退化、组件隔离减少64%影响范围，采用三种以上韧性模式可将MTTR降低71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI系统的可靠安全部署需要理解在组件边界处涌现的故障模式，而非单个模型内部的问题。级联错误跨组件边界传播、静默质量退化逃逸标准监控、协调失败导致个体正确但整体错误，这些都对现有监控与运维构成挑战。

Method: 分析150份来自开源复合AI项目与匿名企业部署的生产事故报告，构建23种故障模式的分类体系（五大类别）；针对每类提出韧性模式，并通过受控故障注入实验测量其有效性。

Result: 熔断器将级联传播减少89%；输出质量门控在用户受影响前捕获73%的静默退化；组件隔离将影响范围减少64%；实施三种及以上韧性模式的系统相比无结构化监控基线，MTTR降低71%。

Conclusion: 论文发布事故分类体系与模式目录作为实践者资源，为复合AI系统的组件边界级故障防控提供了系统化的分类与可量化验证的韧性方案。

Abstract: Deploying compound AI systems reliably and safely requires understanding failure modes that emerge at component boundaries, not within individual models. Cascading errors propagate across component boundaries, silent quality degradation evades standard monitoring, and coordination failures yield incorrect collective behavior from individually correct parts. We analyze 150 production incident reports from open-source compound AI projects and anonymized enterprise deployments to construct a taxonomy of 23 failure modes organized into five categories: retrieval failures, generation failures, tool failures, orchestration failures, and integration failures. For each category, we propose resilience patterns with measured effectiveness from controlled fault injection experiments. Circuit breakers reduce cascade propagation by 89%, output quality gates catch 73% of silent degradation before user impact, and component isolation reduces blast radius by 64%. Systems implementing three or more resilience patterns from our catalog reduce mean-time-to-recovery (MTTR) by 71% compared to unstructured monitoring baselines. We release the incident taxonomy and pattern catalog as a practitioner resource.

</details>


### [293] [An Extensive Empirical Study on Evaluation Metrics for Combinatorial Interaction Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02560)
*Lisha Qin,Chenhui Cui,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Shikai Guo,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述组合测试套件的黑盒评估指标，并通过8个项目、32个场景、295,624个套件的实证研究，发现VCC是有效的故障检测预测指标，并给出指标选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合交互测试（CIT）中，评估指标对评估与比较测试套件至关重要，但可用指标繁多，实践者难以选择合适指标，且此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 首先对组合测试套件的黑盒评估指标进行全面综述，提供严格定义、清晰分类、示例与复杂度分析；随后开展涵盖8个开源项目、32个测试场景、295,624个组合测试套件的实证研究，使用两种相关性度量考察各静态评估指标与故障检测有效性之间的相关性。

Result: VCC（取值组合覆盖）指标可作为测试套件评估的有效预测指标；基于分布的指标通常比基于交互覆盖的指标计算成本更低；合适指标的选择还需考虑测试套件自身特性，这些特性会显著影响评估有效性。

Conclusion: 提供了帮助CIT实践者选择合适评估指标以评估或比较组合测试套件的实用指南。

Abstract: Combinatorial interaction testing (CIT) is a black-box testing method that has received extensive attention in both research and practice over recent years. Its primary objective is to construct an effective combinatorial test suite that detects software failures caused by parameter interactions. As a fundamental component of the CIT testing process, the evaluation metric plays a critical role in assessing and comparing combinatorial test suites, as well as in evaluating various test generation techniques. For CIT practitioners, selecting an appropriate evaluation metric is both important and challenging, given the wide variety of available options. Nevertheless, no prior work has systematically addressed this problem. To fill this gap, this paper first provides a comprehensive survey of black-box evaluation metrics for combinatorial test suites, offering rigorous definitions, clear classifications, illustrative examples, and complexity analyses. We then conduct an extensive empirical study involving eight open-source projects, encompassing 32 test scenarios and 295,624 combinatorial test suites. In this study, we examine the correlation between each static evaluation metric and fault-detection effectiveness using two correlation measures. Experimental results show that the Value Combination Coverage (VCC) metric serves as a valid predictor for test-suite evaluation. However, distribution-based metrics generally incur lower computational costs than interaction coverage-based ones. The choice of an appropriate metric should also account for the test suite's inherent properties, as these characteristics can substantially influence the effectiveness of the evaluation. Finally, we provide practical guidelines to assist CIT practitioners in selecting suitable evaluation metrics for assessing or comparing combinatorial test suites.

</details>


### [294] [Improving the Energy-Efficiency of the Code Generated by LLMs through Effective Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02571)
*Ritika Rekhi,Bing Zhang,Md Arman Islam,Jaya Krishna Pasham,Yeswanth Chitturi,Akshay Paramesha,Isha Valiveti,Asif Imran,Bekir Turkkan,Tevfik Kosar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了21种面向节能代码生成的提示策略，筛选出8种策略在10个大模型上测试Python和C++代码生成，发现提示策略可显著降低生成代码能耗，但效果因模型和编程语言而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助编程日益普及，AI生成软件的环境影响成为重要议题。然而，尽管代码生成技术取得巨大进展，前沿大模型很少从生成代码的能效角度被评估，缺乏对提示策略如何影响能耗的系统研究。

Method: 对21种针对节能代码生成的提示策略进行综合评估，从中选出8种策略，在10个广泛使用的开源权重与专有LLM上进行测试，覆盖Python和C++代码生成，并与基线提示进行对比。

Result: 所选提示策略在评估模型上最高实现Python能耗降低25%、C++降低17%。模型层面，Python能耗降低最高达50%（Granite-4.0-H-Small）、39%（Claude 4.5 Haiku）、28%（MiniMax M3）；C++最高达56%（Granite-4.0-H-Small）和7%（Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct）。

Conclusion: 提示策略能显著影响LLM生成代码的能耗，但有效性因模型和编程语言而异。研究强调应将能效纳入LLM代码生成的评估与优化，并为设计更可持续的AI辅助编程提示提供了实用见解。

Abstract: As AI-assisted programming becomes increasingly mainstream, the environmental impact of AI-generated software has emerged as an important consideration. This motivates evaluating LLM-generated code beyond functional correctness by considering execution efficiency and energy consumption. However, despite substantial advances in code generation, frontier LLMs are rarely evaluated based on the energy efficiency of the code they produce. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 21 prompting strategies for energy-efficient code generation and identify 8 strategies for evaluation across 10 widely used open-weight and proprietary LLMs. We evaluate their effectiveness for both Python and C++ code generation relative to a baseline prompt. Across the evaluated models, the selected prompting strategies achieved energy reductions of up to 25% for Python and 17% for C++ code generation. At the model level, Python energy reductions reached up to 50% for Granite-4.0-H-Small, 39% for Claude 4.5 Haiku, and 28% for MiniMax M3, while C++ reductions reached up to 56% for Granite-4.0-H-Small and 7% for Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct. These results demonstrate that prompting strategies can substantially influence the energy consumption of LLM-generated code, although their effectiveness varies across models and programming languages. Our findings highlight the importance of incorporating energy efficiency into the evaluation and optimization of LLM-based code generation and provide practical insights into designing prompts for more sustainable AI-assisted programming.

</details>


### [295] [WebUIProof: Benchmarking WebUI Code Generators with UI-Agent Execution Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02617)
*Yun-Yun Tsai,Yuning Mao,Shiqi Wang,Junfeng Yang,Sinong Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 WebUIProof，一个面向执行的 WebUI 生成基准，通过结构化规格与可执行交互测试评估 WebUI 与 3D 交互模拟代码生成，并证明其 UI-agent 测试框架可提供结果级训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WebUI 生成基准多依赖自由形式提示和静态检查（如构建成功、截图），无法捕捉用户交互下的功能正确性：输出可能编译通过、外观合理，却在交互中失败。因此需要一个面向执行的评估方式。

Method: 提出 WebUIProof 基准，覆盖两类任务：通用 WebUI（仪表盘、游戏、交互工具）与 3D 交互模拟（粒子/星系系统、物理动力学）。提供结构化规格和密集的可执行交互测试。设计 UI-agent harness，在无头浏览器中以“计划—行动—观察”迭代循环运行测试：定位 DOM 元素、执行动作、观察 UI/DOM 变化并校验断言。评测八种商业 LLM，并利用该框架的可执行交互测试奖励进行 RL 训练。

Result: 评测八种商业 LLM 发现：即使页面成功渲染，仍频繁在交互类需求上失败，3D 模拟界面尤为明显。用可执行交互测试派生奖励对紧凑模型（如 Qwen2.5 14B、MIMO 7B）进行 RL 训练，可提升功能完成度并减少构建失败。

Conclusion: 面向执行的评估与交互测试能揭示静态检查遗漏的功能缺陷；该 UI-agent 框架不仅能评估，还能作为结果级训练信号，通过 RL 有效提升紧凑模型的 WebUI 生成能力。

Abstract: Evaluating WebUI code generation at scale is difficult: outputs may compile and look plausible yet fail under user interaction, and prior benchmarks largely rely on free-form prompts with static checks (build success, screenshots) that miss functional correctness. We introduce WebUIProof, an execution-oriented benchmark that provides structured specifications and dense, executable interaction tests for WebUI generation across two task families: general WebUIs (e.g., dashboards, game, interactive tools) and 3D interactive simulation (e.g., particle/galaxy systems, physics dynamics). WebUIProof includes a UI-agent harness that runs executable interaction tests in a headless browser using an iterative plan--act--observe loop: it locates DOM elements, performs actions, observes resulting UI/DOM changes, and checks the specified assertions. We evaluate across eight commercial LLMs and observe frequent failures on interaction-based requirements even when pages render successfully, especially on 3D simulation interfaces. Finally, we show the UI-agent harness can provide outcome-level training signals. Training compact models (e.g., Qwen2.5 14B and MIMO 7B) with RL rewards derived from executable interaction tests improves functional completion while reducing build failures.

</details>


### [296] [Self-Supervised Scaling of Terminal Environments for Scientific Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02710)
*Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Zongxia Li,Junyao Yang,Ruhan Wang,Yu Wang,Jingyuan Huang,Jichao Yu,Ninghao Liu,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出软件在环重建（SWR）自监督框架，利用现有软件工作流生成参考输出和验证目标，无需为每个任务手工编写参考解与验证器，实现可扩展的行为验证监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体正拓展到科学等专业领域，但构建训练环境需要可执行的参考行为和领域专用验证器，用以区分语义正确与表面合理的产物；为每个任务编写这些组件需重复工程，且难以复用。

Method: SWR 从现有软件工作流（将结构化输入映射到输出的可执行程序）中获取参考输出与验证目标。对每个工作流执行多种输入配置，划分为公开观测与隐藏评估。给定指令、输入模式和公开输入-输出观测，智能体在无法访问源工作流的情况下构建可编辑程序，在隐藏配置上与工作流输出比对。分层验证器融合领域语义比较、结构有效性与防捷径检查，公开反馈支持迭代修订。该构造可扩展更多工作流与配置而无需为每任务编写参考解。

Result: 以 500 个工作流、46 个软件族、跨六个领域实例化 SWR。每任务三次尝试下，Qwen3.8-Max 解决 838 个任务并产生 1,422 条经验证轨迹，过采样为 3,000 条仅重建训练样本。对 Qwen3.8-27B 监督微调使 Terminal-Bench 2 平均性能从 47.94% 提升至 53.56%（三个随机种子），并在四项报告评估上取得四个等 token 语料对照中的最高均值。

Conclusion: 现有科学软件可为终端智能体提供可扩展且经行为验证的监督信号。

Abstract: Terminal agents are increasingly deployed beyond software engineering in science and other specialized domains. Constructing training environments requires executable reference behavior and a domain-specific verifier that distinguishes semantic correctness from superficially plausible artifacts. Authoring these components for each task requires repeated engineering and limits reuse. We introduce software-in-the-loop reconstruction, a self-supervised framework that obtains reference outputs and verification targets from existing software workflows, executable programs mapping structured inputs to outputs. For each workflow, we execute multiple input configurations and partition cases into public observations and hidden evaluations. Given the instruction, input schema, and public input--output observations, an agent constructs an editable program without access to the source workflow. The candidate is evaluated on hidden configurations against workflow outputs. A hierarchical verifier combines domain-specific semantic comparison, structural validity, and anti-shortcut checks, while public feedback supports iterative revision. The construction admits additional workflows and configurations without authoring a reference solution for each task. We instantiate SWR with 500 workflows and 46 software families across six domains. Across three attempts per task, Qwen3.8-Max solves 838 tasks and produces 1,422 verified trajectories, which we oversample to 3,000 reconstruction-only training examples. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.8-27B improves mean Terminal-Bench 2 performance from 47.94% to 53.56% across three seeds and achieves the highest mean among four matched-token corpus controls on all four reported evaluations. These results indicate that existing scientific software can provide scalable, behaviorally verified supervision for terminal agents.

</details>


### [297] [VeriPy Source-Preserving Verification and Compatibility Checking for Python Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02814)
*Naing Oo Lwin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VeriPy 提出一个统一工作流，用于让带注解的 Python 组件与形式化保证保持对齐，涵盖源码级证明开发、功能验证与向后兼容性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python 程序与其形式化保证难以持续对齐：规范须描述实际运行的实现，且更新必须保持既有调用方依赖的行为，这构成持续的维护难题。

Method: 将契约、不变式与证明钩子以 Python 注释形式表达；通过源码定位诊断来细化已检查的辅助引理；直接编码器生成 Dafny 或 Lean 产物，同时保留可执行体与依赖的显式模型；用关系积检查更新后的组件是否仍接受旧输入并保持返回值与建模异常。

Result: 形成一个连接源码级证明开发、功能验证与向后兼容性的工作流，并记录每项保证所依赖的假设。

Conclusion: VeriPy 为 Python 组件提供了统一的验证与兼容性维护流程，代码已在 https://github.com/astrio-labs/veripy 公开。

Abstract: Keeping a Python program and its formal guarantees aligned is a continuing maintenance problem. Specifications must describe the implementation that actually runs, and updates must preserve the behavior on which existing callers depend. VeriPy brings these obligations into a common workflow for annotated Python components. Developers and agents express contracts, invariants, and proof hooks as Python comments, then refine checked auxiliary lemmas using source-located diagnostics. Direct encoders produce Dafny or Lean artifacts while retaining admitted executable bodies and explicit models of their dependencies. A relational product checks whether an updated component still admits old inputs and preserves returned values and modeled exceptions. The resulting workflow connects source-level proof development with functional verification and backward compatibility, recording the assumptions under which each guarantee applies. The code is available at https://github.com/astrio-labs/veripy.

</details>


### [298] [Engineering Sustainable Agents: A Systematic Comparison of Agentic LLMs for Developer Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03010)
*Merve Astekin,Yan Naing Tun,Arda Goknil,Erik Johannes Husom,Lwin Khin Shar,Hasan Sözer,Ratnadira Widyasari,Hui Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对软件工程中的智能体式LLM系统进行大规模实证研究，发现多智能体设计平均能耗为非智能体基线的6.36倍、耗时6.07倍，而精度提升有限，轻量非智能体/单智能体配置在帕累托前沿占绝对主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程中日益普及，多智能体编排系统虽被广泛采用，却带来更高的计算成本与环境代价。然而，学界尚缺乏对"智能体复杂度—能效—精度"三者权衡的系统性量化证据，因此需要跨任务、跨模型、跨硬件的全面实证研究。

Method: 在五类软件工程任务（代码生成、技术债识别、代码漏洞检测、日志解析、日志分析）上开展实证研究。配置从非智能体单次查询基线到多智能体工作流，覆盖6个开放权重LLM、2种提示策略、3种硬件平台。从准确率、推理延迟、能耗三个维度评估每种配置。

Result: 多智能体设计平均能耗为非智能体基线的6.36倍、运行时间为6.07倍，单个"任务—硬件"组合最坏情况下延迟高达160倍。精度收益有限且依赖任务：多智能体仅提升漏洞检测的平均准确率；轻量非智能体与单智能体配置仍主导帕累托前沿，66个帕累托最优配置中占59个。模型与提示策略的作用是任务特定的调节杠杆，其有效方向因任务而异，而非全局默认最优。

Conclusion: 智能体复杂度与能效之间存在显著权衡，更多智能体并不必然带来更好效果。研究据此提出面向可持续、任务感知的LLM开发工具的设计指南，建议优先选择轻量配置并按任务特性调整模型与提示策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering, including agentic systems that coordinate multiple agents, but impose higher computational and environmental costs. In this paper, we present a comprehensive empirical study of agentic LLM systems across five software engineering tasks: code generation, technical debt identification, code vulnerability detection, log parsing, and log analysis. For each task, we compare LLM configurations that range from a non-agentic single-query baseline to multi-agent workflows, using six open-weight LLMs, two prompt strategies, and three hardware platforms. We assess each configuration in terms of accuracy, inference latency, and energy consumption. Our results reveal substantial trade-offs between agentic complexity and energy efficiency: multi-agent designs consume on average 6.36$\times$ as much energy and run 6.07$\times$ as long as the non-agentic baseline, with worst-case slowdowns of up to 160$\times$ for individual task--hardware pairs. Accuracy gains from additional agents are limited and task-specific: multi-agent improves average vulnerability-detection accuracy, but lightweight non-agentic and single-agent configurations still dominate the Pareto front, accounting for 59 of 66 Pareto-optimal configurations. Model and prompt choice act as task-specific levers whose effective direction varies between tasks rather than as global defaults. We translate these findings into design guidelines for sustainable, task-aware LLM-based development tools.

</details>


### [299] [Discriminating Fixture Coverage in Agent-Infrastructure Verification Suites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02928)
*Xin Xu,Siru Tao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过变异分析量化"不变量套件通过正确实现、失败于错误实现"这一单一观察证据的价值：标准验证会放过突变体，冻结后的12项检查套件对10个突变体仅杀死5个；幸存者分两类（三例从未被激活、两例破坏无法观测的内部状态）；按7个判别维度预先登记未覆盖维度并各加一个夹具后，全部5个幸存者被杀，且各自死于预测的检查项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不变量套件和运行时监控器越来越多地用于把关智能体的部署决策，但支撑某个特定套件的证据几乎总是单一观察：它在被认为正确的实现上通过、在被认为错误的实现上失败。作者要衡量这一观察到底有多少价值，即这种标准验证是否足以证明套件的有效性。

Method: 对多会话智能体状态投影层的不变量套件应用变异分析：先构造一阶突变体（移除事件身份去重）验证标准认证的可靠性；随后冻结修复后的12项检查套件并记录其哈希，与一位未设计任何夹具的对抗性读者指定的10个突变体运行一次；对5个幸存者进行插桩对照参考实现以区分失效模式；将缺失输入视为覆盖问题，枚举输入空间的7个判别维度，事先登记哪些维度未被覆盖、应由哪些幸存者解释，并为每个未覆盖维度添加一个夹具，同时原样复用现有预言机。

Result: 标准验证认证的套件中，一个移除事件身份去重的一阶突变体通过了所有检查。冻结的12项检查套件对10个突变体仅杀死5个。插桩显示5个幸存者以两种不同方式失效：3个从未被激活（无夹具提供使突变代码表现不同的输入），另2个破坏了套件中任何预言机都无法观测的内部状态。为7个未覆盖判别维度各加一个夹具后，全部5个幸存者被杀，且各自死在为其预测维度编写的检查上。

Conclusion: 单一观察证据对不变量套件的价值有限，标准验证可能认证存在漏洞的套件。幸存者有两种需不同修复且无法从通过/失败报告中看出的失效模式；通过按判别维度补齐覆盖可有效杀死全部幸存者。作者将此报告为同一挑战集上的修复结果而非第二次留出估计，并公开产物（含冻结哈希、登记预测、全部突变体与运行日志）以使该区分可被检验。

Abstract: Invariant suites and runtime monitors increasingly gate agent deployment decisions, and the evidence offered for any particular suite is almost always a single observation: it passes an implementation believed correct and fails one believed broken. We measure what that observation is worth. Applying mutation analysis to an invariant suite for a multi-session agent state-projection layer, we first find that this standard validation certifies a suite in which a first-order mutant removing event-identity deduplication survives every check. We then freeze the repaired twelve-check suite, record its hash, and run it once against ten mutants specified by an adversarial reader who designed none of its fixtures: it kills five. Instrumenting the five survivors against the reference shows they fail in two distinct ways, not one. Three are never activated, because no fixture supplies an input on which the mutated code behaves differently at all. The other two corrupt internal state that no oracle in the suite can observe. The two modes need different repairs, and neither is visible from a pass/fail report. Treating the missing inputs as a coverage question, we enumerate seven discriminating dimensions of the input space, register in advance which are uncovered and which survivors they should explain, and add one fixture per uncovered dimension while reusing the existing oracles verbatim. All five survivors then die, each to the check written for its predicted dimension. We report this as a repair result on the same challenge set rather than a second held-out estimate, and give the artifact, including the frozen hash, the registered predictions, all mutants and the run logs, so the distinction is checkable.

</details>


### [300] [GTDD: Generative Test-Driven Development for AI Coding Agents with Adversarial Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02952)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出生成式测试驱动开发（GTDD），通过独立的测试代理在候选实现固定后不断生成新输入，用行为契约与历史反馈持续暴露初始测试之外的失败，并在理论上分析自适应选择下的误接受界限，在实验上验证开发过程中重新生成测试能降低失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理能根据观察到的样例调整实现，因此仅通过一组预定测试可能遗漏大量预期行为，导致未被实现的部分被误判为通过。需要一种机制让开发过程持续面对初始示例之外的失败，从而控制误接受风险。

Method: 提出GTDD：测试代理在每轮候选实现固定后，依据人类指定的行为契约与早期轮次反馈生成新输入；可信评估器检查这些输入，将简化后的反例返回给编码代理并保存为回归测试。并用有限总体分析刻画自适应候选选择下的误接受，量化测试可见性与重复反馈的影响，以及候选提交后新鲜随机审计对误接受的控制作用。还进行了有状态键值存储的配对实验，比较不同测试生成策略。

Result: 有限总体分析给出误接受界限，表明候选提交后的新鲜随机审计可在多轮开发中控制误接受。配对实验中，两种在开发过程中重新生成测试的策略，其平均失败率都低于由同一语言模型一次性生成测试的策略；向测试者提供候选源码未带来可检测的额外提升。要求测试数量相等的进一步条件未能将任一单一特征确定为差异来源。

Conclusion: GTDD将自适应开发反馈与既有回归测试和独立接受规则结合，可持续暴露初始示例之外的失败并控制误接受，在实验中优于一次性生成测试的策略，但具体机制仍需进一步分离研究。

Abstract: Test-driven development gives AI coding agents executable requirements for implementing software. Because these agents can adapt their implementations to the examples they observe, passing a predetermined collection of tests can leave substantial parts of the intended behavior unimplemented. We propose Generative Test-Driven Development (GTDD), a formulation of test-driven development in which a separate testing agent generates new inputs after each candidate implementation is fixed, using a human-specified behavioral contract and the feedback from earlier rounds. A trusted evaluator checks these inputs, returns reduced counterexamples to the coding agent, and saves them for regression testing, so development continually confronts failures beyond the initial examples. We characterize the evidence that this process provides through a finite-population analysis of false acceptance under adaptive candidate selection. The resulting bounds quantify how test visibility and repeated feedback affect acceptance, and show that fresh random audits after candidate commitment control false acceptance across development rounds. In a paired experiment on a stateful key-value store, both policies that regenerated tests during development ended with lower mean failure rates than the policy whose tests were generated once by the same language model, and giving the tester the candidate's source produced no detectable additional improvement. Further conditions requesting equal numbers of tests did not isolate any single feature of the policies as the source of this difference. GTDD combines this adaptive development feedback with established regression tests and an independent acceptance rule.

</details>


### [301] [BISCEPTER: Probability-Driven Bisection for Large-Scale System Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02995)
*Mingyan Gao,Celine Wüst,Zuming Jiang,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BISCEPTER：利用历史BIC延迟作为先验的概率驱动二分法，用加权中位数枢轴分割BIC概率质量，大幅减少二分迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分法是获取bug诱发提交（BIC）的常用手段，标准二分法选择剩余good-bad区间的中间提交以平衡提交数量，该策略在"每个提交等概率为BIC"假设下最优。但论文指出该假设与真实世界BIC历史不符。

Method: 构建了来自GCC、Linux内核与MariaDB的8,172个bug报告数据集，发现强时间偏斜：50%的BIC位于报告时间提交历史中最近的0.69%内。基于此提出BISCEPTER：以历史BIC延迟作为轻量先验，选择加权中位数枢轴以分割估计的BIC概率质量，同时保持与标准二分法相同的good-bad oracle与接口。

Result: 在三个大规模系统上评估，BISCEPTER相比标准中位数二分法平均减少25.75%的迭代次数（最高55.55%），在91.26%的测试用例中优于基线；鲁棒性实验表明在噪声历史数据下收益仍稳定。

Conclusion: BISCEPTER可有效节省实际软件调试工作，并为未来软件工程研究提供关于BIC分布的洞见。

Abstract: Identifying the bug-inducing commit (BIC) is a fundamental step in regression debugging and a key input to emerging BIC-aware fault-localization pipelines. In practice, BICs are commonly obtained with bisection. Standard bisection selects the median commit of the remaining good-bad interval, thereby balancing commit count. This strategy is optimal under the assumption that each commit is equally likely to be the BIC.
  This paper shows that this assumption does not match real-world BIC histories. We construct a dataset of 8,172 bug reports from GCC, the Linux kernel, and MariaDB. We find a strong temporal skew: across the studied systems, 50% of BICs lie within the most recent 0.69% of the report-time commit history. Motivated by this observation, we introduce BISCEPTER, a probability-driven bisection approach that uses historical BIC latency as a lightweight prior. Instead of selecting pivots that split the number of remaining commits, BISCEPTER selects weighted-median pivots that split estimated BIC probability mass, while preserving the same good-bad oracle and interface as standard bisection. We evaluate BISCEPTER on three large-scale systems. The evaluation results show that BISCEPTER reduces bisection iterations by 25.75% on average (up to 55.55%) compared with standard median bisection, while improving over the baseline in 91.26% of test cases. Robustness experiments further demonstrate that the benefit remains stable under noisy historical data. We expect that our research can effectively save effort in debugging software in practice and, more broadly, benefit future software engineering research by bringing insights about BIC distribution.

</details>


### [302] [MintEval: Do LLMs Implement the Trading Strategy You Asked For? A Behavioural-Equivalence Benchmark for Natural-Language-to-Strategy Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03080)
*Siyu Wang,Yifan Wang,Yuecheng He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MintEval是一个评估LLM将交易员口头策略指令实现为可执行代码的基准，通过逐bar执行对比动作而非代码相似度或利润，揭示生成代码"静默失败"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从生成交易信号转向编写执行代码，但其失败模式是静默的：代码能跑、回测能画图，却未实现交易员所描述的原始逻辑。现有代码基准只测单元测试正确性，金融基准只测预测，都无法衡量"实现是否忠实于被要求的策略"。

Method: 从可组合构件库程序化生成参考策略，反向翻译成口语化交易员指令，由被测模型重新实现。生成程序与参考程序在相同市场数据和摩擦成本下逐bar执行，比较其动作而非代码相似度或利润（alpha被差分消去）。MintEval v0含800个任务，基于BTCUSDT 15分钟数据，按执行测得的"状态跨度复杂度tau"分层，且tau与描述长度解耦。

Result: 低成本模型平均ActionMatch最高仅0.544，最多0.087任务被精确复现；在200任务分层子集上，前沿模型(Claude Opus 5.5)达0.889、精确复现0.575，但仍有0.275任务静默失败。给定构件菜单时模型几乎完美识别策略，然而79.2%规格被正确读取的实现仍在超过10%的活跃bar上偏离。某近期策略生成基准的LLM评判器原样应用时接受了所有这些静默失败。

Conclusion: 现有基准与LLM评判器无法检测策略实现中的静默失败，MintEval提供了以执行为中心、隔离alpha的评估范式，暴露了LLM将交易策略转化为代码时的忠实性缺口。

Abstract: Large language models are moving from producing trading signals to writing the code that executes them. The failure mode of the second role is silent: generated code runs, a backtest plots, yet the risk logic that the trader described is not the logic being executed. Existing code benchmarks test functional correctness on unit tests and finance benchmarks test forecasting; neither measures whether an implementation behaves like the strategy that was asked for. We introduce MintEval, a benchmark in which reference strategies are generated programmatically from a library of composable building blocks, back-translated into colloquial trader instructions, and re-implemented by the model under test. Generated and reference programs are executed bar by bar on identical market data and frictions, and compared on their actions rather than on code similarity or profit: alpha is differenced away. MintEval v0 contains 800 tasks on BTCUSDT 15-minute data, stratified by an execution-measured state-span complexity tau that is decoupled from description length. Low-cost models reach a mean ActionMatch of at most 0.544 and reproduce at most 0.087 of tasks exactly; on a stratified subset of 200 tasks a frontier model (Claude Opus 5.5) reaches 0.889 and reproduces 0.575 exactly, yet still fails silently on 0.275 of tasks. Given a menu of building blocks, models identify the strategy almost perfectly, yet 79.2% of the implementations whose specification was read correctly diverge on more than 10% of active bars. The LLM judge of a recent strategy-generation benchmark, applied verbatim, accepts every one of these silent failures.

</details>


### [303] [Pinning Decisions Before Failure: Executable Records of Underspecified Choices in AI-Assisted Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03237)
*Takeharu Mitsui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"决策钉(decision pin)"方法:先枚举需求中的欠定点,再为每点构造仅在该点不同的两个临时实现,保留能让两者产生分歧的输入;记录为"点名+确认输入+用户选择值",用于约束生成并按执行判定合规。40任务基准上92.5%的决策点获得分离输入,85-90%获得指明意图的钉子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言需求存在欠定性,模型实现时会静默地自行填补这些空白。在三个模型生成的600个测试套件中,42.7%不含任何能区分竞争性解读的测试。先写验收示例是常规补救办法,但一个被两种解读同时满足的示例无法起到区分作用,因此需要一种能显式暴露并固化欠定决策的机制。

Method: 在实践流程中插入两步:(1)按名称枚举需求中未充分指定的点;(2)对每个点构造两个仅在该点不同的临时实现,仅当执行两者显示分歧时才保留该候选输入。结果记录为决策钉:点名、确认的输入、以及人在两个展示结果之间选择的值。该记录随后用于约束生成,并通过执行来判定合规。

Result: 在40个带配对参考实现与两个模型的基准任务上,92.5%的决策点获得分离输入,85-90%得到指明预期决策的钉子。对703个独立生成实现的检查中,钉子与基准分类在96-97%上一致,分歧出现在三个任务上,其中一个任务两个分类器都出错。作为生成约束,钉子在所有210次生成中于钉定输入处均被遵守,在38个单元格中的留出输入上不差于散文规则,但也不优于陈述相同范围的散文。

Conclusion: 散文规则下的每一次合规失败都源于模型自行决定规则范围;其中一个案例静默推翻了另一条已记录决策,通过两个模型上的留一法归因于单条规则,文本级核对未能发现,而重放记录输入则成功捕获。该设置产生的此类冲突过少,无法评估回归步骤,作者说明了原因。

Abstract: A natural-language requirement leaves questions open, and a model asked to implement it settles them silently: across 600 generated test suites from three models, 42.7% contain no test that distinguishes the competing readings. An acceptance example written before implementing is the usual remedy, but an example both readings satisfy resolves nothing.
  We insert two steps into that practice: enumerate the requirement's underspecified points by name, then for each construct two throwaway implementations differing only in that point and keep a candidate input only if executing both shows they disagree. The result is recorded as a decision pin: the named point, the confirmed input, and the value the person chose between the two exhibited results. The same record then constrains generation and decides compliance by execution.
  On a benchmark of 40 tasks with paired reference implementations and two models, a separating input is obtained for 92.5% of decision points and a pin identifying the intended decision for 85-90%. As checks on 703 independently generated implementations, pins agree with the benchmark's classification on 96-97%, with disagreements on three tasks, one where both classifiers erred. As generation constraints, pins are honoured at the pinned input in all 210 generations and are never worse than a prose rule on held-out inputs in 38 cells, though no better than prose stating the same scope.
  Every compliance failure under a prose rule came from the model deciding the rule's scope itself; one such case silently overturned another recorded decision, was attributed to a single rule by leave-one-out on both models, and was missed by text-level reconciliation but caught by re-running the recorded input. The setting yields too few such conflicts to evaluate a regression step, and we say why.

</details>


### [304] [IntentCoding: Amplifying User Intent in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00066)
*Zheng Fang,Yihong Dong,Lili Mou,Dongming Jin,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出IntentCoding，一种无需训练、模型无关的解码策略，通过放大用户意图影响来提升LLM代码生成中多约束意图的遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成上能力强，但难以遵循包含多重约束的细粒度用户意图。实证分析发现：1) 约束数量增加时模型性能迅速下降；2) 用户意图虽影响模型logits，但影响强度不足以有效引导解码过程。

Method: 提出IntentCoding解码策略：通过掩码（masking out）意图来捕捉用户意图的影响，并应用多强度集成机制放大意图在生成过程中的作用。该方法模型无关、无需额外训练，可无缝集成到现有解码流程。同时构建CodeConstraints基准数据集，用于系统评估不同约束数量下的用户意图遵循情况。

Result: 在CodeConstraints、IFEvalCode、HumanEval和LiveCodeBench数据集上，相较标准解码显著提升约束满足度和功能正确性。相比贪心解码，CodeConstraints上相对提升最高71.0%，IFEvalCode最高67.3%，HumanEval和LiveCodeBench的pass@1最高提升29.3%。

Conclusion: 通过放大用户意图对解码的影响，可有效提升LLM在多约束场景下的意图遵循与代码正确性，且方法通用、即插即用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in code generation, but their adherence to fine-grained user intent with multiple constraints remains a significant challenge. Our empirical analysis reveals two key observations: 1) Model performance deteriorates quickly as the number of constraints in the user intent increases, and 2) While user intent does influence the model's logits, such an influence may not be strong enough to effectively steer the decoding process. To this end, we propose Intent-Amplified Code Generation (IntentCoding), a novel decoding strategy that enhances an LLM's ability to follow user intent. IntentCoding captures the influence of user intent by masking out the intent, and applies a multi-strength ensemble mechanism to amplify the effect of user intent during generation. IntentCoding is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and integrates seamlessly with existing decoding procedures. To enable systematic evaluation, we also construct CodeConstraints, a benchmark dataset specifically designed to test user intent compliance under varying numbers of constraints. Experiments on our constructed Constraints, as well as popular IFEvalCode, HumanEval and LiveCodeBench datasets, show that our IntentCoding model significantly improves both constraint satisfaction and functional correctness compared to standard decoding approaches. IntentCoding achieves up to 71.0% relative improvement on CodeConstraints, achieves up to 67.3% relative improvement on IFEvalCode and achieves up to 29.3% relative improvement in pass@1 on HumanEval and LiveCodeBench compared with greedy decoding.

</details>


### [305] [ClarifyCodeBench: Evaluating LLMs on Clarifying Ambiguous Requirements for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00711)
*Zheng Fang,Dongming Jin,Yihong dong,Yongmin Li,Kechi Zhang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClarifyCodeBench，一个用于评估大语言模型解决需求歧义能力的交互式基准，并揭示代码生成能力与需求澄清能力之间的解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成研究多假设输入需求完整明确，忽视了真实软件工程中需求常模糊、不完整、欠规范这一交互瓶颈，缺乏评估 LLM 主动澄清意图能力的基准。

Method: 构建基于真实编程任务的 ClarifyCodeBench，包含人工标注的多种歧义类型、澄清问题及标准答案；形式化两个评估指标：Turn-discounted Key Question Rate（惩罚低效提问）与 Optimal Round Adherence（衡量澄清过程精确性）；对六种先进 LLM 进行系统评测。

Result: 实验得到三点关键发现：1）能力解耦——强代码生成性能并不必然带来有效需求澄清；2）推理悖论——增加计算式思考提升代码正确性，但对识别歧义收益有限；3）多歧义天花板——歧义密度上升时澄清性能急剧下降，暴露出处理复杂真实需求的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 未来 AI4SE 研究需从静态代码合成转向交互式需求澄清，ClarifyCodeBench 为该方向提供了评估基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as programming assistants. However, the efficacy of code generation is constrained by the quality of input requirements, which are frequently ambiguous, incomplete, or underspecified. While LLMs excel at one-shot code synthesis, their ability to proactively clarify intent remains underexplored, as a critical trait for robust software engineering. Existing benchmarks largely overlook this interactive bottleneck, assuming perfectly specified prompts that do not reflect the iterative nature of requirement elicitation. To bridge this gap, we introduce ClarifyCodeBench, a novel interactive benchmark for evaluating LLMs' capability in resolving requirement ambiguity. Constructed from real-world programming tasks, ClarifyCodeBench features high-quality manual annotations, including N unique ambiguity types, associated clarification questions, and corresponding ground-truth answers. Furthermore, we formalize two rigorous metrics to assess the interaction quality: Turn-discounted Key Question Rate, which penalizes inefficient questioning, and Optimal Round Adherence, which measures the precision of the elicitation process. We conduct a systematic evaluation of six state-of-the-art LLMs using ClarifyCodeBench. Our empirical results yield three critical insights: 1) Capability Decoupling: Strong code generation performance does not inherently translate to effective requirement clarification; 2) The Reasoning Paradox: While increased computational thinking enhances code correctness, it yields marginal gains in identifying ambiguities; 3) The Multi-ambiguity Ceiling: LLMs' clarification performance degrades sharply as the density of ambiguities increases, revealing a significant bottleneck in handling complex, real-world specifications. Our work underscores the necessity for future AI4SE research to transition from static synthesis to interactive elicitation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [306] [Intent-Hiding Jailbreaks: An Information-Theoretic Framework for Compositional Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02302)
*Fengwei Tian,Ravi Tandon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从信息论视角研究"组合式意图隐藏越狱"：将有害目标任务与良性辅助任务打包成组合查询，通过"先验-后验匹配"使整体被判定有害的概率保持不变，从而绕过拒绝。作者分别在查询无关与查询相关两种设定下分析该问题，给出计算复杂性、最优水填充解与最小打包规模刻画，并在多个开源模型上实验验证其有效性与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，孤立状态下会被拒绝的有害请求，若嵌入到更大的、看似良性的查询中，可能得到不同回应，即组合式越狱。作者希望从信息论角度形式化理解这种"意图隐藏"机制，明确辅助任务选择如何影响模型对有害意图的估计，并探讨其可优化性与边界。

Method: 将每个任务关联一个"被判定为有害"的估计概率：全集上的平均为先验概率，包含目标的选定任务束上的平均为后验概率。选择辅助任务使二者相等（"先验-后验匹配"），从而在捆绑中保留有害目标的同时不改变估计意图。研究两种设定：(1) 查询无关设定——任务选择不考虑最终查询表达方式，证明在打包规模约束下精确匹配是计算困难的，给出分数权重下的最优"水填充"解，并刻画满足给定安全阈值的最小打包规模；(2) 查询相关设定——联合考虑任务选择与查询构造，在生成查询上评估意图隐藏与目标保留。

Result: 在多种打包规模、查询生成器和多个开源模型上评估了越狱有效性与目标行为保留。结果显示，在所评估的搜索预算下，组合式查询能够诱发超出直接请求基线的目标行为；同时揭示一个权衡：随着打包规模增大，若干模型在响应层面的目标保留倾向下降。

Conclusion: 组合式意图隐藏越狱可从信息论角度被有效建模，"先验-后验匹配"提供了统一的分析框架。该框架揭示了查询无关设定下的计算困难性与最优结构，以及查询相关设定下越狱有效性与目标保留之间的权衡，为理解和评估此类越狱的机制与限度提供了理论基础与实验证据。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can be vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in which harmful intent is obscured through composition with benign tasks. A harmful request refused in isolation may elicit a different response when embedded within a larger, seemingly benign query. We study these compositional intent-hiding jailbreaks from an information-theoretic perspective. Our formulation associates each task with an estimated probability of being judged harmful: the average over the full task collection defines the prior probability of harmful intent, while the average over a selected bundle containing the target defines the posterior. Selecting auxiliary tasks so that these averages agree, which we call prior-posterior matching, leaves the estimated intent unchanged even though the harmful target remains in the bundle.
  We study two settings that differ in whether query construction is part of the optimization. In the query-independent setting, tasks are selected without regard to how they will be expressed in the final query. We show that exact prior-posterior matching under a bundle-size constraint is computationally hard, derive an optimal water-filling solution for fractional weights, and characterize the smallest bundle satisfying a prescribed safety threshold. In the query-dependent setting, task selection and query construction are considered jointly, and intent concealment and target preservation are evaluated on the resulting query. We evaluate jailbreak effectiveness and preservation of the target behavior across bundle sizes, query generators, and several open-source models. These results show that compositional queries can elicit target behaviors beyond the direct-request baseline under the evaluated search budgets, while revealing a trade-off: as bundle size increases, response-level target preservation tends to decrease for several models.

</details>


### [307] [MIRROR: Multipath Quorum Integrity for LLM Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02349)
*Ryuichi Yamafuji Lun,Jingzhen Wang,Shreyas Kolte,Ruiteng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MIRROR 是一种通信层完整性原语，通过将规范化负载复制到 k 条逻辑路由并在严格多数路由 digest 一致时接受消息，在路由受损比例 α < 0.5 时把 Agent-in-the-Middle 攻击成功率降至 0%，且不增加 LLM token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
