Motivation: 大语言模型多智能体系统依赖智能体间通信，这带来了 Agent-in-the-Middle（AiTM）攻击这一尚未被充分研究的漏洞：攻击者可在传输过程中篡改消息而无需攻破智能体本身。已有工作报告在结构化任务上攻击成功率（ASR）接近 100%。现有防御要么依赖语义验证（需要额外推理且可能阻断良性输出），要么依赖传输层加密（当中间人合法终止 TLS 时无效），因此需要一种通信层的完整性机制。

Method: MIRROR 将单个规范化 payload 复制到 k 条逻辑路由上，只有当严格多数路由报告相同 digest 时才接受该消息。它使用无密钥哈希，本身不认证任何内容——主动在途攻击者总能对已篡改的 payload 重新计算 digest。所有完整性都来自"诚实路由占多数"这一假设：digest 仅用于使见证路由保持常数大小，并在第二原像抗性下把恢复的 payload 绑定到 quorum 一致的值。作者在路由受损比例 α < 0.5 的约束下给出保证，并将其扩展到相关性路由情形，此时关键量是最大共享失效组的规模而非路由数量。

Result: 作者进一步证明可用性与完整性在同一阈值处退化：在 α = 0.5 以下，quorum 拒绝与丢包攻击者都无法阻断诚实流量。在 MMLU、HumanEval 和 MBPP 上，覆盖两个框架与四种通信拓扑，并在 MetaGPT 部署中针对生产 API 进行测试，MIRROR 在阈值以下将 ASR 降至 0%，且 LLM token 成本仅为 1x。相比之下，同等部署下 LLM-as-a-Judge 成本为 35x，并在拓扑扫描中阻断多达 44.2% 的良性输出。

Conclusion: MIRROR 提供了一种低成本、不依赖语义验证或传输层加密的通信层完整性方案，在诚实路由占多数的假设下同时保障完整性与可用性，将 AiTM 攻击成功率降至 0%，且不增加 LLM token 开销，显著优于 LLM-as-a-Judge 等现有防御。

Abstract: Inter-agent communication is central to Large Language Model Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS), but it introduces an underexplored vulnerability: Agent-in-the-Middle (AiTM) attacks that manipulate messages in transit without compromising the agents themselves. Prior work reports Attack Success Rates (ASR) approaching 100% on structured tasks. Existing defenses rely on semantic validation, which requires additional inference and can block benign outputs, or on transport-layer encryption, which does not help when an intermediary legitimately terminates TLS. We present MIRROR, a communication-layer integrity primitive that replicates a single canonicalized payload across k logical routes and accepts a message only when a strict majority of routes report the same digest. MIRROR uses unkeyed hashing and so authenticates nothing on its own, since an active on-path adversary can always recompute a digest over a payload it has modified. All integrity derives from the assumption that honest routes form a majority. The digest serves only to make witness routes constant-size and to bind the recovered payload to the quorum-agreed value under second-preimage resistance. We give the guarantee under a route-compromise bound alpha < 0.5, and extend it to correlated routes, where the quantity that matters is the size of the largest shared-failure group and not the route count. We further show that availability and integrity degrade at the same threshold: below alpha = 0.5, quorum-denial and message-dropping adversaries cannot block honest traffic. Across MMLU, HumanEval, and MBPP on two frameworks and four communication topologies, and in a MetaGPT deployment against a production API, MIRROR reduces ASR to 0% below the threshold at 1x LLM token cost. LLM-as-a-Judge costs 35x in the same deployment, and blocks up to 44.2% of benign outputs in the topology sweep.

</details>


### [308] [Hop-Decayed Influence: New Vulnerabilities of Structural Auxiliary Indexing in GraphRAG Pipelines with LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02373)
*Jisung Park,John Le,Heath Cooper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出GraphRAG中"辅助模式级实体"这一新攻击面，并设计3S框架与HDI攻击，仅修改极少辅助结构即可实现高成功率攻击且规避防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG在离线索引阶段构建语义摘要、层次边、预计算分数等辅助结构，决定查询时的检索优先级。此前攻击仅针对实例级组件（节点、边、三元组），忽视了这些模式级结构的潜在风险。

Method: 将辅助模式级实体形式化为新的攻击面，提出3S框架（语义、结构、评分）。核心HDI攻击通过查询感知的影响传播识别高影响目标，并在索引后破坏其辅助结构。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA两个基准和Microsoft GraphRAG、HippoRAG2两种架构上，HDI以低至0.016%的结构修改量达到88-94%攻击成功率；每次修改影响多达6.00个查询（模式杠杆比），实现1:N放大；以超过99%的规避率绕过困惑度与改写防御。

Conclusion: 辅助模式级实体因是系统生成、语言连贯的产物而被隐式信任、缺乏运行时验证，构成当前GraphRAG防御的结构性盲区。

Abstract: GraphRAG pipelines construct auxiliary structures during offline indexing--semantic summaries, hierarchical edges, and pre-computed scores--that determine how retrieval is prioritised at query time. Prior attacks target only instance-level components (nodes, edges, triples), overlooking these schema-level structures. We formalise Auxiliary Schema-Level Entity as a novel attack surface and propose the 3S Framework (Semantics, Structure, Scoring) for its systematic exploitation. Our Hop-Decayed Influence (HDI) attack identifies high-impact targets through query-aware influence propagation and corrupts their auxiliary structures post-indexing. Across two benchmarks (HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA) and two architectures (Microsoft GraphRAG, HippoRAG2), HDI achieves 88-94% attack success rate while modifying as few as 0.016% of auxiliary structures. Each modification affects up to 6.00 queries (Schema Leverage Ratio), demonstrating 1:N amplification unavailable to instance-level attacks. Manipulated structures evade perplexity and paraphrase defenses with over 99% evasion rate, as they remain linguistically coherent system-generated artifacts. These results reveal that auxiliary schema-level entities receive implicit trust without runtime validation, constituting a structural blind spot in current GraphRAG defenses. https://github.com/Jisung-Pacific/HDI-GraphRAG-Attack.

</details>


### [309] [Unifying Privacy Accounting: Information Equivalence and Information Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02414)
*Buxin Su,Qiaoshi Yang,Yiding Su,Chendi Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在统一的 information-theoretic 框架下研究四类基于曲线的 privacy notion，证明在特定条件下它们信息等价，并量化 RDP 曲线与 zCDP 压缩之间的 information loss 及其实际影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Differential privacy 有多种 notion，不同选择会影响 privacy analysis 与 utility，因此需要厘清这些 notion 之间的关系，并评估简化或压缩表示是否会造成信息损失。

Method: 针对固定有序的 output distribution pair，建立 $(\varepsilon,\delta)$-DP 的两个 directional privacy profile、hypothesis-testing trade-off function pair 与 extended privacy-loss distribution 之间的信息等价；并在 exact RDP curve 于某阶大于 1 处有限时，将其纳入该等价类；随后比较 directional profile 取最大值、或将 RDP 曲线压缩为单一 zCDP 参数所带来的信息损失。

Result: 在 mild condition 下，这些 notion 的差异仅在于语义解释与计算需求。取 directional privacy profile 最大值或压缩 RDP 到 zCDP 会损失信息。RDP 与 zCDP bound 的差距对 Gaussian noise 为零，但对 Gaussian-mixture、Laplace、discrete Gaussian 和 Poisson-subsampled Gaussian mechanisms 通常为正，并随独立 composed mechanisms 数量线性增长。实用上，在相同 certified privacy level 下，保留完整 RDP 曲线相较 zCDP 可将 Gaussian-mixture noise 所需 noise variance 最多降低 45%；在 Poisson subsampling 下对 Fashion-MNIST 的 DP-SGD，RDP-based privacy accountant 相比 zCDP-based accountant 可提升 test accuracy 最多 8.73 个百分点。

Conclusion: 曲线型 privacy notion 在 mild condition 下具有信息等价性，而 zCDP 等压缩会带来可量化的信息损失；保留完整 RDP 曲线在 privacy accounting 与 utility 上具有实际优势。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) admits several notions, but the choice among them may affect both privacy analysis and utility. In this paper, we consider four mainstream curve-based privacy notions within a unified information-theoretic framework. For a fixed ordered pair of output distributions, we establish information equivalence among the two directional privacy profiles of $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP, the pair of hypothesis-testing trade-off functions, and the extended privacy-loss distribution. The exact Rényi differential privacy (RDP) curve joins this equivalence class whenever it is finite at some order greater than one. Under this mild condition, choosing among these notions changes only their semantic interpretation and computational requirements. In contrast, taking the maximum of the directional privacy profiles or compressing the RDP curve into a single zero-concentrated differential privacy (zCDP) parameter can lose information. We quantify the information loss between the exact RDP curve and its zCDP bound for standard noise mechanisms. This gap is zero for Gaussian noise but generally positive for Gaussian-mixture, Laplace, discrete Gaussian, and Poisson-subsampled Gaussian mechanisms. Moreover, this gap grows linearly with the number of independently composed mechanisms. Our information-theoretic perspective has practical consequences. At the same certified privacy level, retaining the full RDP curve rather than using zCDP reduces the required noise variance by up to $45\%$ for Gaussian-mixture noise in workloads comparable in size to the American Community Survey. For DP-SGD on Fashion-MNIST under Poisson subsampling, an RDP-based privacy accountant improves test accuracy by up to $8.73$ percentage points compared to a zCDP-based accountant when both are calibrated to the same $(\varepsilon,δ)$ guarantee.

</details>


### [310] [Mitigating Private Data Leakage in LLMs with Whiteout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02418)
*Anna Yoo Jeong Ha,Ronik Bhaskar,Haitao Zheng,Ben Y. Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Whiteout工具，通过精确设计的混淆样本覆写个人敏感信息，阻止LLM复述目标PSI，兼顾效用与安全性且优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在大规模未过滤数据上训练，常记忆并泄露生日、电话、住址等个人敏感信息（PSI），对高管、政治人物、法官等高风险个体威胁尤大。现有基于机器遗忘的缓解方法过度删除信息、损害模型效用与安全性，且极易被攻击。

Method: Whiteout是一种实用工具，在个人提出请求后，使用精确且精心设计的混淆样本对目标PSI进行覆写，从而阻止LLM复述其真实PSI。

Result: 在不同规模与厂商的现代LLM（含某广泛使用的OpenAI模型）上评估：Whiteout有效阻止目标PSI泄露，对模型效用与安全性影响可忽略，并优于现有替代方案；在黑盒攻击（如越狱）与白盒自适应攻击（如重学习、量化）等广泛对抗手段下仍具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Whiteout为个人提供了一种实用的PSI保护手段，并以对Whiteout安全与伦理影响的讨论作结。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are trained on massive, largely unfiltered datasets, including content scraped from nearly every accessible website and user inputs. As a result, LLMs often memorize and reproduce personally sensitive information (PSI) such as birth dates, phone numbers, and home addresses. This leads to significant privacy risks, particularly for high-profile individuals such as executives, politicians, and judges. Existing mitigations largely rely on machine unlearning. However, these methods often remove more information than needed, degrade model utility and safety, and are highly vulnerable to attacks.
  This paper presents Whiteout, a practical tool that, upon requests by individuals, prevents LLMs from regurgitating their genuine PSIs, by overwriting them using precise and carefully designed obfuscation samples. We evaluate Whiteout on modern LLMs of varying sizes and makers, including a widely-used OpenAI model. Results show that Whiteout effectively prevents disclosure of the targeted PSIs, has negligible impact on model utility and safety, and outperforms existing alternatives. We also test Whiteout against a wide range of countermeasures, from black-box attacks like jailbreaking to white-box adaptive attacks like relearning and quantization. Finally, we conclude with a discussion on the security and ethical implications of Whiteout.

</details>


### [311] [Evaluating and Improving the Robustness of Large Language Models to Input Sequence Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02432)
*Narek Maloyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本论文围绕大语言模型（LLM）在生产环境中的对抗鲁棒性问题，提出生成式鲁棒性度量 R_stab(f)、LLM-as-a-Judge 黑盒攻击 ASA、多模态特洛伊检测方法、多模型委员会防御以及针对 MCP 智能体系统的 AttestMCP 证明机制，系统性地评估并提升了 LLM 对对抗性输入序列变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产系统中的 LLM 面临提示注入、后门（特洛伊）攻击以及自动质量指标被操纵等威胁，亟需系统化的鲁棒性评估与改进方法。

Method: 1) 提出基于每步输出分布间 Jensen-Shannon 散度的生成式鲁棒性度量 R_stab(f)；2) 对局部化攻击证明 V(h) ≤ 1 - R_class(h)；3) 对非局部化攻击提出校准经验模型；4) 针对 LLM-as-a-Judge 开发自适应进化黑盒攻击 ASA；5) 针对 MCP 智能体系统提出基于 HMAC 保护包的 AttestMCP 工具调用证明与 Commit Boundary 隔离模式。

Result: ASA 攻击成功率最高达 73.8%，开放模型间迁移率最高 62.6%；在 Trojan Detection Challenge 2023（Pythia-1.4B）上代理触发器 REASR 约 0.99，但对真实触发器召回率仅约 0.17（基线约 0.14）；在 SaTML CTF 2024 上系统化四类多层防御绕过方法，将 ASR 从 90% 降至 15-25%；5-7 个异构模型组成的委员会使 Gemma-3-4B 的 ASR 降低 47-55 个百分点，7 模型时降至 19.3%；在 847 个场景的 MCPBench 上，AttestMCP 将平均 ASR 从 53.7% 降至 12.4%。

Conclusion: 所提方法已实现于 JudgeGuard、TrojanArmor 软件套件及 MCPSec 模块中，为 LLM 系统的对抗鲁棒性评估与防御提供了可落地的系统性方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) in production systems face prompt injections, trojans (backdoors), and manipulation of automatic quality metrics. This thesis develops models, methods, and algorithms for evaluating and improving LLM robustness to adversarial input sequence variations. We propose R_stab(f), a generative robustness metric based on the Jensen-Shannon divergence between per-step output distributions under small input perturbations. For localized attacks we prove V(h) <= 1 - R_class(h), where R_class(h) is the probability that a decision operator h keeps its decision under small perturbations. For non-localized attacks we propose a calibrated empirical model. For LLM-as-a-Judge systems we develop ASA, an adaptive evolutionary black-box attack that reaches an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 73.8%, with transfer between open models up to 62.6%. On Trojan Detection Challenge 2023 data (Pythia-1.4B), surrogate triggers reach REASR ~0.99 while recall of the true triggers is ~0.17 against a baseline of ~0.14. On SaTML CTF 2024 we systematize four classes of bypasses of multi-layer defenses, which reduce the ASR from 90% to 15-25%. Committees of 5-7 heterogeneous models reduce the ASR for Gemma-3-4B by 47-55 percentage points, to 19.3% with 7 models. For agentic systems based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), we propose AttestMCP, which attests tool calls with HMAC-protected packets at under 0.1 ms per call, and the Commit Boundary isolation pattern. On the MCPBench benchmark of 847 scenarios they reduce the average ASR from 53.7% to 12.4%. The methods are implemented in the JudgeGuard and TrojanArmor software suites and the MCPSec module.

</details>


### [312] [SoK: Stablecoins in the Quantum Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02435)
*Panagiotis Chatzigiannis,Navid Alamati,Suvradip Chakraborty,Duc V. Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于后量子稳定币的知识系统化（SoK）研究，构建了稳定币专属威胁模型，将密码依赖映射到货币控制面，并从授权主体、变更位置、迁移类型三个维度对迁移方案进行分类，揭示了"权限—责任缺口"这一核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定币在数字资产生态中支撑支付、交易、抵押与跨链结算，但其价值集中于发行方、托管、升级、预言机与跨链桥密钥，同时继承宿主链账户、共识、rollup与隐私系统的量子脆弱性。现有研究缺乏针对稳定币这一特殊对象的后量子迁移分析框架，因此需要一篇系统化梳理。

Method: 采用知识系统化（SoK）方法：1）建立稳定币专属威胁模型；2）将密码学依赖关系映射到货币控制面；3）沿三个维度对迁移选择进行分类——谁有权授权变更、变更必须发生在何处、以及属于混合式/后量子原生/经典组件封装中的哪一种；4）回顾相关密码原语，区分一般区块链故障与其在稳定币场景下的特定影响；5）考察近期区块链与发行方控制的互操作性提案，并结合现行稳定币监管分析迁移选择与赎回、连续性、干预要求的关系。

Result: 研究识别出若干开放问题，包括：聚合与门限授权、按操作区分的安全等级与成本、休眠与被包装供应、隐私与合规使能转账，以及量子脆弱稳定币敞口的度量。

Conclusion: 论文强调存在"权限—责任缺口"：有能力迁移某组件的参与方，往往并非在迁移失败时承担损失的持有者、交易所、协议或发行方。这一错配是后量子稳定币迁移的核心治理与安全挑战，需在后续研究中重点解决。

Abstract: Stablecoins support payments, trading, collateral, and cross-chain settlement across the digital-asset ecosystem. They also concentrate value behind issuer, custody, upgrade, oracle, and bridge keys while inheriting the quantum vulnerabilities of host-chain accounts, consensus, rollups, and privacy systems. This paper presents a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) on post-quantum stablecoins. We develop a stablecoin-specific threat model, map cryptographic dependencies to monetary control surfaces, and classify migration choices along three dimensions: who can authorize a change, where the change must occur, and whether it is hybrid, post-quantum native, or an encapsulation of a classical component. We emphasize an authority-liability gap: the party able to migrate a component is often different from the holders, exchanges, protocols, or issuers that bear the loss if it fails. We review the relevant cryptographic primitives, but distinguish general blockchain failures from their stablecoin-specific effects. We also examine recent blockchain and issuer-controlled interoperability proposals, and relate migration choices to redemption, continuity, and intervention requirements under current stablecoin regulation. Our findings identify open problems in aggregate and threshold authorization, operation-specific security levels and costs, dormant and wrapped supply, private and compliance-enabled transfers, and measurement of quantum-vulnerable stablecoin exposure.

</details>


### [313] [SideKernel: A Usable microVM Sandbox for AI Coding Agents on macOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02456)
*Dimitrios Prasakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向AI编程代理的开源本地macOS微虚拟机沙箱SideKernel，基于用户调研发现以可用性为核心设计目标，并通过23项能力测试与现有沙箱对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理是不可信的系统组件，却需要在开发者机器上拥有自主权，这一矛盾构成安全问题。沙箱可提供隔离环境，但面向本地macOS开发的现有开源方案数量少且使用繁琐。作者通过在线用户调研发现，不足40%的AI编程代理用户会在沙箱中运行代理，并识别出阻碍沙箱采用的主要可用性障碍。

Method: 基于调研发现的可用性障碍设计并开发SideKernel——一个开源、本地、基于微虚拟机的macOS AI编程代理沙箱。评估时，作者整理市场上的沙箱清单，按五项纳入标准筛选，再从用户调研揭示的可用性障碍中导出23项能力测试，对SideKernel与符合条件的沙箱进行对比分析。

Result: 对比分析显示，只有少数沙箱与SideKernel相似；在这些沙箱中，Docker Sandboxes与SideKernel在与可用性相关的能力特性上得分最高。

Conclusion: SideKernel为本地macOS环境下的AI编程代理提供了一个可用性优先的开源沙箱方案。论文的次要贡献是对现有本地、开源、基于微虚拟机的macOS沙箱解决方案空间进行了调研。

Abstract: AI coding agents are untrusted system components, yet they require autonomy on the developer machines they run on. This contradiction is a security problem. Sandboxes provide an isolated environment, but for local macOS development, the existing local, open-source options for AI coding agents are few in number and cumbersome to use. I conducted a formative online user survey which indicates that fewer than 40% of AI coding agent users run their agents in a sandbox and identifies the top usability barriers hindering AI coding agent sandbox adoption. These findings are used to develop SideKernel: an open-source, local, microVM-based macOS sandbox for AI coding agents designed for usability. To evaluate SideKernel, I compiled a list of sandboxes available on the market and filtered it against five inclusion criteria. Then I performed a comparative analysis between SideKernel and the sandboxes that satisfy these criteria, across 23 capability tests derived from the usability barriers revealed by the user survey. I discovered that only a few sandboxes are similar to SideKernel, and that among those, Docker Sandboxes and SideKernel score highest on capability features related to usability. A secondary contribution of this paper is a survey of the existing solution space for local, open-source, microVM-based macOS sandboxes for AI coding agents.

</details>


### [314] [Defense-in-Depth at the Perception-Reasoning Interface of LLM-Centric Agentic UAV Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03319)
*Mohammadhossein Homaei,Yousef Emami,Sajad Homayoun,Rahim Taheri,Hao Zhou,Miguel Gutierrez Gaitan,Bo Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在LLM驱动的无人机集群"感知—推理"接口上实现并评估多层防御,推导了输入侧三层防御的闭式触发边界,并通过30组仿真验证;同时指出拒绝式响应会显著增加成本(79%/74%),而安全校验层虽不检测攻击却能降低37.5%攻击代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于无人机集群任务调度(如读取结构化传感器报告并决定访问顺序),攻击者只要悄悄篡改这些报告就能在不改动模型权重或无人机的情况下改变集群行为。此前针对该接口的防御多为架构层面的设想,缺乏实际实现与评测,因此需要实证性的防御纵深评估。

Method: 在LLM中心化集群的感知—推理接口实现五层防御纵深:检查报告来源、数值物理可行性、与集群几何及服务历史的预测一致性、调度是否饿死某些传感器,以及前述失效时交由忽略可疑输入的确定性调度器接管。对输入侧三层,先行推导报告可被扭曲到何种程度才触发该层反应的闭式边界,边界由部署参数确定,且在任何攻击数据收集之前固定;随后让攻击者强到足以击败上一层,进行30组匹配仿真对比预测与实测边界。

Result: 30组匹配仿真中,预测边界与实测边界一致。将攻击检测与响应分离后,系统拒绝报告时以最近一次被接受的报告替代:这阻止了攻击者控制无人机调度,但相比无防御系统,两种检测器分别使累积成本增加79%和74%。安全校验层未检测到任何攻击,却仍将攻击导致的成本降低了37.5%。

Conclusion: 在LLM-无人机集群感知—推理接口上,防御纵深可以实际落地并实现可预测的触发边界;但忽略"检测"与"响应"的区别代价高昂,拒绝式替代响应会大幅抬高累积成本,而看似无效的安全校验层仍能带来可观的安全收益,说明响应策略设计与检测机制同等重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly support Uncrewed Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarm operations such as data collection scheduling, where the model reads structured sensor reports and decides which sensors to visit. An adversary who quietly manipulates those reports can redirect the swarm without modifying the model weights or the UAV. Defenses for this interface have been proposed architecturally but rarely implemented or evaluated. We implement and evaluate defense-in-depth at the perception-reasoning interface of LLM-Centric Agentic UAV Swarms. Five layers check the provenance of a report, whether its values are physically admissible, whether they agree with what swarm geometry and service history predict, whether the resulting schedule starves any sensor, and, when these fail, hand control to a deterministic scheduler that ignores the suspect input. We test each layer against an adversary strong enough to defeat the layer before it. For each of the three input-side layers, we derive in closed form how far a report can be distorted before that layer reacts, fixing each boundary from deployment parameters before any attack data is collected; across thirty matched simulation runs, predicted and measured boundaries agree. Separating attack detection from response is a well-established principle, and we quantify the cost of neglecting this distinction at the perception-reasoning interface. When the system rejects a report, it replaces it with the most recent accepted report. This prevents the adversary from controlling the UAV schedule, but it also increases cumulative cost by 79% and 74% for the two detectors, respectively, compared with the undefended system. The safety check does not detect any attacks, but it nevertheless reduces the attack-induced cost by 37.5%.

</details>


### [315] [CITADEL: CWE-Guided Insertion of Hardware Trojans via Analysis of DFG-Enabled LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02544)
*Jayanth Thangellamudi,Raghul Saravanan,Sudipta Paria,Swarup Bhunia,Sai Manoj P D*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CITADEL是一个利用大语言模型(LLM)与数据流图(DFG)实现CWE引导的硬件木马(HT)自动化植入框架，可生成最小、可综合、接口保持且带超稀有触发条件的木马。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件木马与系统级漏洞日益复杂，对现代集成电路构成重大风险。然而构造真实的HT场景负担沉重：研究人员需手动分析复杂的RTL结构、识别潜在弱点，并设计隐蔽且可综合、同时保持功能正确性的植入方案。

Method: CITADEL结合结构化CWE语义与DFG派生的结构上下文，辅助用户识别相关漏洞、定位所选插入点周围的模块，并执行意图条件化的RTL修改。框架生成最小、可综合、接口保持且具有超稀有触发条件的硬件木马。

Result: 在多种RTL设计上的实验评估表明，所有生成的HT均100%语法正确，在大规模随机仿真下不可检测，并能在其预期激活条件下被功能性触发。

Conclusion: CITADEL是一种可扩展且具有原则性的方法，可用于生成真实的硬件木马基准测试集。

Abstract: The increasing sophistication of Hardware Trojans (HTs) and system-level vulnerabilities poses significant risks to modern integrated circuits. However, constructing realistic HT scenarios, remains a substantial burden: researchers must manually analyze complex RTL structures, identify plausible weaknesses, and craft stealthy, synthesizable insertions that preserve functional correctness. This paper introduces CITADEL CWE-Guided Insertion of Trojans via Analysis of DFG-Enabled LLMs, a framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) and Data Flow Graphs (DFGs) to automate CWE-grounded HT synthesis. CITADEL uses structured CWE semantics together with DFG-derived structural context to assist the user in identifying relevant vulnerabilities, localize the module surrounding the chosen insertion point, and perform intent-conditioned RTL modification. The framework produces minimal, synthesizable, and interface-preserving HTs with ultra-rare triggers. Experimental evaluation across diverse RTL designs demonstrates that all generated HTs are 100% syntactically correct, remain undetectable under large-scale random simulation, and are functionally triggerable under their intended activation conditions. These results highlight CITADEL as a scalable and principled method for generating realistic HT benchmarks.

</details>


### [316] [Out of Sync, Out of Sight: Phantom State Attacks against IIoT Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02552)
*Sabrine Ennaji,Elhadj Benkhelifa,Nadia Kabachi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“幻影状态攻击”（PSA），一种被动、零查询的威胁模型：通过向攻击流量注入有界时序漂移，使观测点跨越最近的时间窗口边界，令IDS聚合出与真实过程状态不符的“幻影状态”，从而在无需修改数据包、扰动特征或查询分类器的情况下降低检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对工业物联网入侵检测系统的对抗研究大多依赖梯度访问、反复查询模型或学习正常流量模型；少数重塑包时序的工作也需拟合正常流量的时序模型。而工业监控流水线普遍依赖滑动/翻滚窗口聚合遥测以重建运行状态，其判断不仅取决于“观察到什么”，还取决于“观测落在窗口边界的哪一侧”。这一时间同步假设长期被忽视，构成了新的攻击面。

Method: PSA 采用被动、零查询威胁模型：不修改数据包、不扰动特征、不查询分类器、不拟合任何正常流量模型。它依据攻击流自身的到达间隔变异性，标定有界时序漂移，仅做跨最近窗口边界所需的最小偏移，使观测被重新分配到相邻窗口，导致IDS重建出偏离真实过程状态的幻影状态。

Result: 在 ToN-IoT 与 CIC IIoT 2025（DataSense）数据集上，针对随机森林、MLP 与 XGBoost 评估检测退化、同步失真、隐蔽性与攻击成本。PSA 对包数足以实现窗口边界重分配的流能显著降低检测性能，而对其他流几乎无影响，效果具有条件性。基于查询的基线原始成功率更高，但每窗口需要大量查询，而 PSA 无需任何查询。

Conclusion: 研究将时间聚合识别为一种攻击面，其可达性所需假设比既有规避技术更弱，揭示了工业物联网入侵检测在时间同步层面的脆弱性。

Abstract: Machine learning-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) are critical for securing Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) environments. Most adversarial research against them perturbs the feature vector or the traffic that produces it, and depends on gradient access, repeated model queries, or a learned model of benign traffic. A smaller line of work reshapes packet timing without querying the detector, but makes malicious traffic mimic a learned model of benign timing. Across these approaches, one assumption of industrial monitoring pipelines has received little attention: temporal synchronization. An IDS reconstructs operational state by aggregating telemetry into sliding or tumbling windows, so its view depends not only on what is observed but on when each observation falls relative to a window boundary.
  We introduce the Phantom State Attack (PSA), which exploits that dependence under a passive, zero-query threat model. Rather than modifying packets, perturbing features, querying the classifier, or fitting any model of benign traffic, PSA injects bounded timing drift calibrated to the attack flow's own inter-arrival variability, moving observations across the nearest window boundary by the minimal shift needed. The IDS then reconstructs a phantom state that diverges from the true process state.
  We evaluate PSA on ToN-IoT and CIC IIoT 2025 (DataSense), against Random Forest, MLP and XGBoost, measuring detection degradation, synchronization distortion, stealth, and attacker cost. PSA degrades detection on flows carrying enough packets for window-boundary redistribution, and leaves others almost unchanged, so its effect is conditional. A query-based baseline reaches higher raw success but needs many queries per window, while PSA needs none. The results identify temporal aggregation as an attack surface reachable under weaker assumptions than prior evasion techniques.

</details>


### [317] [Pincer: Resource Authorization for Agents using a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02569)
*Mayank Rathee,Alexander Stepanov,Shalin Madabhavi,Jinhao Zhu,Raluca Ada Popa,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Pincer 是一种在资源层运行的新型防御机制，通过数字孪生自动学习并执行用户特定的最小权限策略，与工具调用层防御协同工作，在安全性和实用性上均优于多个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理日益长周期、自主化，依赖通用 shell 并维护持久记忆以自我改进，这使其能力增强的同时也更难防御外部对手。现有的限制架构的防御（如类型化工具、信息流控制、策略预测引擎）牺牲过多功能而难以采用。当前部署的代理依赖用户介导和自动模式沙箱作为主要防御，但用户介导策略会随时间衰减且重复权限请求导致用户疲劳，自动模式的工具调用分类器不学习用户特定策略且并非为防御对抗性设置而设计。

Method: Pincer 在资源层运行，与工具调用层防御协同。其核心是数字孪生——一个隔离上下文的模型，能自动学习并执行动态的用户特定最小权限策略。数字孪生持续学习用户偏好，充当用户代理来处理代理的权限请求。为模拟学习阶段，提出了一个新的以用户为中心的数据集，包含多天的用户-代理交互转录示例。

Result: 评估显示，Pincer 在安全性和实用性上均强于多个基线，包括 LLM 评判器变体和 Conseca（HotOS '25）的改编版本。在某些攻击类型上，Pincer 的设计带来了显著的安全提升，优于所有其他基线；即使在其他类型攻击上，也优于基线。

Conclusion: Pincer 通过在资源层引入持续学习的数字孪生，实现了用户特定的动态最小权限策略执行，在不牺牲过多功能的前提下显著提升了编码代理的安全性，是一种可与现有工具调用层防御协同的有效防御方案。

Abstract: Coding agents have become increasingly long-horizon, autonomous, reliant on general-purpose shell and maintain their own persistent memory for self-improvement. While these capabilities have made the agents powerful, they have also made them harder to defend against external adversaries. Defenses that restrict this architecture --- typed tools, information-flow control, or policy prediction engines --- give up too much functionality to be adopted. Agents deployed today (e.g. Claude, Codex) rely on a combination of user-mediated and automode sandboxing as their primary defense. In user-mediated sandboxing, user-maintained policies decay over time and repeated permission requests cause user fatigue, while auto mode's tool-call classifiers learn no user-specific policy and are not meant to defend against adversarial setups. Pincer is a new defense that operates at the resource layer and works alongside existing defenses at the tool-call layer like the auto mode. At the core of Pincer lies a digital twin, an isolated-context model that automatically learns and enforces dynamic user-specific least-privilege policies. The digital twin keeps continually learning the user's preferences allowing it to act as the user's proxy for the agent's permission requests. To emulate the learning phase, we propose a new usercentric dataset with examples following a multi-day transcript of user-agent interaction. Our evaluation shows that Pincer performs strongly on both security and utility in comparison to several baselines which includes variants of LLM judges and adaptations of Conseca (HotOS '25). We highlight attack types where Pincer's design leads to a significant security improvement compared to all other baselines, while outperforming the baselines even for other types of attacks.

</details>


### [318] [From TS-SUF-2 to TS-SUF-4: Practical Security Enhancements for FROST2 Threshold Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02805)
*Will Wang,Syh-Yuan Tan,Ryan Chow,Chanson Chan,Martin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 FROST2 的增强变体 FROST2+，通过引入额外的预处理令牌验证，在同一计算假设下将安全性从 TS-SUF-2 提升至 TS-SUF-4，并保持实用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FROST2 是实用的门限 Schnorr 签名方案，但在 one-more 离散对数假设、静态腐败和集中式密钥生成下仅达到 TS-SUF-2 安全级别，易受 TS-UF-3 攻击，存在安全局限。

Method: FROST2+ 在 FROST2 基础上集成额外的预处理令牌验证，以缓解 TS-UF-3 和 TS-UF-4 漏洞，同时保持实用效率，并支持如 PedPoP 等分布式密钥生成协议初始化。

Result: FROST2+ 在原 FROST2 分析条件下及采用 PedPoP 分布式密钥生成时均可达到 TS-SUF-4 安全。基于 ZCash FROST 库的基准测试显示其性能与 FROST2 相当，在启用预计算时比 FROST 快约 64-79%。

Conclusion: FROST2+ 在原计算假设下将门限 Schnorr 签名安全性提升至 TS-SUF-4，同时保持高实用性，是 FROST2 的有效安全增强方案。

Abstract: Threshold signature schemes play a vital role in securing digital assets within blockchain and distributed systems. FROST2 stands out as a practical threshold Schnorr signature scheme, noted for its efficiency and compatibility with standard verification processes. However, under the one-more discrete logarithm assumption, with static corruption and centralized key generation settings, FROST2 has been shown by Bellare et al. (in CRYPTO 2022) to achieve only TS-SUF-2 security, which is a consequence of its vulnerability to TS-UF-3 attacks.
  In this paper, we address this security limitation by presenting an enhanced variant of FROST2, namely, FROST2+ which achieves the TS-SUF-4 security level under the same computational assumptions as the original FROST2. FROST2+ strengthens FROST2 by integrating additional pre-processing token verifications that help mitigate TS-UF-3 and TS-UF-4 vulnerabilities while maintaining practical efficiency. We show that FROST2+ can achieve TS-SUF-4 security not only under the same conditions as the original FROST2 analysis, but also when initialized with a distributed key generation protocol such as PedPoP. Our benchmark using ZCash's FROST library shows that the performance of FROST2+ is comparable to FROST2 and about 64-79% faster than FROST when precomputation is enabled.

</details>


### [319] [RMCW: A Deletion-Robust Watermark Based on Reed--Muller Codes for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02817)
*Yi Wang,Baicheng Chen,Yu Wang,Jian Zhao,Yilei Chen,Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RMCW，一种基于Reed-Muller码的大语言模型水印方法，通过搜索存活的局部代数结构（Reed-Solomon一致性）应对删除攻击，在C4与ELI5数据集上表现优于或匹配基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM水印可识别特定模型生成的文本，但在后处理攻击下鲁棒性脆弱，尤其删除攻击会改变token位置、破坏观测token与原始水印位置的对齐，现有方法难以应对。

Method: RMCW基于Reed-Muller码，检测时不追求全局码字恢复，而是利用Reed-Muller码字仿射线性限制所诱导的Reed-Solomon一致性，搜索存活的局部代数结构。生成阶段通过密钥化词表划分将Reed-Muller结构注入序列；检测阶段将文本映射到密钥化词表分箱，并用Berlekamp-Welch检验测试局部子序列的低次Reed-Solomon一致性。

Result: 在C4和ELI5数据集上，使用OPT-1.3B与Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct实验表明，RMCW保持强干净文本可检测性，并在多种删除与改写攻击下优于或匹配基线方法。

Conclusion: RMCW通过局部代数结构搜索有效提升了对删除攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持干净文本检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) watermarking provides a lightweight mechanism for identifying text generated by a specific model, but its robustness remains fragile under post-processing attacks. Deletion attacks are particularly challenging because they shift token positions and break the alignment between observed tokens and their original watermark positions. We propose Reed--Muller Code Watermarking (RMCW), an LLM watermarking method based on Reed--Muller codes. In contrast to global codeword recovery, RMCW searches for surviving local algebraic structure, leveraging the Reed--Solomon consistency induced by affine-line restrictions of Reed--Muller codewords. During generation, RMCW injects a Reed--Muller structure into the sequence via a secret-keyed vocabulary partition. During detection, it maps the given text to keyed vocabulary bins and tests local subsequences for low-degree Reed--Solomon consistency using Berlekamp--Welch tests. Experiments on C4 and ELI5 datasets with OPT-1.3B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct show that RMCW preserves strong clean-text detectability and outperforms or matches the baseline methods under several deletion and rewriting attacks. Our code is available at https://github.com/BaichengDanny/RMCW.

</details>


### [320] [PrivDev: Mapping Static-Analysis Data Types to DPV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03518)
*Simon Bernbeck,Ricardo Ramalho,Matheus Amendoeira,Juliana Alves Pereira*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PrivDev 将 Bearer CLI 的 122 种数据类型映射到 DPV-PD 类别并关联 GDPR 条款，结合确定性匹配与检索增强 LLM，生成经 SHACL 验证的 RDF 知识图谱，并通过五重验证与人工评估确认映射的可信性与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析扫描器虽能识别源码中的个人数据类型，却缺乏将其发现连接到标准化隐私词汇表的机制，导致扫描结果难以对接 GDPR 等法规语义体系。

Method: 对 43 个标签完全一致的数据类型采用确定性映射，其余 79 个非平凡映射使用检索增强的 LLM 解决；构建包含 118 个 ODRL 策略资源的 RDF 知识图谱，并用 SHACL 进行结构验证；通过结构正确性、查询一致性、检索质量、LLM 评估和人工评估五道验证关卡。

Result: 九位标注者产生 711 条判断，原始一致率为 0.72，Gwet's AC1 为 0.68，Gwet's AC2 为 0.88，表明映射结果合理且可复现。

Conclusion: 所提出的映射具有合理性与可复现性，同时揭示了扫描器定义的数据类型标签存在歧义，以及 DPV-PD 存在覆盖缺口。

Abstract: Static-analysis scanners can identify personal-data types in source code, but they lack mechanisms to connect these findings to standardized privacy vocabularies. PrivDev maps 122 Bearer CLI data types to Data Privacy Vocabulary Personal Data (DPV-PD) categories and links them to potentially relevant GDPR provisions. Our approach combines deterministic mapping for 43 exact-label matches with a retrieval-grounded Large Language Model (LLM) to resolve the remaining 79 non-trivial mappings. The resulting RDF knowledge graph contains 118 ODRL policy resources that were structurally validated using SHACL. The artifact passed five complementary validation gates that cover structural correctness, query consistency, retrieval quality, LLM-based assessment, and human evaluation. In human evaluation, nine annotators produced 711 judgments, yielding a raw agreement of 0.72, Gwet's AC1 of 0.68, and Gwet's AC2 of 0.88. Our results indicate that the proposed mappings are plausible and reproducible, while also revealing ambiguities in scanner-defined data-type labels and coverage gaps in DPV-PD.

</details>


### [321] [Containing the Autonomous Operator: A Defense-in-Depth Framework and Reference Architecture for Securing AI Agents on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02861)
*Simhadri Podala Narasimha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文主张不应将 LLM 模型本身视为安全边界，Kubernetes 上 agent 安全本质上是基础设施问题：所有安全保证必须在"agent 已被提示注入完全攻陷"的假设下依然成立。作者提出威胁模型与十类威胁分类、九条设计原则、七层纵深防御框架、参考架构及定性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 正从聊天界面进入基础设施运维领域，能够读取遥测数据、调用工具、生成并执行代码、改变生产 Kubernetes 集群状态。这打破了传统云原生安全所依赖的"数据与控制"边界假设：agent 仅仅读取的内容（日志行、工单、工具描述）就可能重定向其行为。因此需要重新思考 agent 安全的责任归属与保障方式。

Method: 提出五方面贡献：(i) 面向在 Kubernetes 上及内部运行的 agent 的威胁模型与十类威胁分类，与 OWASP agentic 应用新兴指南对齐；(ii) 九条设计原则，核心是工具边界的完全中介，以及打破"不可信输入 + 敏感访问 + 外部出口"三者的组合；(iii) 七层纵深防御框架，将原则映射到原生或广泛采用的 Kubernetes 机制：工作负载身份、RBAC 与 ValidatingAdmissionPolicy、基于 SIG Apps Agent Sandbox 项目的 gVisor/Kata 沙箱、FQDN 感知的出站策略、带工具参数策略即代码的 agent/MCP 网关、eBPF 运行时执行；(iv) 参考架构，含具体策略工件及各层在 Amazon EKS、Azure Kubernetes Service 和 Google Kubernetes Engine 上的绑定；(v) 定性评估，包括威胁-控制覆盖矩阵和四个攻击走查，并给出拟议的实证方法。

Result: 论文未报告实测的攻击成功率或性能开销数据；而是识别残余风险，并指出验证该框架所需的测量指标。评估以定性的威胁-控制覆盖矩阵和四个攻击走查为主，辅以拟议的实证方法论。

Conclusion: Kubernetes 上的 agent 安全应作为基础设施问题处理，而非依赖模型自身作为安全边界。唯有在假设 agent 被提示注入完全攻陷的前提下，通过工具边界完全中介与纵深防御机制，才能持续维持安全保证。后续工作需通过实证测量验证框架有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are moving from chat interfaces into infrastructure operations, where they read telemetry, call tools, generate and execute code, and change the state of production Kubernetes clusters. This collapses a boundary that conventional cloud-native security assumes: the boundary between data and control. Content that an agent merely reads (a log line, a ticket, a tool description) can redirect what it does. This paper argues that the model must not be treated as a security boundary and that agent safety on Kubernetes is therefore an infrastructure problem: every guarantee must continue to hold under the assumption that the agent is fully compromised by prompt injection. We contribute (i) a threat model and ten-class threat taxonomy for agents operating on and within Kubernetes, aligned with emerging OWASP guidance for agentic applications; (ii) nine design principles, centered on complete mediation at the tool boundary and on breaking the combination of untrusted input, sensitive access, and external egress; (iii) a seven-layer defense-in-depth framework that maps each principle to native or widely adopted Kubernetes mechanisms: workload identity, RBAC and ValidatingAdmissionPolicy, gVisor/Kata sandboxing via the SIG Apps Agent Sandbox project, FQDN-aware egress policy, an agent/MCP gateway with policy-as-code over tool arguments, and eBPF runtime enforcement; (iv) a reference architecture with concrete policy artifacts and per-layer bindings for Amazon EKS, Azure Kubernetes Service, and Google Kubernetes Engine; and (v) a qualitative evaluation comprising a threat-control coverage matrix and four attack walkthroughs, with a proposed empirical methodology. We report no measured attack-success or overhead figures; instead we identify residual risks and the measurements needed to validate the framework.

</details>


### [322] [AgentTrap: Stateful Feedback Deception against Autonomous Penetration Testing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02869)
*Yuelin Wang,Jiongchi Yu,Yanbang Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentTrap，首个面向自主渗透测试智能体的闭环蜜罐系统，通过哨兵端点、有状态欺骗和行为引导升级机制，降低真实目标攻击成功率并诱导攻击者泄露 API 密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蜜罐依赖静态工件和预定义响应，无法适应自主渗透测试智能体不断演化的攻击策略，因此需要一种能持续适应并与之交互的闭环蜜罐。

Method: AgentTrap 采用三类核心机制：哨兵端点以避免对正常用户的干扰；基于被保护应用的有状态欺骗；行为引导升级以维持交互并在受控披露下收集智能体侧行为证据。在包含真实应用端点与独立蜜罐端点的部署式 Web 应用中，针对八种自主渗透测试智能体和三种防御策略进行评估。

Result: 与无防御相比，AgentTrap 将真实目标的总体攻击成功率从 95.8% 降至 79.2%，并在 18.8% 的运行中成功诱导出攻击者 API 密钥，优于静态欺骗和固定升级策略。追踪分析表明，对此类反制攻击的抵抗能力同时取决于模型层对欺骗请求的识别和架构层对敏感资源的隔离。

Conclusion: AgentTrap 证明了闭环、行为引导的蜜罐能有效应对自主渗透测试智能体，为动态防御此类智能体提供了可行路径；同时揭示防御效果依赖模型识别与架构隔离的协同。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration testing agents conduct multi-step attacks by continuously adapting their plans and actions to target responses. As a common defense, honeypots can be deployed to divert these agents from real assets by presenting decoy services, while also supporting attack tracing and active counterattacks. However, conventional honeypots rely primarily on static artifacts and predefined responses, leaving them unable to adapt to the evolving attack strategies of autonomous penetration testing agents. To this end, we present AgentTrap, the first closed-loop honeypot tailored for autonomous penetration testing agents. AgentTrap uses sentinel endpoints to avoid benign interference, stateful deception grounded in the protected application, and behavior-guided escalation to sustain engagement and collect agent-side behavioral evidence with controlled disclosures.
  We evaluate AgentTrap against eight autonomous penetration-testing agents in a deployed web application containing a real application endpoint and a separate honeypot endpoint configured under three defense strategies. Compared with no defense, AgentTrap reduces the aggregate real-target attack success rate from 95.8% to 79.2% and successfully elicits attacker API keys in 18.8% of the runs, outperforming static deception and fixed escalation. Furthermore, trace analysis shows that resistance to such counterattacks depends jointly on model-level recognition of deceptive requests and architecture-level isolation of sensitive resources.

</details>


### [323] [Digital Twin-Assisted Mapping of ICS Telemetry to ATT&CK for ICS with Evidence-Driven Dependency Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02955)
*Konstantinos E. Kampourakis,Vyron Kampourakis,Vasileios Gkioulos,Sokratis Katsikas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数字孪生辅助框架，将ICS遥测中的同步状态变化转化为证据保留描述，经检索增强LLM推理映射到ATT&CK for ICS，并构建类型化依赖图；实验显示DT增强可减少误报但对召回与外部迁移有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工控系统遥测中，过程观测直接反映物理变化，而难以还原产生这些变化的对抗行为，因此需要一种能够从遥测中重建对抗行为语义的辅助方法。

Method: 框架包括：提取同步状态变化、转化为证据保留描述、通过检索增强大语言模型推理映射到ATT&CK for ICS，并构建类型化依赖图；评估采用SWaT九场景四配置消融、三次独立生成及BATADAL与WADI外部评估。

Result: SWaT三次生成中DT增强映射均减少注释相对误报片段，平均每轮降低34.1%；两种配置平均召回率均为0.633，但召回在0.50至0.70间波动，DT增强并非每轮都提升F1；主配对比较未达统计显著，映射优势未迁移至外部数据集；探索性正例依赖基准在DT上下文下恢复9个已记录共现关系中的8个；端到端基准显示映射误差会传播为无支持边。

Conclusion: DT上下文对语义安全解释兼具潜力与局限，尚不能建立可靠的自主归因、通用因果重建或实际分析收益。

Abstract: Reconstructing adversarial behavior from Industrial Control System (ICS) telemetry is difficult because process observations reveal physical changes more directly than the actions that produced them. This paper presents a Digital Twin (DT)-assisted framework that extracts synchronized state changes, converts them into evidence-preserving descriptions, maps them to ATT&CK for ICS through retrieval-augmented Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, and constructs a typed dependency graph. Evaluation comprises a four-configuration ablation on nine held-out SWaT scenarios containing ten telemetry-evaluable ground-truth episodes, three independent generations of the principal mapping configurations, and external evaluation on BATADAL and WADI. Across the three SWaT generations, DT-enriched mapping produces fewer annotation-relative False Positive (FP) episodes in every run, with a mean per-run reduction of 34.1%. Both configurations obtain a mean recall of 0.633, although recall varies between 0.50 and 0.70 and DT enrichment does not improve F1 in every run. These observations are descriptive: the primary-run paired comparisons do not reach statistical significance, and the mapping advantage does not transfer to either external dataset. An exploratory positive-only dependency benchmark recovers eight of nine documented Co-occurrence relationships with DT context. A separate end-to-end benchmark containing one positive pair and 25 negative controls exposes propagation of mapping errors into unsupported edges. The findings identify both the potential and limitations of DT context for semantic security interpretation, without establishing reliable autonomous attribution, general causal reconstruction, or practical analyst benefit.

</details>


### [324] [Beyond Predefined Sinks: Security-Aware Dependency Analysis for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03014)
*Hang Cui*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 AgentSecGraph 静态分析框架，为安全敏感操作构建 Security-ADG 依赖图，并配套 67 个真实 LLM-agent 仓库的 AgentSecBench 基准，证明仅有操作标识不足以判定安全含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 将模型决策连接到命令执行、文件系统、网络、浏览器等安全敏感能力，但已有分析多以预定义敏感操作为锚点，操作标识本身无法判定其安全含义，缺少来源、依赖、信任边界与防护等上下文证据。

Method: 构建 AgentSecGraph 框架，为每个安全敏感操作生成以候选为中心的 Security-ADG，融合 agent 相关性、来源与依赖证据、信任边界上下文、防护证据和外部效应语义；并建立 AgentSecBench 语料（67 个真实 LLM-agent 仓库、11 个生态、37,542 个源文件），通过独立的复现评估层验证效果。

Result: 在 65 个仓库中识别 23,866 个静态安全敏感操作候选，每个候选产出一份 Security-ADG；语料级分析为 9,821 个候选（41.15%）恢复来源到操作的依赖证据，为 3,075 个（12.88%）恢复潜在防护证据，耗时 50.8 分钟。复现评估在 13 个仓库中确立 22 项安全敏感行为：1 个已确认漏洞、1 个待披露候选、20 个受防护行为。在 9 个留出案例中，Security-ADG 保留 91.1% 的参考上下文和全部 5 个观测到的防护，而仅看 sink 为 20.0%、简化 ADG 为 40.0%。

Conclusion: 安全感知的依赖与上下文证据能带来仅凭敏感操作标识无法恢复的区分能力，说明以候选为中心、融合信任边界与防护证据的静态分析是判定 LLM agent 安全含义的必要途径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly connect model-generated decisions to security-sensitive software capabilities such as command execution, filesystem access, network communication, browser control, and external tools. Existing analyses often use predefined sensitive operations as anchors, but operation identity alone is insufficient to determine security implications.
  We present AgentSecGraph, a security-aware static analysis framework that constructs a candidate-centered Security-Aware Agent Dependency Graph (Security-ADG) for each security-sensitive operation. It augments operation identity with agent relevance, source and dependency evidence, trust-boundary context, guard evidence, and external-effect semantics.
  We further introduce AgentSecBench, a corpus of 67 real-world LLM-agent repositories spanning 11 ecosystems and 37,542 source files. The current analyzer identifies 23,866 static security-sensitive operation candidates across 65 repositories and emits one Security-ADG artifact per candidate. Corpus-wide analysis recovers source-to-operation dependency evidence for 9,821 candidates (41.15%) and potential guard evidence for 3,075 (12.88%), completing in 50.8 minutes.
  Using a separate reproduction-backed evaluation layer, we establish 22 security-sensitive behaviors across 13 repositories: one confirmed vulnerability, one pending disclosure candidate, and 20 guarded behaviors. In nine held-out cases, Security-ADG preserves 91.1% of the reference context and all five observed guards, compared with 20.0% for a sink-only view and 40.0% for a simplified ADG. These results show that security-aware dependency and contextual evidence enable distinctions that cannot be recovered from sensitive-operation identity alone.

</details>


### [325] [SecJev: Bringing Security Expertise to System One Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03073)
*Zheng Chen,Fei Yu,Haohao Huang,Yang Li,Anlong Chen,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出首个面向安全领域的Jev类决策模型族SecJev（0.8B–9B），并构建统一语料SecJev-Corpus，覆盖14项任务8类来源，通过场景加权训练实现安全领域特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全业务流程需要将复杂观测与显式策略转化为决策，而通用模型缺乏安全领域专业知识，因此需要将Jev类类型化决策模型进行安全领域特化。

Method: 基于Kev的单遍候选打分器构建SecJev，学习布尔、选择与有序决策，输入为文本、遥测与观测历史；构建SecJev-Corpus统一源标签预测与显式策略评估（14任务、8来源），并采用场景加权训练在保持共享类型化决策接口的同时适配多领域。

Result: 安全特化使家族内所有模型均获提升，SecJev-0.8B在任务宏平均准确率上比通用Kev-9B高20.51个百分点；与仅答案生成式微调相比，精度与延迟接近且峰值推理内存更低；在新来源组上于提示注入与流量决策优于Kev，但存在依赖捕获的误报。

Conclusion: 安全领域特化能有效提升决策模型性能并降低推理内存，成果包括适配器、决策头、SecJev-Corpus及训练与推理代码的公开释放。

Abstract: Security workflows need models that turn complex observations and explicit policies into decisions. System One models introduced by Jev return typed predictions and probabilities; security specialization supplies the domain expertise behind those predictions. We introduce SecJev, to our knowledge the first family of Jev-like decision models specialized for security, spanning 0.8B to 9B parameters. Built on Kev's single-pass candidate scorer, SecJev learns Boolean, choice, and ordered decisions from text, telemetry, and observation histories. We develop SecJev-Corpus to unify source-label prediction and explicit-policy evaluation across 14 tasks and eight sources. It covers tool outputs, traffic, federated updates, consensus, authentication, and vehicle messages. Scene-weighted training adapts the models across these domains while preserving a shared typed decision interface. Security specialization improves every model in the family; SecJev-0.8B outperforms general Kev-9B by 20.51 percentage points in task-macro accuracy. Comparisons with answer-only generative fine-tuning show close accuracy and latency with lower peak inference memory. Tests on new source groups reproduce gains over Kev in prompt-injection and traffic decisions, with capture-dependent false alarms. We release adapters, decision heads, SecJev-Corpus, and training and inference code.

</details>


### [326] [Securing Computer-Use Agents Against Branch Steering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03089)
*Giulio Zingrillo,Hanna Foerster,Ilia Shumailov,Yiren Zhao,Robert Mullins*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对计算机使用代理（CUA）的"分支引导攻击"，提出STEER-Bench基准，并设计COBRA架构将攻击成功率降至0%同时保持97%的良性效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CUA直接与图形界面交互并执行第三方Web工具，面临间接提示注入风险。Dual-LLM模式虽提供形式化安全保证（P-LLM固定执行路径，Q-LLM处理不可信输入），但在图形环境中保证失效：CUA交互本质上是动态的，计划无法保持数据无关性，必须根据运行时Web内容分支，从而暴露"分支引导攻击"风险——攻击者无需注入显式指令，即可诱导代理走上有害的预批准分支。

Method: 1) 对分支引导攻击进行系统化研究；2) 构建STEER-Bench基准（涵盖9个领域的101个任务）；3) 提出COBRA架构，将可信分支计划与"提前能力约束"相结合，严格限制每个分支可执行的参数与目标。

Result: 在STEER-Bench上：标准CUA的攻击成功率为94.4%，原版Dual-LLM为89.5%；COBRA将攻击成功率降至0%，同时保持97%的良性任务效用。

Conclusion: 分支引导攻击对现有CUA与Dual-LLM架构构成严重威胁，而COBRA通过可信分支计划与提前能力约束，在几乎不损失实用性的前提下实现了对这类攻击的完全防御。

Abstract: Modern Computer Use Agents (CUAs) directly interact with graphical user interfaces and execute third-party web tools, exposing them to indirect prompt injection across every rendered page and tool response. While the Dual-LLM pattern is the primary system-level architecture offering formal security guarantees - using an isolated Planner LLM (P-LLM) to fix execution paths before processing untrusted inputs via a Quarantined LLM (Q-LLM) - these guarantees break down in graphical environments. Because CUA interaction is inherently dynamic, plans cannot remain data-independent; they must branch based on anticipated runtime web content - covering all possible cases the agent may encounter. This exposes agents to branch steering attacks, where an adversary crafts untrusted data to coerce a CUA down a hazardous, pre-approved branch without injecting explicit instructions. We systematically study branch steering attacks and introduce STEER-Bench (101 tasks across 9 domains), showing high attack success against both standard (94.4%) and vanilla Dual-LLM (89.5%) CUAs. We then propose COBRA, an architecture that pairs trusted branching plans with ahead-of-time capability constraints, strictly bounding the parameters and destinations each branch may execute. On STEER-Bench, COBRA reduces attack success to 0% while retaining 97% benign utility.

</details>


### [327] [The Fragility of Trigger-Tag Mechanisms for Misuse Detection in Open-Weight LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03124)
*Toluwani Aremu,Manit Baser,Mohan Gurusamy,Nils Lukas,Dinil Mon Divakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文形式化了开放权重语言模型中的trigger-tag机制，区分token级与weight级两类，并提出统一攻击框架Untag，实验证明在对抗攻击下现有trigger-tag机制完全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重语言模型可被下载、修改并脱离开发者控制部署，中心化保障措施效果有限。近期提出的trigger-tag机制能在模型被用于目标条件（如生成钓鱼内容）时产生可检测信号，但这类机制用于开放权重LLM的条件性滥用检测仍属新兴方向，现有研究尚未系统评估其在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性。

Method: (i) 形式化trigger-tag，区分token级trigger-tag（解码时引入类水印信号）与weight级trigger-tag（学习目标条件与可检测模型行为间的类后门关联）；(ii) 提出统一攻击框架Untag，将两类机制特定的攻击面组织为统一分类体系；以钓鱼场景为案例，对代表性的token级和weight级trigger-tag进行评估。

Result: trigger-tag在受控环境下可提供有用证据，但在所提攻击下，现有trigger-tag机制完全失效（entirely ineffective）。

Conclusion: 当攻击者能够转换输出或修改开放权重时，trigger-tag机制不应被视为鲁棒的滥用检测器。

Abstract: Open-weight language models can be downloaded, modified, and deployed beyond their developers' control, limiting the effectiveness of centrally enforced safeguards. Recent work has therefore proposed \emph{trigger-tag} mechanisms that produce a detectable signal when a model is used under a target condition, such as generating phishing contents. Although these mechanisms borrow from established techniques, their use for conditional misuse detection in open-weight LLMs is relatively new. Therefore, existing research works have not systematically studied the robustness of trigger-tag mechanisms under adversarial attacks. To close this gap, (i)~we formalize trigger-tags and distinguish \emph{token-level trigger-tags}, which introduce watermark-inspired signals during decoding, from \emph{weight-level trigger-tags}, which learn backdoor-inspired associations between target conditions and detectable model behavior. Furthermore, (ii)~we introduce \Untag, a unified attack framework that organizes their mechanism-specific attack surfaces into a common taxonomy. We evaluate representative token-level and weight-level trigger-tags using phishing as a case study. We find that while trigger-tags may provide useful evidence in controlled settings, our attacks render the existing trigger-tag mechanisms to be entirely ineffective. Consequently, we argue that these mechanisms should not be treated as robust misuse detectors when attackers can transform outputs or modify open weights.

</details>


### [328] [EvoRiskBench: An Evolving Benchmark for Runtime Security Risks in Workspace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03153)
*Shiyi Kuang,Xuemei Luo,Kun Liu,Junhai Li,Rui Tian,Feng Shi,Bo Shen,Nianyu Li,Dehui Li,Ping Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出EvoRiskBench，一个围绕EP-Path-EF框架的可演进基准，用450个对抗任务评估工作区智能体的运行时安全风险，发现最脆弱配置ASR达68.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作区智能体结合大模型与执行框架执行有状态、多步骤任务，会访问或修改外部资源，带来运行时安全风险。现有基准在其运行时风险的可执行覆盖上存在空白，且模型能力、执行框架、工具与威胁持续演进，需要可演进的基准。

Method: 提出EP-Path-EF框架，将初始风险入口点经智能体介导的风险路径连接到一跳技术效果；定义九类入口点与五类效果，并用20人研究验证其可解释性与分类一致性。基于该框架构建自动化端到端流程，在隔离环境中构造并执行风险案例，并通过运行时轨迹与环境状态独立验证结果。基准提供6个场景下450个对抗任务的可复现数据集。评估9种模型-执行框架组合，涵盖3个模型（GPT-5.6 Sol、DeepSeek-V4-Pro-0813、Claude Opus 5）与3个执行框架（Claude Code、Codex、OpenClaw）。

Result: 系统普遍存在显著漏洞。最脆弱配置Codex搭配DeepSeek-V4-Pro-0813达到68.44%攻击成功率（ASR），表明工作区智能体的配置不足以保障自主执行的安全性。ASR在模型间的差异大于执行框架间，且执行框架差异取决于模型。基准案例与评估平台将在完成制品安全与可复现性检查后发布。

Conclusion: 工作区智能体存在严重且广泛的运行时安全风险，仅靠配置难以保证安全自主执行；应同时关注模型与执行框架的组合效应。该可演进基准为持续追踪新兴威胁与能力变化提供支撑。

Abstract: Workspace agents combine large language models with execution harnesses to perform stateful, multi-step tasks that access or modify external resources. Existing benchmarks leave gaps in executable coverage of their runtime security risks, while evolving model capabilities, harnesses, tools, and threats motivate benchmark evolution. We introduce EvoRiskBench, an evolving benchmark organized around the EP-Path-EF framework, which links an initial risk entry point to a one-hop technical effect through an agent-mediated risk path. The framework defines nine entry-point categories and five effect categories; a 20-participant study supports their interpretability and classification consistency on representative cases. Guided by this framework, an automated end-to-end workflow constructs and executes risk cases in isolated environments and independently verifies outcomes using runtime traces and environment states. The benchmark provides a reproducible dataset of 450 adversarial tasks across six scenarios. We evaluate nine model-harness configurations spanning three models (GPT-5.6 Sol, DeepSeek-V4-Pro-0813, and Claude Opus 5) and three harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, and OpenClaw). Our results reveal substantial vulnerabilities across systems. The most vulnerable configuration, Codex with DeepSeek-V4-Pro-0813, reaches a 68.44% attack success rate (ASR), indicating that configuration of workspace agent is insufficient to ensure secure autonomous execution. ASR varies more across models than harnesses, and harness differences depend on the model. The benchmark cases and evaluation platform will be released after completion of artifact safety and reproducibility checks.

</details>


### [329] [LiBRA: Detection-Aware Image Watermark Removal via Bidirectional Latent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03166)
*Saibo Ye,Huajie Chen,Xin Guo,Le Yang,Chi Liu,Xiangyu Hu,Jingjing Guo,Tianqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LiBRA（Latent In-band Bidirectional Removal Attack），一种在公共自编码器潜在空间中进行的双向水印移除攻击，使水印不可检测同时保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字水印可支持AI生成图像的来源归属，但其可靠性取决于对移除攻击的抵抗力。现有攻击通过强制解码水印与原始水印不同来移除水印，但这可能产生仍然可检测的"反转水印"，导致移除失败，而进一步修改又会不必要地损害图像质量。

Method: LiBRA在拥有水印密钥和解码器的条件下，于公共自编码器的潜在空间中做有界修改。与无法纠正过度反转的反转驱动目标不同，LiBRA将平均解码置信度从任一方向引导向随机猜测，避免出现反转但仍可检测的水印。保持单个比特灵活，使图像质量约束倾向于选择破坏较小的修改，并可选地用频率引导掩码限制修改位置。最后使用精确双侧二项检验验证移除是否成功。

Result: 通过精确双侧二项检验验证水印移除效果，而非假设置信度目标保证成功，能在使水印不可检测的同时保持图像质量。

Conclusion: LiBRA通过双向引导解码置信度趋于随机猜测，克服了反转驱动攻击导致反转水印仍可检测及过度损害图像质量的局限，实现了水印移除与图像质量的更好平衡。

Abstract: Digital watermarking supports source attribution for AI-generated images, but its reliability depends on resistance to removal attacks. Some attacks attempt to remove watermarks by forcing the decoded watermark to differ from the original. However, this can produce an inverted watermark that remains detectable, causing removal to fail, while further attempts to alter the watermark may unnecessarily degrade image quality. To address these limitations, we present LiBRA (Latent In-band Bidirectional Removal Attack), which aims to make watermarks undetectable while preserving image quality. Instead of continually pushing the watermark toward inversion, LiBRA adjusts the image to conceal the watermark without encouraging further changes that could degrade image quality. Some attacks keep pushing decoded bits away from the original watermark, even when further changes preserve detectability and damage image quality. With access to the watermark key and decoder, LiBRA makes bounded changes in a public autoencoder's latent space. Unlike inversion-driven objectives that cannot correct excessive inversion, LiBRA guides average decoding confidence toward random guessing from either direction. This helps avoid an inverted but detectable watermark. Leaving individual bits flexible allows image-quality constraints to favor less damaging changes, while an optional frequency-guided mask limits their location. We verify removal using an exact two-sided binomial test rather than assuming the confidence target guarantees success.

</details>


### [330] [Reversing the Clock: Layout-Aware Recovery of Design Intent from Clock Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03182)
*Sascha Tommasone,Zehra Karadağ,Christopher Pawlowicz,Michael Green,Bruno Machado Trindade,Eunsung Seo,Christof Paar,René Walendy,Steffen Becker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种版图感知的方法，通过融合从SEM图像提取的版图信息与已恢复的门级网表，实现从实物IC中恢复并分析时钟分布网络，从而推断设计意图，并在商用450nm IC上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件逆向工程技术主要恢复门级网表以反映逻辑功能，却忽略了布局、布线、延迟插入、互连优化等物理设计决策。时钟分布网络集中体现了这些决策，但此前没有公开发表的工作能从已制造的IC中恢复该网络以推断设计意图。

Method: 提出一种版图感知的方法，将已恢复的门级网表与从扫描电子显微镜（SEM）图像中提取的版图信息相结合，采用四阶段流水线，恢复时钟树拓扑、缓冲、门控与切换、互连延迟以及串扰缓解措施。

Result: 在商用450nm IC上验证：恢复出带局部X树状分支的全局H树主干，识别出独立时钟树和全局时钟门控，量化了延迟、偏斜和布线长度，并确认不存在专用串扰缓解措施。相关时钟树恢复算法已开源。

Conclusion: 该方法能够从实物IC中恢复时钟分布网络并据此推理设计者的设计意图，弥补了现有逆向工程忽视物理设计决策的不足，且通过开源算法支持后续研究与可复现性。

Abstract: Hardware reverse engineering supports competitive analysis and hardware assurance by recovering information about an integrated circuit (IC) from its physical implementation. While existing techniques primarily recover the gate-level netlist, which represents logical functionality, they often overlook physical design decisions such as placement, routing, delay insertion, and interconnect optimization. The clock distribution network encapsulates many of these decisions; however, no prior published work has recovered this network from a fabricated IC to infer design intent. We present a layout-aware methodology that recovers and analyzes the clock distribution network by integrating a recovered gate-level netlist with layout information extracted from scanning electron microscope imagery. Our four-phase pipeline recovers clock-tree topology, buffering, gating and switching, interconnect delay, and crosstalk-mitigation measures. We demonstrate our methodology on a commercial 450 nm IC, recovering a global H-tree backbone with local X-tree-like branching, identifying an independent clock tree and global clock gating, quantifying latency, skew, and routing lengths, and confirming the absence of dedicated crosstalk mitigation. Together, these findings let us reason about the designer's intent. To encourage further research and support reproducibility, we release our clock tree recovery algorithm as open source.

</details>


### [331] [Asymptotic Analysis of Trading Fees in CFMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03262)
*Peiyang Jin,Clouds,Jing Qian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动做市商（AMM）中考虑交易手续费时，流动性提供者（LP）从套利交易中获得的手续费收入，给出闭式公式，并发现连续价格过程下手续费可完全抵消LVR损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为去中心化金融的主流交易机制，AMM已被广泛研究，但考虑交易手续费的研究相对有限。作者希望量化当费率趋近于零时，LP从套利交易中能获得多少手续费，并探究手续费与LP损失之间的关系。

Method: 通过理论建模分析AMM中套利交易产生的手续费收入，推导出费率趋近于零时LP所获手续费的闭式公式，并将结论推广到含跳跃的价格过程进行理论分析。

Result: 作者给出了LP从套利交易中获取手续费的闭式公式；结果表明当价格过程连续时，套利交易产生的手续费能够完全抵消LVR损失。

Conclusion: 在含跳跃的价格过程中，跳跃是除市场风险外导致LP损失的唯一原因。研究结果为AMM设计者和LP提供了实践指导。

Abstract: As the dominant trading mechanism in decentralized finance, Automated Market Maker has been widely studied in research. However, limited research has been done with the trading fees taken into consideration. In this work, we study how much trading fee Liquidity Providers(LPs) can receive from arbitrage trading when the fee rate approaches zero. We give a closed-form formula for it and our result shows that the trading fees generated by arbitrage trading can fully offset the LVR loss when the price process is continuous. We further extend our conclusions to price processes with jumps and the theoretical analysis shows that jumps are the only cause of LP loss apart from market risks. Our results provide practical guidance for AMM designers as well as LPs.

</details>


### [332] [Persona Guardrail: A Production-Grade Defense Framework for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03434)
*Bijeeta Pal,Sridhar Reddy Maddireddy,Muhaimin Bin Munir,Zoltan Puha,Max Zhurovich,Adi Raghavendra,Sean Tout*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 Persona Guardrail 运行时防护框架，通过语义白名单/黑名单同步校验输入输出，为面向客户的智能体系统强制功能边界，并发布 PAGE 评测基准；整体准确率从 85.7% 提升至 95.9%，域外检测率从 57.3% 提升至 93.5%，误放行率从 25.0% 降至 4.7%，已在生产环境部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的智能体越来越多地通过与企业知识、工具和外部服务交互来执行特定领域任务。现有的运行时防护措施主要针对提示注入等特定攻击行为，且多基于黑盒威胁模型，无法保证智能体在其预期功能范围内运作。因此，生产环境中的智能体仍易受恶意请求和域外查询的攻击，而现有防御往往难以区分这些情况。

Method: 提出 Persona Guardrail，一个生产级运行时防御框架，通过由语义允许列表和阻止列表规范驱动的同步输入与输出校验，为面向客户的智能体式 AI 系统强制实施明确的功能边界。同时提出 PAGE（Persona-Aware Guardrail Evaluation）基准，用于在用户端和智能体端两个回合上评估功能特定的防护措施，涵盖良性、对抗性和域外交互场景。

Result: 与通用基于 LLM 的防护措施相比，Persona Guardrail 将整体准确率从 85.7% 提升至 95.9%，将域外检测率从 57.3% 提升至 93.5%，并将误批准率从 25.0% 降至 4.7%。该框架目前已在生产环境部署，在满足延迟预算的同时，在真实生产负载下保持极低的误阻断率和误放行率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，Persona Guardrail 为保护智能体式 AI 系统提供了一个实用、可扩展且可投入生产的基础方案。

Abstract: Large language model-based agents are increasingly deployed to perform domain-specific tasks by interacting with enterprise knowledge, tools, and external services. Existing runtime guardrails primarily target prompt injection and other attack-specific behaviors under a black-box threat model, but provide limited guarantees that agents operate within their intended functionality. As a result, production agents remain vulnerable to malicious requests and out-of-domain queries that existing defenses often fail to distinguish. We present Persona Guardrail, a production-grade runtime defense framework that enforces explicit functional boundaries for customer-facing agentic AI systems through synchronous input and output validation driven by semantic allowlist and blocklist specifications. We also introduce PAGE (Persona-Aware Guardrail Evaluation), a benchmark for evaluating function-specific guardrails across benign, adversarial, and out-of-domain interactions on both user and agent turns. Compared with a generic LLM-based guardrail, Persona Guardrail improves overall accuracy from 85.7% to 95.9%, increases out-of-domain detection from 57.3% to 93.5%, and reduces the false-approved rate from 25.0% to 4.7%. Currently deployed in production, Persona Guardrail meets its latency budget while sustaining a very low false-block and false-allow rate under realistic production workloads. These results demonstrate that Persona Guardrail provides a practical, scalable, and production-ready foundation for securing agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [333] [Passing the Test You Trained On: Re-evaluating Prompt-Injection Detectors for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03448)
*Zhuowen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文检验了小模型提示注入检测器在公开基准上的分数（如BIPIA）是否能预测其在真实LLM agent中的表现。通过对AgentDojo和tau-bench的工具调用进行无LLM重放，作者发现检测排名几乎无法跨基准迁移，而误报率却能迁移；训练输入的形式（而非数据来源）是决定表现的关键。结论是评测应以agent自身工具输出为准、报告低误报率下的检测率，并审查检测器的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent越来越多地用小型提示注入检测器来过滤工具输出，而团队通常依据检测器在公开基准上的得分来选择检测器。但尚不清楚这些基准分数能否预测检测器在真实agent环境中的行为，因此需要验证基准得分与agent内表现之间的关系。

Method: 作者无LLM重放AgentDojo与tau-bench的真实工具调用，得到构造上即为良性的工具输出；通过差分重放标注注入输出。评估了15个检测器（含Meta Prompt Guard 2）和2个任务感知LLM评判器，在agent工具输出及BIPIA基准上比较表现，并分析检测排名与误报率的跨基准迁移性，以及训练数据形式对结果的影响。

Result: 检测排名跨基准迁移很差：BIPIA上最佳检测器在1%误报率下仅捕获2%的AgentDojo注入；能捕获72% AgentDojo注入的检测器在tau-bench上仅15%。误报率在工具输出上却可迁移（范围从0到超90%）。在训练数据公开的情况下，训练输入的形式能解释结果：BIPIA冠军用完整BIPIA输入训练，但见过InjecAgent攻击串作为短提示并不能帮它在工具输出中找到这些攻击；两个agent基准上表现最好的检测器与任何基准都无数据重叠，且训练于agent风格输入。

Conclusion: 面向部署的评测应使用agent自身的工具输出、在低误报率下报告检测率，并审查检测器训练所用的数据；公开基准分数不足以预测检测器在agent中的实际表现。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly screen tool outputs with small prompt-injection detectors, and teams choose among detectors by their scores on public benchmarks. We ask whether those scores predict how a detector behaves inside an agent. We replay the ground-truth tool calls of two agent benchmarks, AgentDojo and tau-bench, without an LLM to obtain tool outputs that are benign by construction, label injected outputs by differential replay, and evaluate fifteen detectors, including Meta's Prompt Guard 2, and two task-aware LLM judges on these outputs and on the BIPIA benchmark. Detection rankings transfer poorly between benchmarks: the best detector on BIPIA catches 2% of AgentDojo injections at a 1% false-positive rate, and a detector that catches 72% of AgentDojo injections catches 15% on tau-bench. False-positive rates on tool outputs, which range from none to over 90%, do transfer between the two agent benchmarks. Where training data is public, the form of the training inputs explains the results. The BIPIA leader was trained on full BIPIA inputs, but having seen InjecAgent's attack strings as short prompts does not help it find them inside tool outputs; the best detector on both agent benchmarks shares no data with any benchmark and was trained on agent-style inputs. Evaluations meant to inform deployment should use the agent's own tool outputs, report detection at a low false-positive rate, and audit what the detector was trained on.

</details>


### [334] [CorrectGuard: Eyes-Off Correctness Estimation for Black-Box Security Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03470)
*Adam Faulkner,Nil-Jana Akpinar,Matthew Dressman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 CorrectGuard，在"人不可见"和"机器不可见"两种设置下，用独立模型预测黑盒护栏决策是否正确，无需访问护栏内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 服务依赖黑盒安全护栏，但隐私保护的审计机制难以评估其性能：既不能在"人眼关闭"（禁止人工查看用户输入）的生产环境中检查，也不能在"机器眼关闭"（禁止模型查看输入）的设置下检查。因此需要一种无需特权访问的护栏正确性估计方法。

Method: CorrectGuard 框架：使用仅有的"眼开启"标注数据，训练独立的基于模型的评估器，预测护栏对"人/机器不可访问输入"的决策是否正确。比较了上下文学习、嵌入和微调三类正确性模型；采用留一数据集交叉评估，覆盖 13 个安全与安防数据集（有害内容、越狱、提示注入、提取），并将开源权重护栏统一视为黑盒；机器不可见设置通过隐私保护的输入指纹化实现。

Result: 上下文学习式正确性分类器在各护栏上将错误识别宏准确率最高提升 25 个百分点，微调方法约提升 15 点，但性能在护栏和留出数据集间波动明显。正确性分数还支持决策排序与弃权：3 个护栏上最佳排序将 AURC 从未排序基线的 0.33–0.44 降至 0.17–0.22；最佳工作点在 15% 观测风险下保留 37.5–52.0% 的护栏决策。

Conclusion: 外部正确性模型无需特权访问即可揭示护栏的系统性失败，并支持护栏决策弃权，为隐私保护下的黑盒护栏审计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AI services increasingly rely on black-box security guardrails, yet privacy-preserving model auditing regimes often cannot measure how well these systems perform in both a human eyes-off production setting, which disallows human inspection of user input, and a machine eyes-off setting, which disallows model inspection of such input. We introduce CorrectGuard, an eyes-off correctness estimation framework for both settings, which involves an independent model-based evaluator predicting whether guardrail decisions on human- and machine-inaccessible inputs are correct using only labeled eyes-on data and without access to the guardrail's internals. We evaluate in-context learning, embedding, and finetuning-based correctness models under leave-one-dataset-out evaluation across 13 safety and security datasets spanning harmful content, jailbreaks, prompt injection, and extraction, and across open-weight guardrails treated uniformly as black boxes. Across both human and machine eyes-off settings (the latter implemented using privacy-preserving fingerprinting of inputs), in-context-learning-based correctness classifiers substantially improve error identification across guardrails, achieving up to a 25 percentage-point increase in macro accuracy, as do finetuning-based approaches which provide a nearly 15-point boost, although performance varies sharply across guardrails and held-out datasets. Correctness scores also support guardrail decision ranking and abstention: across 3 guardrails, the best correctness rankings reduce AURC from unranked baselines of 0.33-0.44 to 0.17-0.22, while the best operating points retain 37.5-52.0% of guardrail decisions at 15% observed risk. These results show that external correctness models can expose systematic failures and support guardrail decision abstention without privileged access to the guardrail.

</details>


### [335] [A Secure dToF LiDAR SoC with Dual-Domain Fingerprinting and Event-Driven AFE Circuit Achieving Sensor-Level Attack Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03562)
*Risa Nonaka,Ryoya Matsuno,Shota Nagai,Satomi Miyagi,Yuki Hayakawa,Ryo Suzuki,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Rokuto Nagata,Ryo Yoshida,Shimpei Ando,Wenlun Zhang,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个集成传感器级防欺骗硬件安全的dToF LiDAR SoC，通过双域指纹（DDF）在距离测量前拒绝欺骗信号，并采用事件驱动AFE大幅降低功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用dToF LiDAR易被高频激光脉冲注入欺骗，导致行人从点云中被抹除，存在安全隐患，因此需要传感器级的硬件防欺骗方案。

Method: 提出双域指纹（DDF），发射时间间隔与幅度比均随机的激光脉冲对，并在二维空间认证回波；采用事件驱动AFE（ED-AFE）仅在脉冲峰值附近以1-GHz触发ADC采样三点并做抛物线插值；使用时间调制激光驱动器，通过调制激光电容充电时间实现10%~100%幅度调制。

Result: 16通道65nm CMOS原型SoC实现最远120m测距，AFE每通道功耗3.1mW，比现有技术低60%；获得1cm距离分辨率，ADC功耗降低99%；概念验证实验中，应用双域认证后73%点云在欺骗攻击下受到保护，而未使用DDF时为0%。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在dToF LiDAR SoC中实现传感器级硬件防欺骗，验证了DDF在保护点云方面的有效性，同时显著降低了AFE功耗。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that most commercial direct time-of-flight (dToF) LiDARs can be spoofed by injecting high-frequency laser pulses into the receiver, which can erase pedestrians from the point cloud. This paper presents the first dToF LiDAR system-on-chip (SoC) with integrated sensor-level hardware security against spoofing attacks. We propose Dual-Domain Fingerprinting (DDF), which emits laser pulse pairs whose time interval and amplitude ratio are both randomized and authenticates received echoes in this two-dimensional space, so that spoofed signals are rejected before they corrupt the ranging result. An Event-Driven AFE (ED-AFE) activates the ADC only around pulse peaks: it digitizes three samples around each peak with a triggered ADC at 1-GHz sampling and applies parabolic interpolation, achieving 1-cm distance resolution with a 99% reduction in ADC power. A time-modulated laser driver controls the laser amplitude from 10% to 100% by modulating the charge time of a laser capacitor, providing the microsecond-order amplitude modulation required by DDF. A LiDAR system with a 16-channel 65-nm CMOS prototype SoC demonstrates up to 120-m ranging and an AFE power of 3.1 mW per channel, 60% lower than prior art. In a proof-of-concept experiment in which the dual-domain authentication is applied to measured sensor data, 73% of the point cloud is protected under spoofing attack, compared with 0% without DDF.

</details>


### [336] [Threat-Preserving Representation Sensitivity in Agent-Security Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03585)
*Neeraj Karamchandani,Piyush Nagasubramaniam,Xinhong Xie,Sencun Zhu,Dinghao Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"威胁保持表征敏感性"(TPRS)概念，发现在保持任务、有害行为、安全策略等不变、仅改变智能体可见表征的情况下，LLM智能体的攻击成功率(ASR)会发生显著变化，说明单一表征下的安全评分难以泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准通常以攻击成功率(ASR)衡量模型鲁棒性，并据此比较不同模型与防御机制，隐含假设ASR能描述智能体的安全性。作者质疑这一假设，探究基准的文字表征本身是否会影响测量结果。

Method: 引入威胁保持表征敏感性(TPRS)，即在固定底层任务、有害行为、安全策略、真值、环境和评估标准的前提下，仅改变智能体可见的表征，测量ASR的变化幅度。在Agent Security Bench (ASB)、MCPTox、AgentDojo三个基准上，对GPT-5-mini、Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-4o-mini等模型进行工具名称与措辞的替换实验，并附加了token数、长度与大小写匹配的对照实验。

Result: ASB上将威胁相关工具名替换为中性名，已实施ASR在GPT-5-mini上升11.67个百分点、Claude Haiku 4.5上升13.21个百分点；MCPTox上将中性名替换为显式威胁名，ASR在GPT-5-mini下降11.00个百分点、Claude Haiku 4.5下降4.11个百分点；AgentDojo上添加威胁措辞仅使ASR变化0.50个百分点(GPT-4o-mini)，但需要该工具的任务的良性效用下降5.36个百分点。在MCPTox上，token数、长度与大小写匹配的中性名可复现显式威胁名带来的大部分变化(GPT-5-mini上11.00点中的8.54点)。

Conclusion: 同一安全问题在不同威胁保持表征下的安全评分并不通用，单一表征下的ASR难以反映真实鲁棒性。鲁棒性结论应基于一组受控的威胁保持表征上的表现，而非依赖单一、表征依赖的分数。

Abstract: Security benchmarks for LLM-based agents often report the attack success rate (ASR) as a measure of model robustness and use these scores to compare different models and defense mechanisms, assuming that they describe the security of the agent. In this paper, we explore whether it also influences the benchmark's measurement.
  To measure the effect of the benchmark representation, we introduce threat-preserving representation sensitivity (TPRS), which measures how much the ASR changes when we change the agent-visible representation while holding the underlying task, harmful action, security policy, ground truth, environment, and the evaluation criteria fixed.
  On Agent Security Bench (ASB), replacing threat-related tool names with threat-neutral names raises the committed attack success rate by 11.67 percentage points on GPT-5-mini and by 13.21 points on Claude Haiku 4.5. On MCPTox, replacing the original neutral tool name with an explicit threat-related name lowers the ASR by 11.00 percentage points on GPT-5-mini and 4.11 points on Claude Haiku 4.5. On AgentDojo, adding threat-related wording to the attack-relevant tool changes ASR by only 0.50 percentage points on GPT-4o-mini, yet the benign utility falls by 5.36 points on tasks requiring that tool.
  We ran an experiment on MCPTox where we observed that a threat-neutral name matched on token count, length, and casing reproduces most of the shift produced by the threat-explicit name (8.54 of 11.00 points on GPT-5-mini).
  The results show that a security score measured under one representation may fail to generalize across threat-preserving representations of the same security problem. Robustness claims should therefore be supported by performance across a controlled set of threat-preserving representations rather than relying on a single representation-dependent score.

</details>


### [337] [Constant-Rate Certified Deletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03590)
*Kai-Min Chung,Tzu-Hsiang Huang,Wei-Hsiang Hung,Shota Yamada*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，可将一大类密码原语升级为支持常数速率（constant-rate）的可认证删除（certified deletion），并在明文模型下首次实现了保持永恒安全性的常数速率构造；同时基于SIS困难性实现了公开可验证的常数速率可认证删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前支持可认证删除的构造需要每个可删除明文比特消耗线性数量级的量子比特，效率低下，因此需要实现常数速率的可认证删除，同时不损失永恒安全性且不引入额外假设。

Method: 提出统一框架，适用于基于BB84式编码的"全有或全无"型原语（承诺方案、公钥加密、属性基加密、全同态加密等）；并扩展到基于子空间陪集态的原语（盲委派、安全软件租赁、函数加密、differing-inputs iO、CCA-PKE）；进一步在SIS困难性假设下引入公开可验证性。

Result: 在明文模型下首次得到常数速率的可认证删除构造，且保持永恒安全性；框架不引入超出底层可认证删除原语所需的额外假设；在SIS困难性下将公开可验证性融入BB84与陪集类可认证删除，结合常数速率构造得到公开可验证且常数速率的可认证删除方案。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并显著提升了可认证删除的构造范围与效率，证明常数速率、永恒安全与公开可验证性可以同时实现。

Abstract: We present a unified framework for upgrading a broad class of cryptographic primitives to support constant-rate certified deletion. Previous constructions require a linear number of qubits per encrypted bit of certified-deletable plaintext. In contrast, we obtain the first constant-rate constructions in the plain model that achieve certified deletion while preserving everlasting security.
  Our approach applies to a wide range of "all-or-nothing"-type primitives based on BB84-style encodings, including commitment schemes, public-key encryption, attribute-based encryption, and fully homomorphic encryption. Beyond this class, we also obtain constant-rate certified deletion for primitives built from subspace coset states, such as blind delegation, secure software leasing, functional encryption, and differing-inputs iO, and CCA-PKE. Importantly, our framework does not introduce any additional assumptions beyond those required by the underlying certified deletion primitives.
  Finally, under the hardness of SIS, we show that public verifiability can be incorporated into BB84- and coset-based certified deletion. In combination with our constant-rate constructions, this yields publicly verifiable certified deletion schemes with constant rate.

</details>


### [338] [PoCoFL: POlicy-COmpliant Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03650)
*Dominik Roy George,Varesh Mishra,Aysajan Abidin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PoCoFL，一个策略合规的联邦学习框架，将FL类型、策略语义与密码学实现解耦，并通过四种实例化验证其通用性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽保护数据隐私，但无法保证客户端提交合规贡献，也无法保证聚合方正确处理已接纳的贡献。现有可验证FL系统将验证规则与特定FL设置、学习流程和密码学构造绑定，难以跨网络拓扑、参与者角色和聚合语义通用。

Method: PoCoFL将三方面分离：(i) FL类型、(ii) 策略语义、(iii) 密码学实现。形式化地将客户端与聚合要求建模为策略相关关系；客户端用承诺和非交互式零知识证明证明贡献合规，聚合方证明所记录的已接纳贡献集合按选定聚合策略被处理。论文给出四种形式化实例：vanilla、continual、personalised与threshold-encrypted联邦学习。

Result: 论文评估了策略执行对vanilla、personalised与continual FL学习目标的影响，并实现全部四种实例的概念验证，展示了PoCoFL的通用性与实际可行性。

Conclusion: PoCoFL能够捕捉复杂策略表达，同时保持网络拓扑无关性。这些结果表明该框架在保证策略合规的同时具有广泛适用性与可部署性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is a privacy-oriented learning paradigm that enables collaborative model training while keeping training data local to participating clients. However, it does not guarantee that clients submit policy-compliant contributions or that aggregators process admitted contributions correctly. Existing verifiable FL systems tailor validation rules to specific FL settings, learning workflows, and cryptographic constructions, limiting their applicability across network topologies, participant roles, and aggregation semantics. In this paper, we present PoCoFL, a policy-compliant federated learning framework that separates three aspects: (i) FL type, (ii) policy semantics, and (iii) cryptographic realisation. We provide a formalisation that captures client and aggregation requirements as policy-dependent relations. Clients prove compliance of their contributions using commitments and non-interactive zero-knowledge proofs, while aggregators prove that the recorded set of admitted contributions was processed according to the selected aggregation policy. We demonstrate PoCoFL through four formal instantiations: (i) vanilla, (ii) continual, (iii) personalised, and (iv) threshold-encrypted federated learning. We evaluate the effects of policy enforcement on the learning objectives of vanilla, personalised, and continual FL. We further implement proof-of-concept realisations of all four instantiations, demonstrating the versatility and practical feasibility of PoCoFL. Overall, these results show that PoCoFL can capture complex policy representations while remaining network-topology agnostic.

</details>
